`crates/teeforge/src/cli.rs`:

```rs
use crate::config::{Config, KeyboxSource, SYS_CONFIG, TargetMode, USER_CONFIG, UpdateChannel};
use crate::description;
use crate::error::{Result, TfError};
use crate::info;
use crate::json;
use crate::keybox;
use crate::logging::{self, Level};
use crate::rkp;
use crate::rootdetect;
use crate::target;
use crate::update;
use crate::volume;
use serde_json::{Value, json};
use std::fs;
use std::path::{Path, PathBuf};

#[derive(Default)]
struct Options {
    generate: bool,
    keybox: bool,
    source: Option<String>,
    remote_slot: Option<u8>,
    force: bool,
    save_selection: bool,
    keybox_validate: Option<PathBuf>,
    rkp_status: bool,
    rkp_issue: bool,
    rkp_artifacts: bool,
    rkp_delete: bool,
    rkp_probe: bool,
    target_list: bool,
    target_apply: Option<PathBuf>,
    target_mode: Option<String>,
    self_update: Option<String>,
    update_channel: Option<String>,
    bootstrap: bool,
    json: bool,
    rootdetect: bool,
    update_desc: bool,
    skip_rootdetect: bool,
    volume: Option<u64>,
    verbose: bool,
    help: bool,
    config: Option<PathBuf>,
}

fn parse(args: &[String]) -> Result<Options> {
    let mut options = Options::default();
    let mut index = 1;
    while index < args.len() {
        let need_value = |index: usize, name: &str| -> Result<&String> {
            args.get(index + 1)
                .ok_or_else(|| TfError::new(format!("{name} 需要参数 [{name} requires a value]")))
        };
        match args[index].as_str() {
            "--generate" => options.generate = true,
            "--keybox" => options.keybox = true,
            "--source" => {
                index += 1;
                options.source = Some(need_value(index - 1, "--source")?.clone());
            }
            "--remote-slot" => {
                index += 1;
                options.remote_slot = Some(
                    need_value(index - 1, "--remote-slot")?
                        .parse::<u8>()
                        .ok()
                        .filter(|v| (1..=3).contains(v))
                        .ok_or_else(|| {
                            TfError::new("--remote-slot 只支持 1/2/3 [expects 1/2/3]")
                        })?,
                );
            }
            "--force" => options.force = true,
            "--save-selection" => options.save_selection = true,
            "--keybox-validate" => {
                index += 1;
                options.keybox_validate = Some(need_value(index - 1, "--keybox-validate")?.into());
            }
            "--rkp-status" => options.rkp_status = true,
            "--rkp-issue" => options.rkp_issue = true,
            "--rkp-artifacts" => options.rkp_artifacts = true,
            "--rkp-delete" => options.rkp_delete = true,
            "--rkp-probe" => options.rkp_probe = true,
            "--target-list" => options.target_list = true,
            "--target-apply" => {
                index += 1;
                options.target_apply =
                    Some(PathBuf::from(need_value(index - 1, "--target-apply")?));
            }
            "--target-mode" => {
                index += 1;
                options.target_mode = Some(need_value(index - 1, "--target-mode")?.clone());
            }
            "--self-update" => {
                index += 1;
                options.self_update = Some(need_value(index - 1, "--self-update")?.clone());
            }
            "--update-channel" => {
                index += 1;
                options.update_channel = Some(need_value(index - 1, "--update-channel")?.clone());
            }
            "--bootstrap" => options.bootstrap = true,
            "--json" => options.json = true,
            "--rootdetect" => options.rootdetect = true,
            "--update-desc" => options.update_desc = true,
            "--no-rootdetect" => options.skip_rootdetect = true,
            "--verbose" => options.verbose = true,
            "--help" | "-h" => options.help = true,
            "--volume" => {
                let mut timeout = 10;
                if args
                    .get(index + 1)
                    .is_some_and(|value| !value.starts_with('-'))
                {
                    index += 1;
                    timeout = args[index].parse::<u64>().map_err(|_| {
                        TfError::new(
                            "--volume 需要非负秒数 [--volume requires non-negative seconds]",
                        )
                    })?;
                }
                options.volume = Some(timeout);
            }
            "--config" => {
                index += 1;
                let path = need_value(index - 1, "--config")?;
                options.config = Some(path.into());
            }
            unknown => {
                return Err(TfError::new(format!(
                    "未知选项 [Unknown option]: {unknown}"
                )));
            }
        }
        index += 1;
    }
    let modifiers = options.source.is_some()
        || options.remote_slot.is_some()
        || options.force
        || options.save_selection;
    if modifiers && !options.keybox {
        return Err(TfError::new(
            "Keybox 参数必须搭配 --keybox [Keybox arguments require --keybox]",
        ));
    }
    let other_action = options.generate
        || options.rootdetect
        || options.update_desc
        || options.rkp_status
        || options.rkp_issue
        || options.rkp_artifacts
        || options.rkp_delete
        || options.rkp_probe
        || options.target_list
        || options.target_apply.is_some()
        || options.target_mode.is_some()
        || options.self_update.is_some()
        || options.update_channel.is_some()
        || options.bootstrap
        || options.volume.is_some();
    if (modifiers && other_action)
        || (options.keybox_validate.is_some() && (other_action || options.keybox))
    {
        return Err(TfError::new(
            "Keybox 操作不能混用其他命令 [Do not combine keybox with another command]",
        ));
    }
    Ok(options)
}

fn effective_keybox(options: &Options, config: &Config) -> Result<Config> {
    let mut effective = config.clone();
    if let Some(source) = &options.source {
        effective.keybox_source = KeyboxSource::parse(source).ok_or_else(|| {
            TfError::staged("cli", "source", "未知 Keybox 来源 [Unknown keybox source]")
        })?;
    }
    if effective.keybox_source != KeyboxSource::Remote
        && (options.remote_slot.is_some() || options.force)
    {
        return Err(TfError::staged(
            "cli",
            "remote_only",
            "槽位与强制参数只用于远程池 [Slot/force require remote source]",
        ));
    }
    if let Some(slot) = options.remote_slot {
        effective.keybox_remote_slot = slot;
    }
    Ok(effective)
}

fn usage(program: &str) {
    println!("用法 Usage: {program} [options]");
    println!("\n选项 Options:");
    println!("  --generate          更新目标应用配置 [Update target app configuration]");
    println!("  --target-list       列出已安装应用与选择状态 [List apps and selection]");
    println!("  --target-apply FILE 应用手动选择集合 [Apply a selection file]");
    println!("  --target-mode MODE  切换 auto/manual 目标模式 [Switch target mode]");
    println!("  --keybox            同步 keybox [Sync keybox]");
    println!("  --remote-slot N     远程池槽位 1/2/3 [Remote slot]");
    println!("  --force             远程池相同内容也替换 [Rewrite identical remote data]");
    println!("  --save-selection    安装成功后保存来源与槽位 [Save selection after installation]");
    println!("  --keybox-validate F 只读校验 Keybox 格式 [Validate keybox file]");
    println!("  --source SOURCE     keybox 来源 remote/rkp [Keybox source]");
    println!("  --rkp-status        检查设备端 RKP 条件 [Check device RKP prerequisites]");
    println!("  --rkp-issue         设备端 RKP 签发并保存产物 [Issue RKP artifacts]");
    println!("  --rkp-artifacts     列出 RKP 产物 [List RKP artifacts]");
    println!("  --rkp-delete        删除 RKP 产物 [Delete RKP artifacts]");
    println!(
        "  --rkp-probe         探测 TA 响应约定（诊断用，消耗一次 challenge） [Probe TA response]"
    );
    println!("  --self-update MODE  check/install 更新模块 [Check or install updates]");
    println!("  --update-channel C  stable/dev 更新通道 [Update channel]");
    println!("  --bootstrap         输出首屏信息 [Emit bootstrap information]");
    println!("  --json              以 JSON 输出 [Emit JSON]");
    println!("  --rootdetect        检测 root 方式并输出 [Detect root method]");
    println!("  --update-desc       更新模块描述 [Update module description]");
    println!("  --no-rootdetect     跳过 root 检测 [Skip root detection]");
    println!("  --volume SEC        音量键监听 [Volume key listen]");
    println!("  --verbose           启用调试日志 [Enable debug logging]");
    println!("  --config FILE       使用自定义配置 [Use custom config]");
    println!("  --help              显示帮助 [Show help]");
}

fn banner() {
    println!("TeeForge-CD {}", crate::VERSION);
}

fn record(result: Result<()>, failed: &mut bool) {
    if let Err(error) = result {
        logging::log(Level::Error, error.to_string());
        *failed = true;
    }
}

pub fn run(args: Vec<String>) -> i32 {
    let program = args.first().map_or("teeforge", String::as_str);
    // 内部入口：TA 子进程只做一次厂商调用，不初始化配置、日志或 root 检测。
    if let Some(index) = args.iter().position(|arg| arg == "--rkp-ta-worker") {
        let Some(directory) = args.get(index + 1) else {
            eprintln!("--rkp-ta-worker 需要目录路径 [requires a directory]");
            return 2;
        };
        return match rkp::execute_worker(std::path::Path::new(directory)) {
            Ok(()) => 0,
            Err(error) => {
                eprintln!("{error}");
                1
            }
        };
    }
    // 内部入口：探测变体同样只做一次厂商调用。
    if let Some(index) = args.iter().position(|arg| arg == "--rkp-ta-probe") {
        let (Some(directory), Some(variant)) = (args.get(index + 1), args.get(index + 2)) else {
            eprintln!("--rkp-ta-probe 需要目录与变体序号 [requires a directory and a variant]");
            return 2;
        };
        let Ok(variant) = variant.parse::<usize>() else {
            eprintln!("变体序号非法 [invalid variant index]");
            return 2;
        };
        return match rkp::execute_probe(std::path::Path::new(directory), variant) {
            Ok(()) => 0,
            Err(error) => {
                eprintln!("{error}");
                1
            }
        };
    }
    let options = match parse(&args) {
        Ok(options) => options,
        Err(error) => {
            if args.iter().any(|arg| arg == "--json") {
                return json::fail(&error);
            }
            eprintln!("{error}");
            usage(program);
            return 1;
        }
    };
    if options.help {
        usage(program);
        return 0;
    }

    if let Some(path) = &options.keybox_validate {
        let result = fs::File::open(path)
            .map_err(TfError::from)
            .and_then(keybox::read_limited)
            .and_then(|data| crate::keybox_format::validate(&data));
        if options.json {
            return json::finish(result.map(|()| json!({"valid": true})));
        }
        return match result {
            Ok(()) => {
                println!("格式有效 [Format valid]");
                0
            }
            Err(error) => {
                eprintln!("{error}");
                1
            }
        };
    }

    let user_config = options
        .config
        .clone()
        .unwrap_or_else(|| PathBuf::from(USER_CONFIG));
    let config = Config::load(&user_config);
    let config = if options.keybox {
        match effective_keybox(&options, &config) {
            Ok(config) => config,
            Err(error) => {
                if options.json {
                    return json::fail(&error);
                }
                eprintln!("{error}");
                return 1;
            }
        }
    } else {
        config
    };
    logging::init(&config, options.verbose);

    if options.json {
        return run_json(&options, &config, &user_config);
    }

    if options.rkp_status {
        for line in rkp::status_lines(&config) {
            println!("{line}");
        }
        return if rkp::available() { 0 } else { 1 };
    }

    let no_action = !options.generate
        && !options.keybox
        && !options.rootdetect
        && !options.update_desc
        && !options.rkp_issue
        && !options.rkp_artifacts
        && !options.rkp_delete
        && !options.rkp_probe
        && !options.target_list
        && options.target_apply.is_none()
        && options.target_mode.is_none()
        && options.self_update.is_none()
        && options.update_channel.is_none()
        && !options.bootstrap
        && options.volume.is_none();
    if args.len() == 1 && no_action {
        banner();
        let root = rootdetect::detect();
        println!("Root: {} (v{})\n", root.method, root.version);
        let _ = rootdetect::save(std::path::Path::new(SYS_CONFIG), &root);
        usage(program);
        return 0;
    }

    let mut failed = false;
    if !options.skip_rootdetect && !options.rootdetect {
        let root = rootdetect::detect();
        logging::log(
            Level::Info,
            format!("Root: {} (v{})", root.method, root.version),
        );
        record(
            rootdetect::save(std::path::Path::new(SYS_CONFIG), &root),
            &mut failed,
        );
    }
    if options.bootstrap {
        for line in info::bootstrap_lines(&config) {
            println!("{line}");
        }
    }
    if options.generate {
        match target::generate(&config) {
            Ok(outcome) => outcome.log(),
            Err(error) => record(Err(error), &mut failed),
        }
    }
    if options.keybox {
        match keybox::fetch(
            &config,
            options.force,
            options.save_selection.then_some(user_config.as_path()),
        ) {
            Ok(report) => {
                println!(
                    "来源 Source: {}，目标 {} 个 [targets]",
                    report.source.label(),
                    report.targets.len()
                );
                record(description::update(&config), &mut failed);
            }
            Err(error) => record(Err(error), &mut failed),
        }
    }
    if options.rkp_issue {
        match rkp::issue_and_save(&config) {
            Ok(saved) => {
                println!("已签发 [issued]: {}", saved.keybox.display());
                println!("证书链 [chain]: {}", saved.chain.display());
                record(description::update(&config), &mut failed);
            }
            Err(error) => record(Err(error), &mut failed),
        }
    }
    if options.rkp_artifacts {
        let value = rkp::artifacts_value(&config);
        println!("{value:#}");
    }
    if options.rkp_delete {
        match rkp::delete_artifacts(&config) {
            Ok(value) => println!("{value:#}"),
            Err(error) => record(Err(error), &mut failed),
        }
    }
    if options.rkp_probe {
        match rkp::probe(&config) {
            Ok(value) => println!("{value:#}"),
            Err(error) => record(Err(error), &mut failed),
        }
    }
    if options.target_list {
        match target::list_value(&config) {
            Ok(value) => {
                let count = value["counts"]["installed"].as_u64().unwrap_or(0);
                let selected = value["counts"]["selected"].as_u64().unwrap_or(0);
                println!("已安装 {count} 个应用，当前选中 {selected} 个 [installed/selected]");
                println!("模式 Mode: {}", value["mode"].as_str().unwrap_or("-"));
            }
            Err(error) => record(Err(error), &mut failed),
        }
    }
    if let Some(path) = options.target_apply.clone() {
        match read_selection(&path)
            .and_then(|names| target::apply_selection(&config, &user_config, names).map(|_| ()))
        {
            Ok(()) => println!("目标作用域已更新 [target scope updated]"),
            Err(error) => record(Err(error), &mut failed),
        }
    }
    if let Some(mode) = options.target_mode.as_deref() {
        let parsed = TargetMode::parse(mode);
        match parsed {
            Some(parsed) => record(
                target::switch_mode(&config, &user_config, parsed).map(|_| ()),
                &mut failed,
            ),
            None => record(
                Err(TfError::new(format!("未知模式 [unknown mode]: {mode}"))),
                &mut failed,
            ),
        }
    }
    if let Some(channel) = options.update_channel.as_deref() {
        match UpdateChannel::parse(channel) {
            Some(parsed) => record(
                crate::config::update_key(&user_config, "update_channel", parsed.label()),
                &mut failed,
            ),
            None => record(
                Err(TfError::new(format!(
                    "未知通道 [unknown channel]: {channel}"
                ))),
                &mut failed,
            ),
        }
    }
    if let Some(action) = options.self_update.as_deref() {
        match action {
            "check" => match update::check_value(&config) {
                Ok(value) => {
                    println!(
                        "当前 [current]: {} ({})  最新 [latest]: {} ({})",
                        value["current"]["version"].as_str().unwrap_or("-"),
                        value["current"]["version_code"],
                        value["latest"]["version"].as_str().unwrap_or("-"),
                        value["latest"]["version_code"]
                    );
                    println!(
                        "{}",
                        if value["available"] == json!(true) {
                            "有可用更新 [update available]"
                        } else {
                            "已是最新 [up to date]"
                        }
                    );
                }
                Err(error) => record(Err(error), &mut failed),
            },
            "install" => match update::install_value(&config) {
                Ok(value) => {
                    if value["installed"] == json!(true) {
                        println!(
                            "已安装 [installed]: {}，重启后生效 [reboot to apply]",
                            value["version"].as_str().unwrap_or("-")
                        );
                    } else {
                        println!(
                            "未安装 [not installed]: {}",
                            value["reason"].as_str().unwrap_or("-")
                        );
                        if let Some(path) = value["saved_to"].as_str() {
                            println!("已保存 [saved]: {path}");
                        }
                    }
                }
                Err(error) => record(Err(error), &mut failed),
            },
            other => record(
                Err(TfError::new(format!(
                    "--self-update 只支持 check/install，收到 [got]: {other}"
                ))),
                &mut failed,
            ),
        }
    }
    if options.rootdetect {
        let root = rootdetect::detect();
        println!("{}\n{}", root.method, root.version);
        record(
            rootdetect::save(std::path::Path::new(SYS_CONFIG), &root),
            &mut failed,
        );
    }
    if let Some(timeout) = options.volume {
        match volume::listen(timeout) {
            Ok(value) => {
                println!("{value}");
                if value < 0 {
                    failed = true;
                }
            }
            Err(error) => record(Err(error), &mut failed),
        }
    }
    if options.update_desc {
        record(description::update(&config), &mut failed);
    }
    if failed { 1 } else { 0 }
}

/// JSON 模式：只允许一个命令，保证输出是一个完整的信封。
fn run_json(options: &Options, config: &Config, user_config: &Path) -> i32 {
    let mut commands = Vec::new();
    if options.bootstrap {
        commands.push("bootstrap");
    }
    if options.generate {
        commands.push("generate");
    }
    if options.keybox {
        commands.push("keybox");
    }
    if options.rkp_status {
        commands.push("rkp-status");
    }
    if options.rkp_issue {
        commands.push("rkp-issue");
    }
    if options.rkp_artifacts {
        commands.push("rkp-artifacts");
    }
    if options.rkp_delete {
        commands.push("rkp-delete");
    }
    if options.rkp_probe {
        commands.push("rkp-probe");
    }
    if options.target_list {
        commands.push("target-list");
    }
    if options.target_apply.is_some() {
        commands.push("target-apply");
    }
    if options.target_mode.is_some() {
        commands.push("target-mode");
    }
    if options.self_update.is_some() {
        commands.push("self-update");
    }
    if options.update_channel.is_some() {
        commands.push("update-channel");
    }
    if commands.len() != 1 {
        return json::fail(
            &TfError::staged(
                "cli",
                "command_count",
                "--json 需要且只能搭配一个命令 [--json expects exactly one command]",
            )
            .with_raw(json!({ "commands": commands })),
        );
    }

    if options.bootstrap {
        json::ok(info::bootstrap_value(config));
        return 0;
    }
    if options.generate {
        let value = target::generate(config).map(|outcome| match outcome {
            target::GenerateOutcome::SkippedNoBackend => json!({
                "state": "skipped",
                "reason": "no_backend",
            }),
            target::GenerateOutcome::Unchanged {
                package_count,
                backend_count,
            } => json!({
                "state": "unchanged",
                "packages": package_count,
                "backends": backend_count,
            }),
            target::GenerateOutcome::Updated {
                package_count,
                backend_count,
            } => json!({
                "state": "updated",
                "packages": package_count,
                "backends": backend_count,
            }),
        });
        return json::finish(value);
    }
    if options.keybox {
        let value = keybox::fetch(
            config,
            options.force,
            options.save_selection.then_some(user_config),
        )
        .map(|report| {
            json!({
                "source": report.source.label(),
                "remote_slot": report.remote_slot,
                "targets": report.targets.iter().map(|(path, changed)| json!({
                    "path": path.display().to_string(),
                    "changed": changed,
                })).collect::<Vec<_>>(),
            })
        });
        let code = json::finish(value);
        let _ = description::update(config);
        return code;
    }
    if options.rkp_status {
        json::ok(rkp::status_value(config));
        return 0;
    }
    if options.rkp_issue {
        let value = rkp::issue_and_save(config).map(|saved| {
            let meta =
                serde_json::from_str::<Value>(&fs::read_to_string(&saved.meta).unwrap_or_default())
                    .unwrap_or(Value::Null);
            json!({
                "keybox": saved.keybox.display().to_string(),
                "chain": saved.chain.display().to_string(),
                "meta_file": saved.meta.display().to_string(),
                "meta": meta,
            })
        });
        let code = json::finish(value);
        let _ = description::update(config);
        return code;
    }
    if options.rkp_artifacts {
        json::ok(rkp::artifacts_value(config));
        return 0;
    }
    if options.rkp_delete {
        return json::finish(rkp::delete_artifacts(config));
    }
    if options.rkp_probe {
        return json::finish(rkp::probe(config));
    }
    if options.target_list {
        return json::finish(target::list_value(config));
    }
    if let Some(path) = options.target_apply.as_deref() {
        let value = read_selection(path)
            .and_then(|names| target::apply_selection(config, user_config, names));
        return json::finish(value);
    }
    if let Some(mode) = options.target_mode.as_deref() {
        let Some(parsed) = TargetMode::parse(mode) else {
            return json::fail(&TfError::staged(
                "cli",
                "target_mode",
                format!("未知模式 [unknown mode]: {mode}"),
            ));
        };
        return json::finish(target::switch_mode(config, user_config, parsed));
    }
    if let Some(channel) = options.update_channel.as_deref() {
        let Some(parsed) = UpdateChannel::parse(channel) else {
            return json::fail(&TfError::staged(
                "cli",
                "update_channel",
                format!("未知通道 [unknown channel]: {channel}"),
            ));
        };
        return match crate::config::update_key(user_config, "update_channel", parsed.label()) {
            Ok(()) => {
                json::ok(json!({ "channel": parsed.label() }));
                0
            }
            Err(error) => json::fail(&error),
        };
    }
    if let Some(action) = options.self_update.as_deref() {
        return match action {
            "check" => json::finish(update::check_value(config)),
            "install" => json::finish(update::install_value(config)),
            other => json::fail(&TfError::staged(
                "cli",
                "self_update",
                format!("--self-update 只支持 check/install，收到 [got]: {other}"),
            )),
        };
    }
    json::fail(&TfError::new("未识别的命令 [no command]"))
}

/// 读取选择文件：接受 `["pkg"]` 或 `{"packages":["pkg"]}` 两种形态。
fn read_selection(path: &Path) -> Result<Vec<String>> {
    let text = fs::read_to_string(path).map_err(|error| {
        TfError::staged(
            "target",
            "selection_unreadable",
            format!("无法读取选择文件 [cannot read selection file]: {error}"),
        )
        .with_raw(json!({ "path": path.display().to_string() }))
    })?;
    let value: Value = serde_json::from_str(&text).map_err(|error| {
        TfError::staged(
            "target",
            "selection_invalid",
            format!("选择文件不是合法 JSON [selection is not valid JSON]: {error}"),
        )
    })?;
    let array = match &value {
        Value::Array(items) => items.clone(),
        other => other
            .get("packages")
            .and_then(Value::as_array)
            .cloned()
            .ok_or_else(|| {
                TfError::staged(
                    "target",
                    "selection_invalid",
                    "选择文件缺少 packages 数组 [missing packages array]",
                )
            })?,
    };
    Ok(array
        .iter()
        .filter_map(Value::as_str)
        .map(str::to_owned)
        .collect())
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    #[test]
    fn keybox_modifiers_validate_before_side_effects() {
        let parse_args = |args: &[&str]| {
            parse(
                &std::iter::once("teeforge".to_owned())
                    .chain(args.iter().map(|s| (*s).to_owned()))
                    .collect::<Vec<_>>(),
            )
        };
        for args in [
            vec!["--force"],
            vec!["--remote-slot", "2"],
            vec!["--save-selection"],
            vec!["--keybox", "--remote-slot", "4"],
            vec!["--keybox", "--remote-slot", "x"],
            vec!["--keybox", "--force", "--generate"],
            vec!["--keybox-validate", "file", "--keybox"],
        ] {
            assert!(parse_args(&args).is_err());
        }
        let options = parse_args(&[
            "--keybox",
            "--source",
            "remote",
            "--remote-slot",
            "2",
            "--force",
            "--save-selection",
        ])
        .unwrap();
        assert!(options.force && options.save_selection);
        assert_eq!(
            effective_keybox(&options, &Config::default())
                .unwrap()
                .keybox_remote_slot,
            2
        );
        let rkp = Config {
            keybox_source: KeyboxSource::Rkp,
            ..Config::default()
        };
        let options = parse_args(&["--keybox", "--force"]).unwrap();
        assert!(effective_keybox(&options, &rkp).is_err());
        let options = parse_args(&["--keybox", "--source", "rkp", "--remote-slot", "1"]).unwrap();
        assert!(effective_keybox(&options, &Config::default()).is_err());
        let options = parse_args(&["--keybox"]).unwrap();
        assert!(!options.save_selection && !options.force);
    }

    #[test]
    fn parses_compatible_options() {
        let args = vec![
            "teeforge".into(),
            "--generate".into(),
            "--volume".into(),
            "12".into(),
            "--config".into(),
            "/tmp/config".into(),
        ];
        let options = parse(&args).expect("valid options");
        assert!(options.generate);
        assert_eq!(options.volume, Some(12));
        assert_eq!(options.config, Some(PathBuf::from("/tmp/config")));
    }

    #[test]
    fn parses_structured_commands() {
        let args = vec![
            "teeforge".into(),
            "--json".into(),
            "--keybox".into(),
            "--source".into(),
            "rkp".into(),
        ];
        let options = parse(&args).expect("valid options");
        assert!(options.json);
        assert_eq!(options.source.as_deref(), Some("rkp"));

        let args = vec![
            "teeforge".into(),
            "--target-apply".into(),
            "/tmp/selection.json".into(),
        ];
        let options = parse(&args).expect("valid options");
        assert_eq!(
            options.target_apply,
            Some(PathBuf::from("/tmp/selection.json"))
        );
    }

    #[test]
    fn rejects_unknown_option() {
        assert!(parse(&["teeforge".into(), "--unknown".into()]).is_err());
    }
}
```

```rs
use crate::atomic_file;
use crate::error::{Result, TfError};
use std::fs;
use std::path::{Path, PathBuf};

pub(crate) const SYS_CONFIG: &str = "/data/adb/teeforge/sys.conf";
pub(crate) const USER_CONFIG: &str = "/data/adb/teeforge/config.conf";
pub(crate) const DEFAULT_RKP_SERVER: &str = "https://remoteprovisioning.googleapis.com/v1";
pub(crate) const DEFAULT_UPDATE_BASE: &str = "https://teeforge.mcxiaochen.top/files/update";

/// Keybox 来源 [Where the installed keybox comes from].
///
/// 由用户显式选择，不再隐式回退；`Remote` 是升级后的默认值，保持旧行为。
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub(crate) enum KeyboxSource {
    /// 远程公共密钥池 [Shared keybox pool from the CDN]
    Remote,
    /// 本机 RKP 产物 [Artifacts produced by this device]
    Rkp,
}

impl KeyboxSource {
    pub(crate) fn parse(value: &str) -> Option<Self> {
        match value.trim().to_ascii_lowercase().as_str() {
            "remote" => Some(Self::Remote),
            "rkp" | "device" | "local" => Some(Self::Rkp),
            _ => None,
        }
    }

    pub(crate) fn label(self) -> &'static str {
        match self {
            Self::Remote => "remote",
            Self::Rkp => "rkp",
        }
    }
}

/// 目标作用域模式 [How the target list is produced].
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub(crate) enum TargetMode {
    /// 每次扫描全部已安装用户应用 [Every installed user app]
    Auto,
    /// 只使用 `target-selection.json` 中的集合 [Only the saved selection]
    Manual,
}

impl TargetMode {
    pub(crate) fn parse(value: &str) -> Option<Self> {
        match value.trim().to_ascii_lowercase().as_str() {
            "auto" => Some(Self::Auto),
            "manual" => Some(Self::Manual),
            _ => None,
        }
    }

    pub(crate) fn label(self) -> &'static str {
        match self {
            Self::Auto => "auto",
            Self::Manual => "manual",
        }
    }
}

/// 更新通道 [Update channel].
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub(crate) enum UpdateChannel {
    Stable,
    Dev,
}

impl UpdateChannel {
    pub(crate) fn parse(value: &str) -> Option<Self> {
        match value.trim().to_ascii_lowercase().as_str() {
            "stable" | "release" => Some(Self::Stable),
            "dev" | "canary" => Some(Self::Dev),
            _ => None,
        }
    }

    pub(crate) fn label(self) -> &'static str {
        match self {
            Self::Stable => "stable",
            Self::Dev => "dev",
        }
    }

    /// 通道对应的清单文件名 [Manifest file name for this channel].
    pub(crate) fn manifest(self) -> &'static str {
        match self {
            Self::Stable => "release.json",
            Self::Dev => "dev.json",
        }
    }
}

#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub(crate) struct Config {
    pub(crate) packages_xml: PathBuf,
    pub(crate) target_txt: PathBuf,
    pub(crate) teesim_config: PathBuf,
    pub(crate) omk_enabled: bool,
    pub(crate) omk_injector_config: PathBuf,
    pub(crate) keybox_dir: PathBuf,
    pub(crate) rkp_dir: PathBuf,
    pub(crate) keybox_source: KeyboxSource,
    pub(crate) keybox_remote_slot: u8,
    pub(crate) target_mode: TargetMode,
    pub(crate) target_selection: PathBuf,
    pub(crate) rkp_server: String,
    pub(crate) update_base: String,
    pub(crate) update_dir: PathBuf,
    pub(crate) update_channel: UpdateChannel,
    pub(crate) sources_conf: PathBuf,
    pub(crate) log_dir: PathBuf,
    pub(crate) root_method: String,
    pub(crate) root_version: String,
    pub(crate) debug: bool,
}

impl Default for Config {
    fn default() -> Self {
        Self {
            packages_xml: "/data/system/packages.xml".into(),
            target_txt: "/data/adb/tricky_store/target.txt".into(),
            teesim_config: "/data/adb/teesim/config.json".into(),
            omk_enabled: false,
            omk_injector_config: "/data/misc/keystore/omk/injector.toml".into(),
            keybox_dir: "/data/adb/teeforge/keybox".into(),
            rkp_dir: "/data/adb/teeforge/RKP".into(),
            keybox_source: KeyboxSource::Remote,
            keybox_remote_slot: 1,
            target_mode: TargetMode::Auto,
            target_selection: "/data/adb/teeforge/target-selection.json".into(),
            rkp_server: DEFAULT_RKP_SERVER.into(),
            update_base: DEFAULT_UPDATE_BASE.into(),
            update_dir: "/data/adb/teeforge/update".into(),
            update_channel: UpdateChannel::Stable,
            sources_conf: "/data/adb/teeforge/sources.conf".into(),
            log_dir: "/data/adb/teeforge/logs".into(),
            root_method: "Unknown".into(),
            root_version: "unknown".into(),
            debug: false,
        }
    }
}

fn parse_bool(value: &str) -> bool {
    value.trim().parse::<i32>().unwrap_or(0) != 0
}

impl Config {
    pub(crate) fn load(user_path: &Path) -> Self {
        let mut config = Self::default();
        let sys_path = existing_or_legacy(Path::new(SYS_CONFIG), Path::new("./sys.conf"));
        if let Some(path) = sys_path {
            config.apply_file(&path);
        }
        let fallback = (user_path == Path::new(USER_CONFIG)).then_some(Path::new("./config.conf"));
        if let Some(path) = existing_or_legacy(user_path, fallback.unwrap_or(user_path)) {
            config.apply_file(&path);
        }
        config
    }

    fn apply_file(&mut self, path: &Path) {
        let Ok(data) = fs::read_to_string(path) else {
            return;
        };
        for raw in data.lines() {
            let line = raw.trim();
            if line.is_empty() || line.starts_with('#') {
                continue;
            }
            let Some((key, value)) = line.split_once('=') else {
                continue;
            };
            self.apply(key.trim(), value.trim());
        }
    }

    fn apply(&mut self, key: &str, value: &str) {
        match key {
            "packages_xml" => self.packages_xml = value.into(),
            "target_txt" => self.target_txt = value.into(),
            "teesim_config" => self.teesim_config = value.into(),
            "omk_enabled" => self.omk_enabled = parse_bool(value),
            "omk_injector_config" => self.omk_injector_config = value.into(),
            "keybox_dir" => self.keybox_dir = value.into(),
            "rkp_dir" => self.rkp_dir = value.into(),
            "keybox_source" => {
                if let Some(source) = KeyboxSource::parse(value) {
                    self.keybox_source = source;
                }
            }
            "keybox_remote_slot" => {
                if let Ok(slot @ 1..=3) = value.parse::<u8>() {
                    self.keybox_remote_slot = slot;
                }
            }
            // 旧键：1.0 之前用自动优先设备端；升级后统一按 keybox_source 处理，
            // 这里仅解析不生效，避免用户配置报错。
            "rkp_mode" => {}
            "target_mode" => {
                if let Some(mode) = TargetMode::parse(value) {
                    self.target_mode = mode;
                }
            }
            "target_selection" => self.target_selection = value.into(),
            "rkp_server" => {
                let server = value.trim();
                if !server.is_empty() {
                    self.rkp_server = server.into();
                }
            }
            "update_base" => {
                let base = value.trim().trim_end_matches('/');
                if !base.is_empty() {
                    self.update_base = base.into();
                }
            }
            "update_dir" => self.update_dir = value.into(),
            "update_channel" => {
                if let Some(channel) = UpdateChannel::parse(value) {
                    self.update_channel = channel;
                }
            }
            "sources_conf" => self.sources_conf = value.into(),
            "log_dir" => self.log_dir = value.into(),
            "root_method" => self.root_method = value.into(),
            "root_version" => self.root_version = value.into(),
            "debug" => self.debug = parse_bool(value),
            _ => {}
        }
    }
}

fn existing_or_legacy(primary: &Path, legacy: &Path) -> Option<PathBuf> {
    if primary.is_file() {
        Some(primary.to_path_buf())
    } else if legacy.is_file() {
        Some(legacy.to_path_buf())
    } else {
        None
    }
}

pub(crate) fn update_key(path: &Path, key: &str, value: &str) -> Result<()> {
    if key.contains(['=', '\n', '\r']) || value.contains(['\n', '\r']) {
        return Err(TfError::new(
            "配置键值包含非法字符 [Configuration key/value contains invalid characters]",
        ));
    }
    let current = fs::read_to_string(path).unwrap_or_default();
    let mut replaced = false;
    let mut lines = Vec::new();
    for line in current.lines() {
        if line.split_once('=').is_some_and(|(found, _)| found == key) {
            lines.push(format!("{key}={value}"));
            replaced = true;
        } else {
            lines.push(line.to_owned());
        }
    }
    if !replaced {
        lines.push(format!("{key}={value}"));
    }
    atomic_file::write(path, format!("{}\n", lines.join("\n")).as_bytes())
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    #[test]
    fn later_values_override_defaults() {
        let mut config = Config::default();
        config.apply("debug", "1");
        config.apply("prop_tool", "rs");
        config.apply("target_txt", "/tmp/target");
        assert!(config.debug);
        assert_eq!(config.target_txt, PathBuf::from("/tmp/target"));
        assert_eq!(
            config.teesim_config,
            PathBuf::from("/data/adb/teesim/config.json")
        );
    }

    #[test]
    fn invalid_boolean_is_off() {
        let mut config = Config::default();
        config.apply("debug", "invalid");
        assert!(!config.debug);
    }

    #[test]
    fn omk_defaults_and_user_overrides_ignore_legacy_rs_keys() {
        let mut config = Config::default();
        assert!(!config.omk_enabled);
        let original = config.clone();
        config.apply("prop_tool", "rs");
        config.apply("blhide_compact", "1");
        assert_eq!(config, original);
        config.apply("omk_enabled", "1");
        config.apply("omk_injector_config", "/tmp/omk/injector.toml");
        assert!(config.omk_enabled);
        assert_eq!(
            config.omk_injector_config,
            PathBuf::from("/tmp/omk/injector.toml")
        );
        config.apply("omk_enabled", "0");
        assert!(!config.omk_enabled);
    }

    #[test]
    fn keybox_source_defaults_to_remote_and_accepts_overrides() {
        let mut config = Config::default();
        assert_eq!(config.keybox_source, KeyboxSource::Remote);
        assert_eq!(config.keybox_remote_slot, 1);
        config.apply("keybox_remote_slot", "3");
        assert_eq!(config.keybox_remote_slot, 3);
        for invalid in ["0", "4", "-1", "bad"] {
            config.apply("keybox_remote_slot", invalid);
            assert_eq!(config.keybox_remote_slot, 3);
        }

        config.apply("keybox_source", "rkp");
        assert_eq!(config.keybox_source, KeyboxSource::Rkp);
        assert_eq!(config.keybox_remote_slot, 3);
        config.apply("keybox_source", "remote");
        assert_eq!(config.keybox_source, KeyboxSource::Remote);
        config.apply("keybox_source", "nonsense");
        assert_eq!(
            config.keybox_source,
            KeyboxSource::Remote,
            "unknown values stay safe"
        );
    }

    #[test]
    fn legacy_rkp_mode_is_ignored_without_error() {
        let mut config = Config::default();
        config.apply("rkp_mode", "device");
        assert_eq!(config.keybox_source, KeyboxSource::Remote);
    }

    #[test]
    fn target_mode_and_update_settings_are_validated() {
        let mut config = Config::default();
        assert_eq!(config.target_mode, TargetMode::Auto);
        assert_eq!(config.update_channel, UpdateChannel::Stable);
        assert_eq!(config.update_base, DEFAULT_UPDATE_BASE);

        config.apply("target_mode", "manual");
        assert_eq!(config.target_mode, TargetMode::Manual);
        config.apply("target_mode", "bogus");
        assert_eq!(config.target_mode, TargetMode::Manual);

        config.apply("update_channel", "dev");
        assert_eq!(config.update_channel, UpdateChannel::Dev);
        config.apply("update_base", "https://example.test/update/");
        assert_eq!(config.update_base, "https://example.test/update");
        config.apply("update_base", "  ");
        assert_eq!(config.update_base, "https://example.test/update");

        config.apply("rkp_server", "  ");
        assert_eq!(config.rkp_server, DEFAULT_RKP_SERVER);
    }

    #[test]
    fn user_fixture_overrides_system_fixture() {
        let mut config = Config::default();
        for (key, value) in include_str!("../../../tests/fixtures/sys.conf")
            .lines()
            .filter_map(|line| line.split_once('='))
        {
            config.apply(key, value);
        }
        for (key, value) in include_str!("../../../tests/fixtures/config.conf")
            .lines()
            .filter_map(|line| line.split_once('='))
        {
            config.apply(key, value);
        }
        assert_eq!(config.root_method, "KernelSU");
        assert!(config.debug);
    }
}
```

`crates/teeforge/src/description.rs`:

```rs
use crate::config::{self, Config};
use crate::error::{Result, TfError};
use crate::logging::{self, Level};
use crate::rootdetect;
use std::fs;
use std::path::Path;
use std::time::UNIX_EPOCH;

const MODULE_PROP: &str = "/data/adb/modules/teeforge_cd/module.prop";

pub(crate) fn update(config: &Config) -> Result<()> {
    let path = Path::new(MODULE_PROP);
    if !path.is_file() {
        return Err(TfError::new(
            "模块未安装，跳过描述更新 [Module not installed; skipping description update]",
        ));
    }
    let root = rootdetect::detect();
    let keybox = config.keybox_dir.join("keybox.xml");
    let keybox_time = fs::metadata(&keybox)
        .and_then(|metadata| metadata.modified())
        .ok()
        .and_then(|time| time.duration_since(UNIX_EPOCH).ok())
        .map_or_else(|| "N/A".into(), |value| value.as_secs().to_string());
    let face = if root.method == "Unknown" {
        "(;´д`)"
    } else if keybox_time == "N/A" {
        "(・・?"
    } else {
        "( •̀ ω •́ )✧"
    };
    let description = format!(
        "{face} ✅ [{}] {} | arch: {} | keybox: {keybox_time}",
        root.method,
        crate::VERSION,
        android_abi()
    );
    config::update_key(path, "description", &description)?;
    logging::log(
        Level::Info,
        format!("模块描述已更新 [Description updated]: {description}"),
    );
    Ok(())
}

fn android_abi() -> &'static str {
    if cfg!(target_arch = "aarch64") {
        "arm64-v8a"
    } else if cfg!(target_arch = "arm") {
        "armeabi-v7a"
    } else if cfg!(target_arch = "x86_64") {
        "x86_64"
    } else if cfg!(target_arch = "x86") {
        "x86"
    } else {
        "unknown"
    }
}
```

`crates/teeforge/src/error.rs`:

```rs
use std::fmt::{self, Display, Formatter};

/// 统一错误类型。
///
/// `stage` / `code` / `hint` / `raw` 只在需要结构化输出时填充；
/// 文本模式下 `Display` 的行为保持不变，因此既有调用点无需改动。
/// Structured fields are optional so existing `Display`-only call sites keep working.
#[derive(Debug)]
pub struct TfError {
    message: String,
    stage: Option<&'static str>,
    code: Option<String>,
    hint: Option<String>,
    raw: Option<serde_json::Value>,
}

impl TfError {
    pub(crate) fn new(message: impl Into<String>) -> Self {
        Self {
            message: message.into(),
            stage: None,
            code: None,
            hint: None,
            raw: None,
        }
    }

    pub(crate) fn context(mut self, context: impl Display) -> Self {
        self.message = format!("{context}: {}", self.message);
        self
    }

    /// 带阶段与错误码的错误；`code` 保持原始值（如 TA 状态码 `-18`）。
    pub(crate) fn staged(
        stage: &'static str,
        code: impl Into<String>,
        message: impl Into<String>,
    ) -> Self {
        Self {
            message: message.into(),
            stage: Some(stage),
            code: Some(code.into()),
            hint: None,
            raw: None,
        }
    }

    /// 从跨进程回执还原结构化错误。
    ///
    /// `stage` 在 JSON 里是字符串，只能映射回已知阶段；未知阶段按无阶段处理，
    /// 避免为了传递一个标签而泄漏内存或放宽类型。
    /// Restores a structured error from a worker reply; unknown stages degrade to `None`.
    pub(crate) fn from_parts(
        stage: Option<&str>,
        code: Option<String>,
        message: impl Into<String>,
        hint: Option<String>,
        raw: Option<serde_json::Value>,
    ) -> Self {
        Self {
            message: message.into(),
            stage: stage.and_then(known_stage),
            code,
            hint,
            raw,
        }
    }

    /// 附加 i18n 提示键，供 WebUI 直接查表。
    pub(crate) fn with_hint(mut self, hint: impl Into<String>) -> Self {
        self.hint = Some(hint.into());
        self
    }

    /// 附加原始诊断数据（状态码、长度、hex 片段等）。
    pub(crate) fn with_raw(mut self, raw: serde_json::Value) -> Self {
        self.raw = Some(raw);
        self
    }

    pub(crate) fn stage(&self) -> Option<&'static str> {
        self.stage
    }

    pub(crate) fn code(&self) -> Option<&str> {
        self.code.as_deref()
    }

    pub(crate) fn hint(&self) -> Option<&str> {
        self.hint.as_deref()
    }

    pub(crate) fn raw(&self) -> Option<&serde_json::Value> {
        self.raw.as_ref()
    }

    pub(crate) fn message(&self) -> &str {
        &self.message
    }
}

impl Display for TfError {
    fn fmt(&self, f: &mut Formatter<'_>) -> fmt::Result {
        f.write_str(&self.message)
    }
}

impl std::error::Error for TfError {}

impl From<std::io::Error> for TfError {
    fn from(value: std::io::Error) -> Self {
        Self::new(value.to_string())
    }
}

pub type Result<T> = std::result::Result<T, TfError>;

/// 已知错误阶段。只用于把回执里的字符串映射回 `&'static str`。
const STAGES: [&str; 9] = [
    "prerequisite",
    "transport",
    "frame",
    "ta",
    "server",
    "keybox",
    "target",
    "update",
    "cli",
];

fn known_stage(value: &str) -> Option<&'static str> {
    STAGES.iter().copied().find(|stage| *stage == value)
}

```

`crates/teeforge/src/info.rs`:

```rs
//! 首屏所需的一次性信息采集 [One-shot information gathering for the UI]
//!
//! WebUI 打开时会调用 `--bootstrap --json`：一次 shell 调用拿到设备、Root、
//! 模块版本与能力位，避免首屏发四五条命令。
//! The UI calls `--bootstrap --json` once instead of issuing several shell calls.

use crate::config::{Config, KeyboxSource, TargetMode};
use crate::logging::{self, Level};
use crate::process;
use crate::rkp;
use serde_json::{Value, json};
use std::collections::BTreeMap;
use std::fs;

const MODULE_PROP: &str = "/data/adb/modules/teeforge_cd/module.prop";

/// `getprop` 全量输出解析成键值表。
fn properties() -> BTreeMap<String, String> {
    let mut map = BTreeMap::new();
    let Ok(output) = process::output("getprop", std::iter::empty::<&str>()) else {
        return map;
    };
    let Ok(text) = process::stdout_text(output, "getprop") else {
        return map;
    };
    for line in text.lines() {
        // 形如 `[ro.product.brand]: [Xiaomi]`
        let Some(rest) = line.strip_prefix('[') else {
            continue;
        };
        let Some((key, value)) = rest.split_once("]: [") else {
            continue;
        };
        let value = value.strip_suffix(']').unwrap_or(value);
        map.insert(key.to_owned(), value.trim().to_owned());
    }
    map
}

fn prop<'a>(map: &'a BTreeMap<String, String>, key: &str) -> &'a str {
    map.get(key).map(String::as_str).unwrap_or_default()
}

fn command_text(program: &str, args: &[&str]) -> String {
    process::output(program, args)
        .ok()
        .and_then(|output| process::stdout_text(output, program).ok())
        .map(|text| text.trim().to_owned())
        .unwrap_or_default()
}

fn module_identity() -> (String, u64) {
    let prop = fs::read_to_string(MODULE_PROP).unwrap_or_default();
    let version = prop
        .lines()
        .find_map(|line| line.strip_prefix("version="))
        .map(str::trim)
        .filter(|value| !value.is_empty())
        .map(str::to_owned)
        .unwrap_or_else(|| crate::VERSION.to_owned());
    let code = prop
        .lines()
        .find_map(|line| line.strip_prefix("versionCode="))
        .and_then(|value| value.trim().parse::<u64>().ok())
        .unwrap_or(0);
    (version, code)
}

/// 组装首屏数据。
pub(crate) fn bootstrap_value(config: &Config) -> Value {
    let props = properties();
    let brand = prop(&props, "ro.product.brand");
    let market = prop(&props, "ro.product.marketname");
    let model = prop(&props, "ro.product.model");
    let display_model = if market.is_empty() {
        format!("{brand} {model}").trim().to_owned()
    } else {
        market.to_owned()
    };
    let (version, version_code) = module_identity();
    let diagnosis = rkp::diagnose();
    let artifacts_dir = config.rkp_dir.join("keybox.xml");

    logging::log(Level::Debug, "首屏信息采集完成 [bootstrap collected]");
    json!({
        "device": {
            "brand": brand,
            "model": model,
            "name": display_model,
            "android": prop(&props, "ro.build.version.release"),
            "sdk": prop(&props, "ro.build.version.sdk"),
            "security_patch": prop(&props, "ro.build.version.security_patch"),
            "abi": prop(&props, "ro.product.cpu.abi"),
            "soc_model": prop(&props, "ro.soc.model"),
            "soc_manufacturer": prop(&props, "ro.soc.manufacturer"),
            "verified_boot": prop(&props, "ro.boot.verifiedbootstate"),
        },
        "system": {
            "kernel": command_text("uname", &["-r"]),
            "selinux": command_text("getenforce", &[]),
        },
        "module": {
            "version": version,
            "version_code": version_code,
        },
        "root": {
            "method": config.root_method,
            "version": config.root_version,
        },
        "config": {
            "keybox_source": config.keybox_source.label(),
            "keybox_remote_slot": config.keybox_remote_slot,
            "target_mode": config.target_mode.label(),
            "update_channel": config.update_channel.label(),
        },
        "capabilities": {
            "rkp_platform": rkp::device_path_supported(),
            "rkp_supported": diagnosis.blockers.is_empty(),
            "rkp_artifacts": artifacts_dir.is_file(),
        },
        "paths": {
            "rkp_dir": config.rkp_dir.display().to_string(),
            "keybox_dir": config.keybox_dir.display().to_string(),
        },
        "defaults": {
            "keybox_source": KeyboxSource::Remote.label(),
            "keybox_remote_slot": 1,
            "target_mode": TargetMode::Auto.label(),
        }
    })
}

/// 供 CLI 文本模式使用的摘要行。
pub(crate) fn bootstrap_lines(config: &Config) -> Vec<String> {
    let value = bootstrap_value(config);
    let text = |path: &[&str]| {
        path.iter()
            .fold(&value, |current, key| &current[*key])
            .as_str()
            .unwrap_or_default()
            .to_owned()
    };
    vec![
        format!(
            "设备 Device: {} {}",
            text(&["device", "brand"]),
            text(&["device", "model"])
        ),
        format!(
            "系统 System: Android {} (SDK {})",
            text(&["device", "android"]),
            text(&["device", "sdk"])
        ),
        format!(
            "模块 Module: {} ({})",
            text(&["module", "version"]),
            text(&["module", "version_code"])
        ),
        format!(
            "Root: {} {}",
            text(&["root", "method"]),
            text(&["root", "version"])
        ),
        format!(
            "Keybox 来源 Source: {} / 目标模式 Target: {}",
            text(&["config", "keybox_source"]),
            text(&["config", "target_mode"])
        ),
        format!(
            "设备端 RKP: {} / 产物: {}",
            if value["capabilities"]["rkp_supported"] == json!(true) {
                "可用 available"
            } else {
                "不可用 unavailable"
            },
            if value["capabilities"]["rkp_artifacts"] == json!(true) {
                "已生成 present"
            } else {
                "未生成 absent"
            }
        ),
    ]
}
```

`crates/teeforge/src/json.rs`:

```rs
//! `--json` 输出外壳 [JSON envelope for `--json` mode]
//!
//! 约定 [Contract]
//! - 成功：`{"ok":true,"data":{...}}`
//! - 失败：`{"ok":false,"error":{"stage":..,"code":..,"message":..,"hint":..,"raw":..}}`
//! - JSON 只写 stdout；日志仍走 stderr（`logging` 已固定为 eprintln），互不污染。
//! - 无论成功失败退出码仍保持原有语义，调用方两者都要检查。

use crate::error::TfError;
use serde_json::{Value, json};

/// 打印成功信封。
pub(crate) fn ok(data: Value) {
    println!("{}", json!({ "ok": true, "data": data }));
}

/// 打印失败信封，并返回应使用的退出码。
pub(crate) fn fail(error: &TfError) -> i32 {
    let mut payload = json!({
        "message": error.message(),
    });
    if let Some(value) = error.stage() {
        payload["stage"] = json!(value);
    }
    if let Some(value) = error.code() {
        payload["code"] = json!(value);
    }
    if let Some(value) = error.hint() {
        payload["hint"] = json!(value);
    }
    if let Some(value) = error.raw() {
        payload["raw"] = value.clone();
    }
    println!("{}", json!({ "ok": false, "error": payload }));
    1
}

/// 便捷入口：成功打印数据并返回 0，失败打印信封并返回 1。
pub(crate) fn finish(result: crate::error::Result<Value>) -> i32 {
    match result {
        Ok(data) => {
            ok(data);
            0
        }
        Err(error) => fail(&error),
    }
}
```
