`crates/teeforge/src/keybox.rs`:

```rs
use crate::atomic_file;
use crate::config::{Config, KeyboxSource};
use crate::error::{Result, TfError};
use crate::logging::{self, Level};
use crate::process;
use base64::Engine;
use base64::engine::general_purpose::STANDARD;
use sha2::{Digest, Sha256};
use std::borrow::Cow;
use std::fs;
use std::io::Read;
use std::path::{Path, PathBuf};
use std::process::{Command, Stdio};

const MAX_DOWNLOAD: usize = 2 * 1024 * 1024;

fn without_ascii_whitespace(data: &[u8]) -> Cow<'_, [u8]> {
    if data.iter().any(u8::is_ascii_whitespace) {
        Cow::Owned(
            data.iter()
                .copied()
                .filter(|byte| !byte.is_ascii_whitespace())
                .collect(),
        )
    } else {
        Cow::Borrowed(data)
    }
}

fn decode_text(encoded: &str, label: &str) -> Result<String> {
    let bytes = STANDARD
        .decode(encoded)
        .map_err(|_| TfError::new(format!("{label} base64 解码失败 [decode failed]")))?;
    String::from_utf8(bytes).map_err(|_| TfError::new(format!("{label} 不是 UTF-8 [is not UTF-8]")))
}

fn decode_transport_base64(data: &[u8], error: impl Into<String>) -> Result<Vec<u8>> {
    let normalized = without_ascii_whitespace(data);
    STANDARD
        .decode(normalized.as_ref())
        .map_err(|_| TfError::new(error))
}

fn endpoints() -> Result<(String, String)> {
    let cdn = decode_text(
        &[
            "aHR0cHM6Ly90",
            "ZWVmb3JnZS5t",
            "Y3hpYW9jaGVu",
            "LnRvcC9maWxl",
            "cy9rZXlib3gv",
        ]
        .concat(),
        "CDN URL",
    )?;
    let public_key = decode_text(
        &[
            "c3NoLWVkMjU1MTkg",
            "QUFBQUMzTnphQzFs",
            "WkRJMU5URTVBQUFB",
            "SUU5K1J3NVhadklV",
            "bW1Wc3pKR0FZRHIwV0krRWp6cHFnSCtVZ0NwL05pZlM=",
        ]
        .concat(),
        "Public key",
    )?;
    Ok((cdn, public_key))
}

fn current_month() -> Result<String> {
    let output = process::output("date", ["+%Y-%m"])?;
    let month = process::stdout_text(output, "无法读取系统月份 [Unable to read system month]")?;
    validate_month(month.trim())
}

fn validate_month(value: &str) -> Result<String> {
    let bytes = value.as_bytes();
    let month = value.get(5..).and_then(|part| part.parse::<u8>().ok());
    if bytes.len() == 7
        && bytes[4] == b'-'
        && bytes[..4].iter().all(u8::is_ascii_digit)
        && bytes[5..].iter().all(u8::is_ascii_digit)
        && month.is_some_and(|month| (1..=12).contains(&month))
    {
        return Ok(value.to_owned());
    }
    Err(TfError::new(format!(
        "系统月份格式无效 [Invalid system month format]: {value}"
    )))
}

/// 远程槽位按月轮换；设备本地月份可能早于发布方（UTC + CI 延迟），
/// 因此允许回退到最近已发布的月份。[Remote slots rotate monthly; fall back when
/// the device month is ahead of the published month.]
const REMOTE_MONTH_CANDIDATES: u8 = 3;

fn candidate_months(current: &str) -> Result<Vec<String>> {
    let month = validate_month(current)?;
    let mut year: i32 = month[..4]
        .parse()
        .map_err(|_| TfError::new("系统年份无效 [Invalid system year]"))?;
    let mut number: i32 = month[5..]
        .parse()
        .map_err(|_| TfError::new("系统月份无效 [Invalid system month]"))?;
    let mut months = Vec::with_capacity(REMOTE_MONTH_CANDIDATES as usize);
    for _ in 0..REMOTE_MONTH_CANDIDATES {
        months.push(format!("{year:04}-{number:02}"));
        number -= 1;
        if number == 0 {
            number = 12;
            year -= 1;
        }
    }
    Ok(months)
}

fn slot_filename(month: &str, slot: u8) -> Result<String> {
    validate_month(month)?;
    if !(1..=3).contains(&slot) {
        return Err(TfError::new("远程槽位无效 [Invalid remote slot]"));
    }
    let input = if slot == 1 {
        month.to_owned()
    } else {
        format!("{month}_slot_{slot}")
    };
    let digest = Sha256::digest(input.as_bytes());
    Ok(digest[..6]
        .iter()
        .map(|byte| format!("{byte:02x}"))
        .collect())
}

pub(crate) fn read_limited(mut reader: impl Read) -> Result<Vec<u8>> {
    let mut data = Vec::new();
    reader
        .by_ref()
        .take((MAX_DOWNLOAD + 1) as u64)
        .read_to_end(&mut data)?;
    if data.is_empty() {
        return Err(TfError::new("下载结果为空 [Downloaded response is empty]"));
    }
    if data.len() > MAX_DOWNLOAD {
        return Err(TfError::new(
            "下载结果超过 2 MiB 限制 [Downloaded response exceeds the 2 MiB limit]",
        ));
    }
    Ok(data)
}

fn try_download(program: &Path, args: &[&str], url: &str) -> Result<Vec<u8>> {
    let mut command = Command::new(program);
    command
        .args(args)
        .arg(url)
        .stderr(Stdio::null())
        .stdout(Stdio::piped());
    let mut child = command
        .spawn()
        .map_err(|error| TfError::from(error).context(program.display()))?;
    let stdout = child
        .stdout
        .take()
        .ok_or_else(|| TfError::new("无法读取下载输出 [Unable to read download output]"))?;
    let data = read_limited(stdout);
    if data.is_err() {
        let _ = child.kill();
    }
    let status = child.wait()?;
    if !status.success() {
        return Err(TfError::new(format!(
            "下载工具退出码 {} [Downloader exit code {}]",
            status.code().unwrap_or(128),
            status.code().unwrap_or(128)
        )));
    }
    data
}

fn download(url: &str) -> Result<Vec<u8>> {
    let mut tools: Vec<(PathBuf, Vec<&str>, &str)> = vec![
        ("wget".into(), vec!["-qO-"], "wget"),
        ("curl".into(), vec!["-fsSL"], "curl"),
    ];
    for (dir, label) in [
        ("/data/adb/ksu/bin", "KernelSU busybox wget"),
        ("/data/adb/ap/bin", "APatch busybox wget"),
        ("/data/adb/magisk", "Magisk busybox wget"),
    ] {
        if Path::new(dir).is_dir() {
            tools.push((Path::new(dir).join("busybox"), vec!["wget", "-qO-"], label));
        }
    }
    download_with(tools, |program, args| try_download(program, args, url))
}

fn download_with(
    tools: Vec<(PathBuf, Vec<&str>, &str)>,
    mut attempt: impl FnMut(&Path, &[&str]) -> Result<Vec<u8>>,
) -> Result<Vec<u8>> {
    let mut errors = Vec::new();
    for (program, args, label) in tools {
        match attempt(&program, &args) {
            Ok(data) => {
                logging::log(Level::Info, format!("下载工具 [Download tool]: {label}"));
                return Ok(data);
            }
            Err(error) => errors.push(format!("{label}: {error}")),
        }
    }
    Err(TfError::new(format!(
        "所有下载工具均失败 [All download tools failed]: {}",
        errors.join("; ")
    )))
}

fn decode_hex(data: &[u8]) -> Result<Vec<u8>> {
    let data = without_ascii_whitespace(data);
    if data.is_empty() || data.len() % 2 != 0 {
        return Err(TfError::new("hex 长度无效 [Invalid hex length]"));
    }
    fn nibble(value: u8) -> Option<u8> {
        match value {
            b'0'..=b'9' => Some(value - b'0'),
            b'a'..=b'f' => Some(value - b'a' + 10),
            b'A'..=b'F' => Some(value - b'A' + 10),
            _ => None,
        }
    }
    data.chunks_exact(2)
        .map(|pair| {
            let high = nibble(pair[0])
                .ok_or_else(|| TfError::new("非法 hex 字符 [Invalid hex character]"))?;
            let low = nibble(pair[1])
                .ok_or_else(|| TfError::new("非法 hex 字符 [Invalid hex character]"))?;
            Ok((high << 4) | low)
        })
        .collect()
}

fn decrypt(encrypted: &[u8], public_key: &str) -> Result<Vec<u8>> {
    let mut current = decode_transport_base64(
        encrypted,
        "首次 base64 解码失败 [First base64 decode failed]",
    )?;
    let key = Sha256::digest(public_key.as_bytes());
    for (index, byte) in current.iter_mut().enumerate() {
        *byte ^= key[index % key.len()];
    }
    for round in 1..=10 {
        current = decode_transport_base64(
            &current,
            format!("base64 第 {round} 层无效 [Invalid base64 at layer {round}]"),
        )?;
    }
    current = decode_hex(&current)?;
    for byte in &mut current {
        *byte = match *byte {
            b'a'..=b'm' | b'A'..=b'M' => *byte + 13,
            b'n'..=b'z' | b'N'..=b'Z' => *byte - 13,
            _ => *byte,
        };
    }
    crate::keybox_format::validate(&current)?;
    Ok(current)
}

struct SavedFile {
    bytes: Option<Vec<u8>>,
    metadata: Option<atomic_file::PreservedMetadata>,
}

impl SavedFile {
    fn read(path: &Path) -> Result<Self> {
        match fs::read(path) {
            Ok(bytes) => Ok(Self {
                bytes: Some(bytes),
                metadata: Some(atomic_file::PreservedMetadata::read(path)?),
            }),
            Err(error) if error.kind() == std::io::ErrorKind::NotFound => Ok(Self {
                bytes: None,
                metadata: None,
            }),
            Err(error) => Err(error.into()),
        }
    }

    fn restore(&self, path: &Path) -> Result<()> {
        match (&self.bytes, &self.metadata) {
            (Some(bytes), Some(metadata)) => atomic_file::write_preserved(path, bytes, metadata),
            _ => match fs::remove_file(path) {
                Ok(()) => Ok(()),
                Err(error) if error.kind() == std::io::ErrorKind::NotFound => Ok(()),
                Err(error) => Err(error.into()),
            },
        }
    }
}

struct KeyboxUpdate {
    path: PathBuf,
    data: Vec<u8>,
    original: SavedFile,
    backup: Option<(PathBuf, SavedFile)>,
    private: bool,
}

fn selection_bytes(path: &Path, config: &Config) -> Result<Vec<u8>> {
    let text = match fs::read_to_string(path) {
        Ok(text) => text,
        Err(error) if error.kind() == std::io::ErrorKind::NotFound => String::new(),
        Err(error) => return Err(error.into()),
    };
    let mut lines: Vec<String> = text
        .lines()
        .filter(|line| {
            !line.split_once('=').is_some_and(|(key, _)| {
                matches!(key.trim(), "keybox_source" | "keybox_remote_slot")
            })
        })
        .map(str::to_owned)
        .collect();
    lines.push(format!("keybox_source={}", config.keybox_source.label()));
    lines.push(format!("keybox_remote_slot={}", config.keybox_remote_slot));
    Ok(format!("{}\n", lines.join("\n")).into_bytes())
}

fn install_keyboxes_with(
    paths: &[PathBuf],
    decoded: &[u8],
    force: bool,
    selection: Option<(&Path, &Config)>,
    mut write: impl FnMut(&Path, &[u8], bool, bool) -> Result<()>,
) -> Result<Vec<(PathBuf, bool)>> {
    let mut updates = Vec::new();
    let mut targets = Vec::new();
    for path in paths {
        let original = SavedFile::read(path)?;
        let changed = force || original.bytes.as_deref() != Some(decoded);
        targets.push((path.clone(), changed));
        if changed {
            let backup = if original.bytes.is_some() {
                let path = PathBuf::from(format!("{}.bak", path.display()));
                let saved = SavedFile::read(&path)?;
                Some((path, saved))
            } else {
                None
            };
            updates.push(KeyboxUpdate {
                path: path.clone(),
                data: decoded.to_vec(),
                original,
                backup,
                private: true,
            });
        }
    }
    if let Some((path, config)) = selection {
        if paths.iter().any(|target| target == path) {
            return Err(TfError::new(
                "配置路径不能与 Keybox 目标相同 [Config overlaps keybox target]",
            ));
        }
        let original = SavedFile::read(path)?;
        let data = selection_bytes(path, config)?;
        if original.bytes.as_deref() != Some(data.as_slice()) {
            updates.push(KeyboxUpdate {
                path: path.into(),
                data,
                original,
                backup: None,
                private: false,
            });
        }
    }
    for (index, update) in updates.iter().enumerate() {
        let result = write(
            &update.path,
            &update.data,
            update.private,
            update.backup.is_some(),
        )
        .and_then(|()| {
            let actual = fs::read(&update.path)?;
            if actual != update.data {
                return Err(TfError::staged(
                    "keybox",
                    "readback",
                    "写入回读不一致 [Written file verification failed]",
                ));
            }
            Ok(())
        });
        if let Err(error) = result {
            let mut rollback_errors = Vec::new();
            // 包含本次失败的写入，因为文件可能已替换但回读失败。
            // Include the failing write: replacement may have succeeded before readback.
            for previous in updates[..=index].iter().rev() {
                if let Err(e) = previous.original.restore(&previous.path) {
                    rollback_errors.push(e.to_string());
                }
                if let Some((path, saved)) = &previous.backup {
                    if let Err(e) = saved.restore(path) {
                        rollback_errors.push(e.to_string());
                    }
                }
            }
            if !rollback_errors.is_empty() {
                return Err(TfError::new(format!(
                    "Keybox 回滚失败 [Rollback failed]: {error}; {}",
                    rollback_errors.join("; ")
                )));
            }
            return Err(error);
        }
    }
    Ok(targets)
}

fn install_keyboxes_options(
    paths: &[PathBuf],
    decoded: &[u8],
    force: bool,
    selection: Option<(&Path, &Config)>,
) -> Result<Vec<(PathBuf, bool)>> {
    install_keyboxes_with(
        paths,
        decoded,
        force,
        selection,
        |path, bytes, private, backup| match (private, backup) {
            (true, true) => atomic_file::write_private_with_backup(path, bytes),
            (true, false) => atomic_file::write_private(path, bytes),
            (false, _) => atomic_file::write(path, bytes),
        },
    )
}

#[cfg(test)]
fn install_keyboxes(paths: &[PathBuf], decoded: &[u8]) -> Result<Vec<(PathBuf, bool)>> {
    install_keyboxes_options(paths, decoded, false, None)
}

#[cfg(test)]
fn install_keybox(local: &Path, destination: &Path, decoded: &[u8]) -> Result<()> {
    install_keyboxes(&[local.to_path_buf(), destination.to_path_buf()], decoded).map(|_| ())
}

/// 安装结果：来源与每个目标的写入状态，供 JSON 输出使用。
pub(crate) struct InstallReport {
    pub(crate) source: KeyboxSource,
    pub(crate) remote_slot: Option<u8>,
    /// (路径, 是否发生写入) [path and whether it changed]
    pub(crate) targets: Vec<(PathBuf, bool)>,
}

pub(crate) fn fetch(config: &Config, force: bool, save: Option<&Path>) -> Result<InstallReport> {
    logging::log(Level::Info, "开始获取 keybox [Starting keybox fetch]...");
    let decoded = match config.keybox_source {
        KeyboxSource::Remote => remote_keybox(config.keybox_remote_slot, force)?,
        KeyboxSource::Rkp => rkp_keybox(config)?,
    };
    let targets = install(config, &decoded, force, save)?;
    logging::log(
        Level::Info,
        "Keybox 已更新并同步 [Keybox updated and synchronized]",
    );
    Ok(InstallReport {
        source: config.keybox_source,
        remote_slot: (config.keybox_source == KeyboxSource::Remote)
            .then_some(config.keybox_remote_slot),
        targets,
    })
}

/// 读取本机 RKP 产物；不联网、不回退。
fn rkp_keybox(config: &Config) -> Result<Vec<u8>> {
    let path = config.rkp_dir.join("keybox.xml");
    let bytes = match fs::read(&path) {
        Ok(value) => value,
        Err(error) if error.kind() == std::io::ErrorKind::NotFound => {
            return Err(TfError::staged(
                "keybox",
                "rkp_missing",
                "本机 RKP 产物不存在，请先在 RKP 页面签发 [RKP artifacts missing]",
            )
            .with_hint("keybox.error.rkp_missing")
            .with_raw(serde_json::json!({ "path": path.display().to_string() })));
        }
        Err(error) => {
            return Err(TfError::staged(
                "keybox",
                "rkp_unreadable",
                format!("无法读取 RKP 产物 [cannot read RKP artifacts]: {error}"),
            )
            .with_raw(serde_json::json!({ "path": path.display().to_string() })));
        }
    };
    crate::keybox_format::validate(&bytes).map_err(|_| {
        TfError::staged(
            "keybox",
            "rkp_invalid",
            "RKP 产物格式无效 [Invalid RKP artifact]",
        )
        .with_hint("keybox.error.rkp_invalid")
    })?;
    Ok(bytes)
}

fn remote_keybox(slot: u8, force: bool) -> Result<Vec<u8>> {
    let (cdn, public_key) = endpoints()?;
    let current = current_month()?;
    remote_keybox_from(&current, slot, force, |month, slot, force| {
        let mut url = format!("{cdn}{}", slot_filename(month, slot)?);
        if force {
            let nonce = std::time::SystemTime::now()
                .duration_since(std::time::UNIX_EPOCH)
                .map_err(|_| TfError::new("系统时间无效 [Invalid system time]"))?
                .as_nanos();
            url.push_str(&format!("?refresh={nonce}"));
        }
        let encrypted = download(&url)?;
        decrypt(&encrypted, &public_key)
    })
}

/// 依次尝试当前月及前两个月，只有全部失败才报错。
/// Try the current month then the previous ones; only report after all fail.
fn remote_keybox_from(
    current: &str,
    slot: u8,
    force: bool,
    mut fetch: impl FnMut(&str, u8, bool) -> Result<Vec<u8>>,
) -> Result<Vec<u8>> {
    let mut errors = Vec::new();
    for month in candidate_months(current)? {
        match fetch(&month, slot, force) {
            Ok(data) => {
                if month != current {
                    logging::log(
                        Level::Warn,
                        format!(
                            "当前月份槽位尚未发布，回退到 {month} [Current month slot not published; falling back to {month}]"
                        ),
                    );
                }
                return Ok(data);
            }
            Err(error) => errors.push(format!("{month}: {error}")),
        }
    }
    Err(TfError::new(format!(
        "远程 Keybox 获取失败 [Remote keybox fetch failed]: {}",
        errors.join("; ")
    )))
}

fn install(
    config: &Config,
    decoded: &[u8],
    force: bool,
    save: Option<&Path>,
) -> Result<Vec<(PathBuf, bool)>> {
    crate::keybox_format::validate(decoded)?;
    let mut destinations = Vec::new();
    if config
        .target_txt
        .parent()
        .is_some_and(|parent| parent.is_dir())
    {
        destinations.push(config.target_txt.parent().unwrap().join("keybox.xml"));
    }
    if config.teesim_config.is_file() {
        if let Some(parent) = config.teesim_config.parent() {
            destinations.push(parent.join("keybox.xml"));
        }
    }
    destinations.sort();
    destinations.dedup();
    if destinations.is_empty() {
        return Err(TfError::new(
            "未发现兼容目标目录，保留现有 Keybox [No compatible target directory found; existing Keybox preserved]",
        ));
    }
    fs::create_dir_all(&config.keybox_dir)?;
    let local = config.keybox_dir.join("keybox.xml");
    destinations.insert(0, local);
    destinations.sort();
    destinations.dedup();
    install_keyboxes_options(
        &destinations,
        decoded,
        force,
        save.map(|path| (path, config)),
    )
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    const VALID: &[u8] = include_bytes!("../../../tests/fixtures/keybox-valid.xml");

    #[test]
    fn slots_are_distinct_and_slot_one_is_backward_compatible() {
        let month = "2026-10";
        let legacy = format!("{:x}", Sha256::digest(month.as_bytes()));
        assert_eq!(slot_filename(month, 1).unwrap(), &legacy[..12]);
        let names = (1..=3)
            .map(|slot| slot_filename(month, slot).unwrap())
            .collect::<std::collections::HashSet<_>>();
        assert_eq!(names.len(), 3);
        assert!(slot_filename(month, 0).is_err());
        assert!(slot_filename("2026-13", 1).is_err());
    }

    #[test]
    fn candidate_months_walk_back_across_year_boundary() {
        assert_eq!(
            candidate_months("2026-11").unwrap(),
            ["2026-11", "2026-10", "2026-09"]
        );
        assert_eq!(
            candidate_months("2026-01").unwrap(),
            ["2026-01", "2025-12", "2025-11"]
        );
        assert!(candidate_months("2026-13").is_err());
        assert!(candidate_months("26-01").is_err());
    }

    #[test]
    fn remote_fetch_falls_back_to_the_last_published_month() {
        let requested = std::cell::RefCell::new(Vec::new());
        let data = remote_keybox_from("2026-11", 2, false, |month, slot, _| {
            requested.borrow_mut().push((month.to_owned(), slot));
            if month == "2026-11" {
                Err(TfError::new("404"))
            } else {
                Ok(b"payload".to_vec())
            }
        })
        .expect("previous month still published");
        assert_eq!(data, b"payload");
        assert_eq!(
            requested.borrow().as_slice(),
            [("2026-11".to_owned(), 2u8), ("2026-10".to_owned(), 2u8)]
        );
    }

    #[test]
    fn remote_fetch_reports_every_attempted_month() {
        let error = remote_keybox_from("2026-02", 1, false, |month, _, _| {
            Err(TfError::new(format!("missing {month}")))
        })
        .expect_err("no published month available");
        let message = error.to_string();
        for month in ["2026-02", "2026-01", "2025-12"] {
            assert!(message.contains(month), "{message}");
        }
    }

    fn directory(label: &str) -> PathBuf {
        let path =
            std::env::temp_dir().join(format!("teeforge-keybox-{label}-{}", std::process::id()));
        fs::create_dir_all(&path).unwrap();
        path
    }

    #[test]
    fn force_rewrites_identical_bytes_and_saves_selection_only_at_end() {
        let dir = directory("force-selection");
        let path = dir.join("keybox.xml");
        let config_path = dir.join("config.conf");
        fs::write(&path, VALID).unwrap();
        fs::write(&config_path, "# keep\ndebug=1\nkeybox_remote_slot = 1\n").unwrap();
        let config = Config {
            keybox_remote_slot: 3,
            ..Config::default()
        };
        let normal =
            install_keyboxes_options(std::slice::from_ref(&path), VALID, false, None).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(normal, vec![(path.clone(), false)]);
        assert!(!path.with_extension("xml.bak").exists());
        let forced = install_keyboxes_options(
            std::slice::from_ref(&path),
            VALID,
            true,
            Some((&config_path, &config)),
        )
        .unwrap();
        assert_eq!(forced, vec![(path.clone(), true)]);
        assert_eq!(fs::read(path.with_extension("xml.bak")).unwrap(), VALID);
        assert_eq!(fs::read(&path).unwrap(), VALID);
        let saved = fs::read_to_string(&config_path).unwrap();
        assert!(saved.contains("# keep\ndebug=1"));
        assert_eq!(saved.matches("keybox_remote_slot").count(), 1);
        assert_eq!(Config::load(&config_path).keybox_remote_slot, 3);
        fs::remove_dir_all(dir).unwrap();
    }

    #[test]
    fn readback_or_config_failure_restores_targets_backups_and_selection() {
        for corrupt in [false, true] {
            let dir = directory(if corrupt {
                "bad-readback"
            } else {
                "bad-config"
            });
            let first = dir.join("first.xml");
            let second = dir.join("second.xml");
            let backup = first.with_extension("xml.bak");
            let config_path = dir.join("config.conf");
            fs::write(&first, b"old-first").unwrap();
            fs::write(&backup, b"older-backup").unwrap();
            fs::write(&config_path, b"debug=1\nkeybox_remote_slot=1\n").unwrap();
            let config = Config {
                keybox_remote_slot: 2,
                ..Config::default()
            };
            let result = install_keyboxes_with(
                &[first.clone(), second.clone()],
                VALID,
                true,
                Some((&config_path, &config)),
                |path, data, private, needs_backup| {
                    if needs_backup {
                        atomic_file::write_private_with_backup(path, data)?;
                    } else if private {
                        atomic_file::write_private(path, data)?;
                    } else {
                        assert_eq!(fs::read(&first).unwrap(), VALID);
                        assert_eq!(fs::read(&second).unwrap(), VALID);
                        return Err(TfError::new("injected config failure"));
                    }
                    if corrupt && path == second {
                        fs::write(path, b"corrupt")?;
                    }
                    Ok(())
                },
            );
            assert!(result.is_err());
            assert_eq!(fs::read(&first).unwrap(), b"old-first");
            assert_eq!(fs::read(&backup).unwrap(), b"older-backup");
            assert!(!second.exists());
            assert_eq!(
                fs::read(&config_path).unwrap(),
                b"debug=1\nkeybox_remote_slot=1\n"
            );
            fs::remove_dir_all(dir).unwrap();
        }
    }

    #[test]
    fn invalid_install_preserves_existing_files_and_config() {
        let dir = directory("invalid-install");
        let target = dir.join("backend");
        fs::create_dir(&target).unwrap();
        fs::write(target.join("keybox.xml"), b"old").unwrap();
        let config_path = dir.join("config.conf");
        let config = Config {
            target_txt: target.join("target.txt"),
            keybox_dir: dir.join("local"),
            teesim_config: dir.join("missing"),
            ..Config::default()
        };
        assert!(
            install(
                &config,
                b"<AndroidAttestation>broken",
                true,
                Some(&config_path)
            )
            .is_err()
        );
        assert_eq!(fs::read(target.join("keybox.xml")).unwrap(), b"old");
        assert!(!config_path.exists());
        assert!(!config.keybox_dir.exists());
        fs::remove_dir_all(dir).unwrap();
    }

    #[test]
    fn ci_python_to_module_decode_install_and_readback() {
        use std::io::Write;
        let script =
            Path::new(env!("CARGO_MANIFEST_DIR")).join("../../.github/scripts/keybox_sync.py");
        let dir = directory("ci-roundtrip");
        let target = dir.join("backend");
        fs::create_dir(&target).unwrap();
        for slot in 1..=3 {
            let xml = String::from_utf8(VALID.to_vec())
                .unwrap()
                .replace("synthetic-test", &format!("synthetic-slot-{slot}"));
            let rotated: Vec<u8> = xml
                .bytes()
                .map(|b| match b {
                    b'a'..=b'm' | b'A'..=b'M' => b + 13,
                    b'n'..=b'z' | b'N'..=b'Z' => b - 13,
                    _ => b,
                })
                .collect();
            let mut raw = rotated
                .iter()
                .map(|b| format!("{b:02x}"))
                .collect::<String>()
                .into_bytes();
            for _ in 0..10 {
                raw = STANDARD.encode(raw).into_bytes();
            }
            // 使用真正的 CI 包装函数，避免测试复制一份相同实现。
            // Exercise the actual CI wrapper rather than duplicating it in the test.
            let mut child = Command::new(if cfg!(windows) { "python" } else { "python3" })
                .args(["-c", "import runpy,sys; m=runpy.run_path(sys.argv[1]); sys.stdout.buffer.write(m['wrap'](sys.stdin.buffer.read(), b'synthetic-key'))"])
                .arg(&script).stdin(Stdio::piped()).stdout(Stdio::piped()).stderr(Stdio::piped()).spawn().unwrap();
            child.stdin.take().unwrap().write_all(&raw).unwrap();
            let output = child.wait_with_output().unwrap();
            assert!(output.status.success(), "CI wrapper failed");
            let decoded = decrypt(&output.stdout, "synthetic-key").unwrap();
            assert!(decoded == xml.as_bytes(), "decoded bytes differ");
            let config = Config {
                target_txt: target.join("target.txt"),
                keybox_dir: dir.join("local"),
                teesim_config: dir.join("missing"),
                keybox_remote_slot: slot,
                ..Config::default()
            };
            let config_path = dir.join("config.conf");
            let report = install(&config, &decoded, false, Some(&config_path)).unwrap();
            assert_eq!(report.len(), 2);
            for (path, changed) in report {
                assert!(changed);
                assert!(fs::read(path).unwrap() == xml.as_bytes());
            }
            assert_eq!(Config::load(&config_path).keybox_remote_slot, slot);
        }
        fs::remove_dir_all(dir).unwrap();
    }

    #[test]
    fn strict_hex_rejects_partial_and_invalid_input() {
        assert!(decode_hex(b"abc").is_err());
        assert!(decode_hex(b"00xz").is_err());
        assert!(decode_hex(b" \t\r\n").is_err());
        assert_eq!(decode_hex(b"4142").expect("valid hex"), b"AB");
    }

    #[test]
    fn hex_decoder_accepts_ascii_transport_whitespace() {
        assert_eq!(decode_hex(b" \t41\r\n42\n").expect("wrapped hex"), b"AB");
    }

    #[test]
    fn month_format_is_strict() {
        assert_eq!(validate_month("2026-09").expect("valid month"), "2026-09");
        assert!(validate_month("2026-13").is_err());
        assert!(validate_month("26-09").is_err());
    }

    #[test]
    fn download_limit_rejects_oversized_input() {
        let data = vec![b'x'; MAX_DOWNLOAD + 1];
        assert!(read_limited(data.as_slice()).is_err());
    }

    #[test]
    fn downloader_falls_back_after_a_failed_tool() {
        let tools = vec![
            (PathBuf::from("first"), vec!["-q"], "first"),
            (PathBuf::from("second"), vec!["-q"], "second"),
        ];
        let mut attempts = 0;
        let result = download_with(tools, |program, _| {
            attempts += 1;
            if program == Path::new("first") {
                Err(TfError::new("mock failure"))
            } else {
                Ok(b"payload".to_vec())
            }
        })
        .expect("second downloader succeeds");
        assert_eq!(attempts, 2);
        assert_eq!(result, b"payload");
    }

    #[test]
    fn base64_decoder_rejects_invalid_characters() {
        assert!(STANDARD.decode(b"%%%=").is_err());
        assert!(decode_transport_base64(b" %%%=\r\n", "invalid").is_err());
        assert!(decode_transport_base64(b"AA=A", "invalid padding").is_err());
        assert!(decode_transport_base64(b"A", "truncated").is_err());
        assert!(
            decrypt(
                include_bytes!("../../../tests/fixtures/invalid-download.txt"),
                "test-key"
            )
            .is_err()
        );
    }

    #[test]
    fn decrypts_a_valid_contract_fixture() {
        let public_key = "ssh-ed25519 test-key";
        let xml = include_bytes!("../../../tests/fixtures/keybox-valid.xml");
        let mut encoded = xml
            .iter()
            .map(|byte| match *byte {
                b'a'..=b'm' | b'A'..=b'M' => *byte + 13,
                b'n'..=b'z' | b'N'..=b'Z' => *byte - 13,
                _ => *byte,
            })
            .map(|byte| format!("{byte:02x}"))
            .collect::<String>()
            .into_bytes();
        for _ in 0..10 {
            encoded = STANDARD.encode(encoded).into_bytes();
        }
        let key = Sha256::digest(public_key.as_bytes());
        for (index, byte) in encoded.iter_mut().enumerate() {
            *byte ^= key[index % key.len()];
        }
        let encrypted = format!(" \t{}\r\n", STANDARD.encode(encoded));
        assert_eq!(
            decrypt(encrypted.as_bytes(), public_key).expect("valid fixture"),
            xml
        );
    }

    #[test]
    fn decrypts_base64_with_transport_whitespace() {
        fn wrap(data: String) -> Vec<u8> {
            let mut wrapped = Vec::new();
            for (index, chunk) in data.as_bytes().chunks(48).enumerate() {
                if index > 0 {
                    wrapped.extend_from_slice(if index % 2 == 0 { b"\n" } else { b"\r\n" });
                }
                wrapped.extend_from_slice(chunk);
            }
            wrapped
        }

        let public_key = "ssh-ed25519 test-key";
        let xml = include_bytes!("../../../tests/fixtures/keybox-valid.xml");
        let mut encoded = xml
            .iter()
            .map(|byte| match *byte {
                b'a'..=b'm' | b'A'..=b'M' => *byte + 13,
                b'n'..=b'z' | b'N'..=b'Z' => *byte - 13,
                _ => *byte,
            })
            .map(|byte| format!("{byte:02x}"))
            .collect::<String>()
            .into_bytes();
        for _ in 0..10 {
            encoded = wrap(STANDARD.encode(encoded));
        }
        let key = Sha256::digest(public_key.as_bytes());
        for (index, byte) in encoded.iter_mut().enumerate() {
            *byte ^= key[index % key.len()];
        }
        let encrypted = STANDARD.encode(encoded);

        assert_eq!(
            decrypt(encrypted.as_bytes(), public_key).expect("wrapped base64 fixture"),
            xml
        );
    }

    #[test]
    fn failed_second_write_restores_local_keybox() {
        let directory =
            std::env::temp_dir().join(format!("teeforge-keybox-{}", std::process::id()));
        fs::create_dir_all(&directory).expect("create keybox fixture");
        let local = directory.join("keybox.xml");
        fs::write(&local, b"old").expect("write old keybox");
        let invalid_destination = directory.join("missing").join("keybox.xml");
        fs::create_dir_all(&invalid_destination).expect("block destination parent with a file");
        assert!(install_keybox(&local, &invalid_destination, b"new").is_err());
        assert_eq!(fs::read(&local).expect("read rolled back keybox"), b"old");
        let _ = fs::remove_dir_all(directory);
    }

    #[test]
    fn unchanged_keybox_is_not_rewritten() {
        let directory =
            std::env::temp_dir().join(format!("teeforge-keybox-unchanged-{}", std::process::id()));
        fs::create_dir_all(&directory).expect("create keybox fixture");
        let path = directory.join("keybox.xml");
        fs::write(&path, b"same").expect("write keybox");
        install_keyboxes(std::slice::from_ref(&path), b"same").expect("skip unchanged keybox");
        assert!(!path.with_extension("xml.bak").exists());
        assert_eq!(fs::read(&path).expect("read keybox"), b"same");
        let _ = fs::remove_dir_all(directory);
    }

    #[test]
    fn rkp_source_without_artifacts_fails_without_network() {
        let directory =
            std::env::temp_dir().join(format!("teeforge-keybox-rkp-{}", std::process::id()));
        let config = Config {
            keybox_source: KeyboxSource::Rkp,
            rkp_dir: directory.clone(),
            ..Config::default()
        };
        let error = rkp_keybox(&config).expect_err("missing artifacts must fail");
        assert_eq!(error.stage(), Some("keybox"));
        assert_eq!(error.code(), Some("rkp_missing"));

        std::fs::create_dir_all(&directory).expect("create fixture");
        std::fs::write(directory.join("keybox.xml"), b"<html/>").expect("write bad artifact");
        let error = rkp_keybox(&config).expect_err("invalid artifact must fail");
        assert_eq!(error.code(), Some("rkp_invalid"));
        let _ = std::fs::remove_dir_all(directory);
    }

    #[cfg(unix)]
    #[test]
    fn keybox_is_written_owner_only() {
        use std::os::unix::fs::PermissionsExt;
        let directory =
            std::env::temp_dir().join(format!("teeforge-keybox-mode-{}", std::process::id()));
        fs::create_dir_all(&directory).expect("create keybox fixture");
        let path = directory.join("keybox.xml");
        install_keyboxes(std::slice::from_ref(&path), b"<AndroidAttestation/>")
            .expect("write keybox");
        let mode = fs::metadata(&path)
            .expect("keybox metadata")
            .permissions()
            .mode();
        assert_eq!(mode & 0o777, 0o600);
        let _ = fs::remove_dir_all(directory);
    }
}

```

`crates/teeforge/src/keybox_format.rs`:

```rs
use crate::error::{Result, TfError};
use base64::Engine;
use base64::engine::general_purpose::STANDARD;
use roxmltree::{Document, Node};
use x509_cert::Certificate;
use x509_cert::der::asn1::{AnyRef, OctetStringRef, UintRef};
use x509_cert::der::{Decode, Reader, SliceReader};

fn invalid(code: &str) -> TfError {
    TfError::staged(
        "keybox",
        code,
        "Keybox 格式校验失败，保留旧文件 [Invalid keybox; existing files preserved]",
    )
}

fn children<'a>(node: Node<'a, 'a>, name: &str) -> Vec<Node<'a, 'a>> {
    node.children()
        .filter(|n| {
            n.is_element() && n.tag_name().name() == name && n.tag_name().namespace().is_none()
        })
        .collect()
}

fn one<'a>(node: Node<'a, 'a>, name: &str) -> Result<Node<'a, 'a>> {
    let found = children(node, name);
    if found.len() != 1 {
        return Err(invalid("xml_structure"));
    }
    Ok(found[0])
}

fn shape(node: Node<'_, '_>, allowed: &[&str]) -> Result<()> {
    if node.children().any(|n| {
        (n.is_element()
            && (n.tag_name().namespace().is_some() || !allowed.contains(&n.tag_name().name())))
            || (n.is_text() && !n.text().unwrap_or_default().trim().is_empty())
    }) {
        return Err(invalid("xml_structure"));
    }
    Ok(())
}

fn count(node: Node<'_, '_>, name: &str, actual: usize) -> Result<()> {
    let count = one(node, name)?;
    if count.children().any(|n| n.is_element()) {
        return Err(invalid("xml_count"));
    }
    let declared = count.text().and_then(|s| s.trim().parse::<usize>().ok());
    if actual == 0 || declared != Some(actual) {
        return Err(invalid("xml_count"));
    }
    Ok(())
}

fn pem(node: Node<'_, '_>, allowed: &[&str]) -> Result<(String, Vec<u8>)> {
    if node.attribute("format") != Some("pem") || node.children().any(|n| n.is_element()) {
        return Err(invalid("pem_format"));
    }
    let text = node.text().unwrap_or_default().trim();
    for label in allowed {
        let begin = format!("-----BEGIN {label}-----");
        let end = format!("-----END {label}-----");
        if let Some(body) = text.strip_prefix(&begin).and_then(|s| s.strip_suffix(&end)) {
            let body: String = body.chars().filter(|c| !c.is_ascii_whitespace()).collect();
            let der = STANDARD.decode(body).map_err(|_| invalid("pem_encoding"))?;
            if der.is_empty() {
                return Err(invalid("pem_encoding"));
            }
            return Ok(((*label).into(), der));
        }
    }
    Err(invalid("pem_label"))
}

fn private_der(data: &[u8], rsa: bool) -> x509_cert::der::Result<()> {
    let mut reader = SliceReader::new(data)?;
    reader.sequence(|reader| {
        let version = reader.decode::<u8>()?;
        if version != if rsa { 0 } else { 1 } {
            return Err(reader.error(x509_cert::der::ErrorKind::Failed));
        }
        if rsa {
            for _ in 0..8 {
                reader.decode::<UintRef<'_>>()?;
            }
        } else {
            let key = reader.decode::<OctetStringRef<'_>>()?;
            if key.as_bytes().is_empty() {
                return Err(reader.error(x509_cert::der::ErrorKind::Failed));
            }
            while !reader.is_finished() {
                reader.decode::<AnyRef<'_>>()?;
            }
        }
        Ok(())
    })?;
    reader.finish(())
}

/// 仅验证结构和编码；不把格式有效等同于信任、未吊销或后端验收。
/// Validate structure/encoding only, not trust, revocation or backend acceptance.
pub(crate) fn validate(data: &[u8]) -> Result<()> {
    let text = std::str::from_utf8(data).map_err(|_| invalid("xml_utf8"))?;
    let doc = Document::parse(text).map_err(|_| invalid("xml_parse"))?;
    let root = doc.root_element();
    if root.tag_name().name() != "AndroidAttestation" || root.tag_name().namespace().is_some() {
        return Err(invalid("xml_root"));
    }
    let boxes = children(root, "Keybox");
    shape(root, &["NumberOfKeyboxes", "Keybox"])?;
    count(root, "NumberOfKeyboxes", boxes.len())?;
    for keybox in boxes {
        shape(keybox, &["Key"])?;
        if keybox
            .attribute("DeviceID")
            .is_none_or(|s| s.trim().is_empty())
        {
            return Err(invalid("xml_device_id"));
        }
        let keys = children(keybox, "Key");
        if keys.is_empty() {
            return Err(invalid("xml_keys"));
        }
        let mut algorithms = Vec::new();
        for key in keys {
            shape(key, &["PrivateKey", "CertificateChain"])?;
            let algorithm = key.attribute("algorithm").unwrap_or_default();
            if !matches!(algorithm, "rsa" | "ecdsa") || algorithms.contains(&algorithm) {
                return Err(invalid("xml_algorithm"));
            }
            algorithms.push(algorithm);
            let rsa = algorithm == "rsa";
            let labels = if rsa {
                ["RSA PRIVATE KEY", "PRIVATE KEY"]
            } else {
                ["EC PRIVATE KEY", "PRIVATE KEY"]
            };
            let (label, bytes) = pem(one(key, "PrivateKey")?, &labels)?;
            if label == "PRIVATE KEY" {
                let info = p256::pkcs8::PrivateKeyInfo::from_der(&bytes)
                    .map_err(|_| invalid("private_key_der"))?;
                let expected = if rsa {
                    "1.2.840.113549.1.1.1"
                } else {
                    "1.2.840.10045.2.1"
                };
                if info.algorithm.oid.to_string() != expected {
                    return Err(invalid("private_key_algorithm"));
                }
                private_der(info.private_key, rsa).map_err(|_| invalid("private_key_der"))?;
            } else {
                private_der(&bytes, rsa).map_err(|_| invalid("private_key_der"))?;
            }
            let chain = one(key, "CertificateChain")?;
            shape(chain, &["NumberOfCertificates", "Certificate"])?;
            let certificates = children(chain, "Certificate");
            count(chain, "NumberOfCertificates", certificates.len())?;
            for certificate in certificates {
                let (_, bytes) = pem(certificate, &["CERTIFICATE"])?;
                Certificate::from_der(&bytes).map_err(|_| invalid("certificate_der"))?;
            }
        }
    }
    Ok(())
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;
    const VALID: &str = include_str!("../../../tests/fixtures/keybox-valid.xml");

    #[test]
    fn accepts_multiple_algorithms_and_keyboxes() {
        validate(VALID.as_bytes()).unwrap();
        let start = VALID.find("<Keybox ").unwrap();
        let end = VALID.find("</Keybox>").unwrap() + "</Keybox>".len();
        let multi = VALID
            .replace("<NumberOfKeyboxes>1", "<NumberOfKeyboxes>2")
            .replace(
                "</AndroidAttestation>",
                &format!("{}</AndroidAttestation>", &VALID[start..end]),
            );
        validate(multi.as_bytes()).unwrap();
    }

    #[test]
    fn rejects_broken_structure_counts_and_pem_without_echoing_material() {
        let mutations = [
            VALID.replace("</AndroidAttestation>", ""),
            VALID.replace("<NumberOfKeyboxes>1", "<NumberOfKeyboxes>2"),
            VALID.replace("<NumberOfCertificates>1", "<NumberOfCertificates>2"),
            VALID.replace("PrivateKey", "MissingKey"),
            VALID.replace("-----BEGIN CERTIFICATE-----", "-----BEGIN PUBLIC KEY-----"),
            VALID.replace("-----END CERTIFICATE-----", "!-----END CERTIFICATE-----"),
            VALID.replace("DeviceID=\"synthetic-test\"", ""),
            VALID.replace("algorithm=\"rsa\"", "algorithm=\"ecdsa\""),
            format!("<html><!-- AndroidAttestation -->{VALID}</html>"),
            "<AndroidAttestation>marker-only</AndroidAttestation>".into(),
            format!("{VALID}\0"),
        ];
        for data in mutations {
            let error = validate(data.as_bytes()).expect_err("reject malformed fixture");
            assert_eq!(error.stage(), Some("keybox"));
            assert!(!error.to_string().contains("BEGIN"));
        }
    }
}
```

`crates/teeforge/src/logging.rs`:

```rs
use crate::config::Config;
use std::fs::{self, OpenOptions};
use std::io::Write;
use std::sync::{Mutex, OnceLock};
use std::time::{SystemTime, UNIX_EPOCH};

#[derive(Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq, PartialOrd, Ord)]
pub(crate) enum Level {
    Debug,
    Info,
    Warn,
    Error,
}

struct State {
    level: Level,
    debug_file: Option<std::path::PathBuf>,
}

static STATE: OnceLock<Mutex<State>> = OnceLock::new();

pub(crate) fn init(config: &Config, verbose: bool) {
    let timestamp = now();
    let debug_file = config.debug.then(|| {
        config
            .log_dir
            .join(format!("teeforge_{}.log", timestamp / 86_400))
    });
    if let Some(path) = &debug_file {
        let _ = fs::create_dir_all(path.parent().unwrap_or_else(|| std::path::Path::new(".")));
        if let Some(directory) = path.parent() {
            cleanup_old_logs(directory);
        }
    }
    let state = State {
        level: if verbose { Level::Debug } else { Level::Info },
        debug_file,
    };
    let _ = STATE.set(Mutex::new(state));
}

fn cleanup_old_logs(directory: &std::path::Path) {
    let Ok(entries) = fs::read_dir(directory) else {
        return;
    };
    let mut logs = entries
        .flatten()
        .filter_map(|entry| {
            let name = entry.file_name();
            let name_text = name.to_string_lossy();
            (entry.file_type().ok()?.is_file()
                && name_text.starts_with("teeforge_")
                && name_text.ends_with(".log"))
            .then_some((name, entry.path()))
        })
        .collect::<Vec<_>>();
    logs.sort_by(|left, right| right.0.cmp(&left.0));
    for (_, path) in logs.into_iter().skip(15) {
        let _ = fs::remove_file(path);
    }
}

fn now() -> u64 {
    SystemTime::now()
        .duration_since(UNIX_EPOCH)
        .map_or(0, |value| value.as_secs())
}

pub(crate) fn log(level: Level, message: impl AsRef<str>) {
    if let Some(state) = STATE.get()
        && let Ok(state) = state.lock()
        && level < state.level
    {
        return;
    }
    let label = match level {
        Level::Debug => "DEBUG",
        Level::Info => "INFO",
        Level::Warn => "WARN",
        Level::Error => "ERROR",
    };
    let line = format!("[{}] [{label}] {}", now(), message.as_ref());
    eprintln!("{line}");

    let Some(state) = STATE.get() else {
        return;
    };
    let Ok(state) = state.lock() else {
        return;
    };
    if let Some(path) = &state.debug_file
        && let Ok(mut file) = OpenOptions::new().create(true).append(true).open(path)
    {
        let _ = writeln!(file, "{line}");
    }
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    #[test]
    fn keeps_only_newest_fifteen_log_files() {
        let directory =
            std::env::temp_dir().join(format!("teeforge-logs-{}-{}", std::process::id(), now()));
        fs::create_dir_all(&directory).expect("create log fixture");
        for index in 0..18 {
            fs::write(directory.join(format!("teeforge_{index:02}.log")), b"log")
                .expect("write log fixture");
        }
        fs::write(directory.join("unrelated.txt"), b"keep").expect("write unrelated fixture");
        cleanup_old_logs(&directory);
        let remaining = fs::read_dir(&directory)
            .expect("read fixture")
            .flatten()
            .filter(|entry| entry.file_name().to_string_lossy().ends_with(".log"))
            .count();
        assert_eq!(remaining, 15);
        assert!(directory.join("unrelated.txt").is_file());
        let _ = fs::remove_dir_all(directory);
    }
}
```

`crates/teeforge/src/omk.rs`:

```rs
use crate::error::{Result, TfError};
use std::collections::BTreeSet;
use std::ops::Range;

const BEGIN: &str = "# BEGIN TeeForge-CD managed scoop";
const END: &str = "# END TeeForge-CD managed scoop";

fn invalid(reason: &str) -> TfError {
    TfError::new(format!(
        "OMK 作用域配置无效，未覆盖 [Invalid OMK scope; not overwritten]: {reason}"
    ))
}

// 只用于识别注释和扩展表头；TOML 语法和数值仍由解析器验证。
// Identify comments and extension headers only; the TOML parser validates syntax and values.
fn lexical_classes(text: &str) -> Vec<u8> {
    let bytes = text.as_bytes();
    let mut classes = vec![0; bytes.len()];
    let mut i = 0;
    while i < bytes.len() {
        if bytes[i] == b'#' {
            while i < bytes.len() && bytes[i] != b'\n' {
                classes[i] = 2;
                i += 1;
            }
        } else if bytes[i] == b'"' || bytes[i] == b'\'' {
            let quote = bytes[i];
            let triple = bytes.get(i..i + 3) == Some(&[quote; 3]);
            let start = i;
            i += if triple { 3 } else { 1 };
            while i < bytes.len() {
                if quote == b'"' && bytes[i] == b'\\' {
                    i = (i + 2).min(bytes.len());
                } else if bytes[i] == quote {
                    let mut count = 1;
                    while bytes.get(i + count) == Some(&quote) {
                        count += 1;
                    }
                    if !triple || count >= 3 {
                        i += if triple { count } else { 1 };
                        break;
                    }
                    i += count;
                } else {
                    i += 1;
                }
            }
            classes[start..i].fill(1);
        } else {
            i += 1;
        }
    }
    classes
}

fn parse_view(text: &str, classes: &[u8]) -> Result<String> {
    let alias = (0..1000)
        .map(|n| format!("_o{n:03}"))
        .find(|alias| !text.contains(alias))
        .ok_or_else(|| invalid("extension namespace collision"))?;
    let mut view = text.to_owned();
    let mut offset = 0;
    for line in text.split_inclusive('\n') {
        let trimmed = line.trim_start_matches([' ', '\t']);
        let start = offset + line.len() - trimmed.len();
        if trimmed.starts_with("[scoop.") && classes[start] == 0 {
            // 等长替换使解析 span 与原文件字节位置一致。
            // Equal-length aliases preserve byte offsets in the original file.
            view.replace_range(start + 1..start + 6, &alias);
        }
        offset += line.len();
    }
    Ok(view)
}

fn markers(text: &str, classes: &[u8], array: &Range<usize>) -> Result<Option<Range<usize>>> {
    let mut begin = None;
    let mut end = None;
    let mut offset = 0;
    for line in text.split_inclusive('\n') {
        let trimmed = line.trim();
        let start = offset + line.len() - line.trim_start().len();
        if classes.get(start) == Some(&2)
            && (trimmed.starts_with(BEGIN) || trimmed.starts_with(END))
            && trimmed != BEGIN
            && trimmed != END
        {
            return Err(invalid("damaged managed marker"));
        }
        if (trimmed == BEGIN || trimmed == END) && classes.get(start) == Some(&2) {
            if offset <= array.start || offset + line.len() > array.end {
                return Err(invalid("managed marker outside scoop"));
            }
            let slot = if trimmed == BEGIN {
                &mut begin
            } else {
                &mut end
            };
            if slot.replace(offset..offset + line.len()).is_some() {
                return Err(invalid("duplicate managed marker"));
            }
        }
        offset += line.len();
    }
    match (begin, end) {
        (None, None) => Ok(None),
        (Some(begin), Some(end)) if begin.end <= end.start => Ok(Some(begin.start..end.end)),
        _ => Err(invalid("incomplete or reversed managed markers")),
    }
}

pub(crate) fn render(text: &str, packages: &[String]) -> Result<String> {
    let classes = lexical_classes(text);
    let view = parse_view(text, &classes)?;
    // 解析错误可能包含整行配置，日志只报告类别。
    // Parser errors may expose configuration lines; report only the category.
    let document = toml_edit::ImDocument::parse(&view).map_err(|_| invalid("TOML syntax"))?;
    if let Some(version) = document.get("version")
        && !matches!(version.as_integer(), Some(0 | 1))
    {
        return Err(invalid("unsupported version"));
    }
    let array = document
        .get("scoop")
        .and_then(|value| value.as_array())
        .ok_or_else(|| invalid("scoop must be a string array"))?;
    let span = array.span().ok_or_else(|| invalid("missing scoop span"))?;
    let managed = markers(text, &classes, &span)?;
    let mut manual = BTreeSet::new();
    let mut last_manual = None;
    for value in array.iter() {
        let name = value
            .as_str()
            .ok_or_else(|| invalid("non-string scoop entry"))?;
        let value_span = value.span().ok_or_else(|| invalid("missing entry span"))?;
        if let Some(region) = &managed {
            if value_span.start < region.end && value_span.end > region.start {
                if value_span.start < region.start || value_span.end > region.end {
                    return Err(invalid("entry crosses managed marker"));
                }
                continue;
            }
        }
        manual.insert(name.trim().to_owned());
        last_manual = Some(value_span);
    }
    let newline = if text.contains("\r\n") { "\r\n" } else { "\n" };
    let generated: BTreeSet<_> = packages
        .iter()
        .filter(|name| !manual.contains(name.as_str()))
        .collect();
    let mut block = format!("  {BEGIN}{newline}");
    for name in generated {
        // 扫描器已校验包名，仍使用 JSON 字符串转义作为 TOML 基本字符串。
        // Package names are validated by the scanner; JSON escaping also forms a TOML basic string.
        block.push_str(&format!(
            "  {},{newline}",
            serde_json::to_string(name).map_err(|_| invalid("package encoding"))?
        ));
    }
    block.push_str(&format!("  {END}{newline}"));
    let mut result = text.to_owned();
    if let Some(region) = managed {
        result.replace_range(region, &block);
    } else {
        let close = span.end - 1;
        // 在最后一个手动值后补逗号，不移动行尾注释。
        // Insert the separator after the value without moving trailing comments.
        let comma = last_manual
            .as_ref()
            .filter(|value| {
                !(value.end..close).any(|i| classes[i] == 0 && text.as_bytes()[i] == b',')
            })
            .map(|value| value.end);
        let prefix = if text[..close].ends_with('\n') {
            ""
        } else {
            newline
        };
        result.insert_str(close, &format!("{prefix}{block}"));
        if let Some(at) = comma {
            result.insert(at, ',');
        }
    }
    // 复核生成结果；不把扩展表头映射写回文件。
    // Validate the result without persisting the extension-header aliases.
    let validation = parse_view(&result, &lexical_classes(&result))?;
    toml_edit::ImDocument::parse(validation).map_err(|_| invalid("rendered TOML syntax"))?;
    Ok(result)
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    #[test]
    fn preserves_manual_comments_extensions_and_is_idempotent() {
        let input = "# title\nversion = 1\nscoop = ['manual.app' # keep\n]\n[main]\nenabled = true\n[scoop.manual.app]\nmode = 'strict'\n";
        let output = render(
            input,
            &["new.app".into(), "manual.app".into(), "new.app".into()],
        )
        .unwrap();
        assert!(output.contains("'manual.app', # keep"));
        assert!(output.ends_with("[main]\nenabled = true\n[scoop.manual.app]\nmode = 'strict'\n"));
        assert_eq!(output, render(&output, &["new.app".into()]).unwrap());
        let removed = render(&output, &[]).unwrap();
        assert!(!removed.contains("\"new.app\""));
        assert!(removed.contains("'manual.app'"));
    }

    #[test]
    fn supports_old_versions_inline_arrays_and_crlf() {
        for version in ["", "version = 0\r\n", "version = 1\r\n"] {
            let input = format!("{version}scoop = [\"manual.app\"] # keep\r\n");
            let output = render(&input, &["new.app".into()]).unwrap();
            assert!(output.starts_with(version));
            assert!(output.ends_with("] # keep\r\n"));
            assert_eq!(output, render(&output, &["new.app".into()]).unwrap());
        }
    }

    #[test]
    fn rejects_invalid_inputs_without_exposing_contents() {
        for input in [
            "scoop = [1]",
            "scoop = {}",
            "version = 2\nscoop = []",
            "scoop = []\nscoop = []",
            "other = []",
            "scoop = [\n# BEGIN TeeForge-CD managed scoop\n]",
            "# BEGIN TeeForge-CD managed scoop\nscoop = []",
            "scoop = [\n# END TeeForge-CD managed scoop\n# BEGIN TeeForge-CD managed scoop\n]",
        ] {
            assert!(render(input, &[]).is_err(), "{input}");
        }
    }

    #[test]
    fn marker_text_in_multiline_strings_is_not_a_marker() {
        let input =
            "note = '''\n[scoop.not.a.table]\n# BEGIN TeeForge-CD managed scoop\n'''\nscoop = []\n";
        let output = render(input, &[]).unwrap();
        assert!(output.starts_with(input.split("scoop = []").next().unwrap()));
        assert_eq!(output, render(&output, &[]).unwrap());
    }

    #[test]
    fn preserves_manual_entries_on_both_sides_of_managed_region() {
        let input = format!(
            "scoop = [\n'before.app', # before\n  {BEGIN}\n  'old.app',\n  {END}\n'after.app' # after\n]\n"
        );
        let output = render(&input, &["new.app".into(), "after.app".into()]).unwrap();
        assert!(output.contains("'before.app', # before"));
        assert!(output.contains("'after.app' # after"));
        assert!(!output.contains("old.app"));
        assert_eq!(output, render(&output, &["new.app".into()]).unwrap());
        assert!(render(&input.replace(BEGIN, &format!("{BEGIN} broken")), &[]).is_err());
    }
}
```

`crates/teeforge/src/process.rs`:

```rs
use crate::error::{Result, TfError};
use std::ffi::OsStr;
use std::io::{self, Read};
use std::process::{Command, Output, Stdio};
use std::sync::Arc;
use std::sync::atomic::{AtomicBool, Ordering};
use std::thread::{self, JoinHandle};
use std::time::{Duration, Instant};

const MAX_STDERR_CHARS: usize = 512;
const MAX_CAPTURED_STDERR_BYTES: usize = 16 * 1024;
const WAIT_INTERVAL: Duration = Duration::from_millis(10);

pub(crate) fn output<I, S>(program: &str, args: I) -> Result<Output>
where
    I: IntoIterator<Item = S>,
    S: AsRef<OsStr>,
{
    Command::new(program)
        .args(args)
        .output()
        .map_err(|e| TfError::from(e).context(program))
}

struct BoundedRead {
    data: Vec<u8>,
    overflowed: bool,
}

fn read_bounded(
    mut reader: impl Read,
    limit: usize,
    overflow_signal: Option<&AtomicBool>,
) -> io::Result<BoundedRead> {
    let mut data = Vec::with_capacity(limit.min(8192));
    let mut buffer = [0_u8; 8192];
    let mut overflowed = false;
    loop {
        let count = reader.read(&mut buffer)?;
        if count == 0 {
            break;
        }
        let remaining = limit.saturating_sub(data.len());
        let retained = remaining.min(count);
        data.extend_from_slice(&buffer[..retained]);
        overflowed |= retained < count;
        if overflowed && let Some(signal) = overflow_signal {
            signal.store(true, Ordering::Release);
        }
    }
    Ok(BoundedRead { data, overflowed })
}

fn join_reader(handle: JoinHandle<io::Result<BoundedRead>>, context: &str) -> Result<BoundedRead> {
    handle
        .join()
        .map_err(|_| TfError::new(format!("{context}，读取线程异常 [reader thread failed]")))?
        .map_err(|error| TfError::from(error).context(context))
}

pub(crate) fn output_bounded<I, S>(
    program: &str,
    args: I,
    timeout: Duration,
    stdout_limit: usize,
    context: &str,
) -> Result<Output>
where
    I: IntoIterator<Item = S>,
    S: AsRef<OsStr>,
{
    let mut command = Command::new(program);
    command.args(args);
    run_bounded(command, program, timeout, stdout_limit, context)
}

/// 运行已配置好的命令，并保留超时、输出上限与双管道排空行为。
/// Runs a preconfigured command with the same timeout, output cap and pipe draining.
pub(crate) fn run_bounded(
    mut command: Command,
    label: &str,
    timeout: Duration,
    stdout_limit: usize,
    context: &str,
) -> Result<Output> {
    let mut child = command
        .stdin(Stdio::null())
        .stdout(Stdio::piped())
        .stderr(Stdio::piped())
        .spawn()
        .map_err(|error| {
            TfError::new(format!(
                "{context}，无法启动 {label} [failed to start {label}]: {error}"
            ))
        })?;
    let Some(stdout) = child.stdout.take() else {
        let _ = child.kill();
        let _ = child.wait();
        return Err(TfError::new(format!(
            "{context}，无法读取 stdout [stdout unavailable]"
        )));
    };
    let Some(stderr) = child.stderr.take() else {
        let _ = child.kill();
        let _ = child.wait();
        return Err(TfError::new(format!(
            "{context}，无法读取 stderr [stderr unavailable]"
        )));
    };
    // 同时排空两个管道，避免子进程因任一缓冲区写满而无法退出。
    // Drain both pipes concurrently so neither full buffer can block child exit.
    let stdout_overflow = Arc::new(AtomicBool::new(false));
    let reader_overflow = Arc::clone(&stdout_overflow);
    let stdout_reader =
        thread::spawn(move || read_bounded(stdout, stdout_limit, Some(&reader_overflow)));
    let stderr_reader =
        thread::spawn(move || read_bounded(stderr, MAX_CAPTURED_STDERR_BYTES, None));
    let started = Instant::now();

    let status = loop {
        if stdout_overflow.load(Ordering::Acquire) {
            let _ = child.kill();
            let _ = child.wait();
            let _ = join_reader(stdout_reader, context);
            let _ = join_reader(stderr_reader, context);
            return Err(TfError::new(format!(
                "{context}，输出超过限制 [output exceeds limit]: {stdout_limit} bytes"
            )));
        }
        match child.try_wait() {
            Ok(Some(status)) => break status,
            Ok(None) if started.elapsed() < timeout => {
                thread::sleep(WAIT_INTERVAL.min(timeout.saturating_sub(started.elapsed())));
            }
            Ok(None) => {
                let _ = child.kill();
                let _ = child.wait();
                let _ = join_reader(stdout_reader, context);
                let _ = join_reader(stderr_reader, context);
                return Err(TfError::new(format!(
                    "{context}，执行超时 [timed out]: {}s",
                    timeout.as_secs()
                )));
            }
            Err(error) => {
                let _ = child.kill();
                let _ = child.wait();
                let _ = join_reader(stdout_reader, context);
                let _ = join_reader(stderr_reader, context);
                return Err(TfError::new(format!(
                    "{context}，无法等待子进程 [failed to wait for child]: {error}"
                )));
            }
        }
    };
    let stdout = join_reader(stdout_reader, context);
    let stderr = join_reader(stderr_reader, context);
    let stdout = stdout?;
    let stderr = stderr?;
    if stdout.overflowed {
        return Err(TfError::new(format!(
            "{context}，输出超过限制 [output exceeds limit]: {stdout_limit} bytes"
        )));
    }
    Ok(Output {
        status,
        stdout: stdout.data,
        stderr: stderr.data,
    })
}

pub(crate) fn status<I, S>(program: &str, args: I) -> Result<i32>
where
    I: IntoIterator<Item = S>,
    S: AsRef<OsStr>,
{
    let status = Command::new(program)
        .args(args)
        .stdin(Stdio::null())
        .status()
        .map_err(|e| TfError::from(e).context(program))?;
    Ok(status.code().unwrap_or(128))
}

pub(crate) fn stdout_text(output: Output, context: &str) -> Result<String> {
    if !output.status.success() {
        // 外部命令输出不可信；只保留单行且有长度上限的错误摘要。
        // External command output is untrusted; retain only a bounded one-line summary.
        let stderr = stderr_summary(&output.stderr);
        let detail = (!stderr.is_empty()).then(|| format!(": {stderr}"));
        let code = output.status.code().unwrap_or(128);
        return Err(TfError::new(format!(
            "{context}，退出码 {code} [exit code {code}]{}",
            detail.unwrap_or_default()
        )));
    }
    String::from_utf8(output.stdout)
        .map_err(|_| TfError::new(format!("{context} 输出不是 UTF-8 [output is not UTF-8]")))
}

fn stderr_summary(stderr: &[u8]) -> String {
    let normalized = String::from_utf8_lossy(stderr)
        .split_whitespace()
        .collect::<Vec<_>>()
        .join(" ");
    let mut chars = normalized.chars();
    let summary = chars.by_ref().take(MAX_STDERR_CHARS).collect::<String>();
    if chars.next().is_some() {
        format!("{summary}...")
    } else {
        summary
    }
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;
    use std::time::Duration;

    fn shell_output(command: &str, limit: usize, timeout: Duration) -> Result<Output> {
        if cfg!(windows) {
            output_bounded(
                "cmd",
                ["/C", command],
                timeout,
                limit,
                "测试命令 [test command]",
            )
        } else {
            output_bounded(
                "sh",
                ["-c", command],
                timeout,
                limit,
                "测试命令 [test command]",
            )
        }
    }

    #[test]
    fn stderr_summary_is_single_line_and_bounded() {
        assert_eq!(
            stderr_summary(b"  first\r\n second\tthird  "),
            "first second third"
        );
        let summary = stderr_summary("错".repeat(MAX_STDERR_CHARS + 1).as_bytes());
        assert_eq!(summary.chars().count(), MAX_STDERR_CHARS + 3);
        assert!(summary.ends_with("..."));
    }

    #[test]
    fn failed_command_reports_exit_code_and_stderr() {
        let output = if cfg!(windows) {
            shell_output(
                "echo package manager unavailable 1>&2 & exit /B 7",
                1024,
                Duration::from_secs(2),
            )
        } else {
            shell_output(
                "echo package manager unavailable >&2; exit 7",
                1024,
                Duration::from_secs(2),
            )
        }
        .expect("run failing command");
        let error = stdout_text(output, "获取包列表失败 [Failed to list packages]")
            .expect_err("non-zero command must fail")
            .to_string();
        assert!(error.contains("exit code 7"));
        assert!(error.contains("package manager unavailable"));
    }

    #[test]
    fn bounded_command_returns_stdout_and_stderr() {
        let command = if cfg!(windows) {
            "echo package-list & echo diagnostic 1>&2"
        } else {
            "printf 'package-list\\n'; printf 'diagnostic\\n' >&2"
        };
        let output = shell_output(command, 1024, Duration::from_secs(2)).expect("bounded output");
        assert!(output.status.success());
        assert!(String::from_utf8_lossy(&output.stdout).contains("package-list"));
        assert!(String::from_utf8_lossy(&output.stderr).contains("diagnostic"));
    }

    #[test]
    fn bounded_command_rejects_stdout_over_limit() {
        let command = if cfg!(windows) {
            "echo 123456789"
        } else {
            "printf '123456789\\n'"
        };
        let error = shell_output(command, 4, Duration::from_secs(2))
            .expect_err("oversized stdout must fail")
            .to_string();
        assert!(error.contains("输出超过限制 [output exceeds limit]"));
    }

    #[test]
    fn bounded_command_times_out_and_reaps_child() {
        let command = if cfg!(windows) {
            "ping -n 3 127.0.0.1 >NUL"
        } else {
            "sleep 2"
        };
        let error = shell_output(command, 1024, Duration::from_millis(50))
            .expect_err("slow command must time out")
            .to_string();
        assert!(error.contains("执行超时 [timed out]"));
    }

    #[test]
    fn bounded_stderr_capture_is_truncated() {
        let input = vec![b'x'; MAX_CAPTURED_STDERR_BYTES + 128];
        let captured = read_bounded(input.as_slice(), MAX_CAPTURED_STDERR_BYTES, None)
            .expect("read bounded stderr");
        assert_eq!(captured.data.len(), MAX_CAPTURED_STDERR_BYTES);
        assert!(captured.overflowed);
    }

    #[test]
    fn bounded_command_reports_spawn_failure() {
        let error = output_bounded(
            "teeforge-command-that-does-not-exist",
            std::iter::empty::<&str>(),
            Duration::from_secs(1),
            1024,
            "测试命令 [test command]",
        )
        .expect_err("missing command must fail")
        .to_string();
        assert!(error.contains("无法启动 teeforge-command-that-does-not-exist"));
    }
}
```

`crates/teeforge/src/rkp/elf.rs`:

```rs
//! 极简 ELF64 动态符号读取 [Minimal ELF64 dynsym reader]
//!
//! 只用于诊断：列出厂商库导出的方法，判断是否存在必须先调用的初始化入口、
//! `send_cmd` 的其它重载，或构造函数的不同变体。
//! 不解析重定位、不加载库、不执行任何厂商代码。
//!
//! Values are read through bounds-checked accessors so a malformed file returns an
//! error instead of panicking.

use crate::error::{Result, TfError};
use std::fs;

const SHT_DYNSYM: u32 = 11;
const SYMBOL_SIZE: usize = 24;
const STT_FUNC: u8 = 2;

/// 读取 `path` 中名字包含 `needle` 的函数符号，去重排序。
pub(crate) fn function_symbols(path: &str, needle: &str) -> Result<Vec<String>> {
    let data = fs::read(path)
        .map_err(|error| TfError::new(format!("读取 {path} 失败 [read failed]: {error}")))?;
    parse(&data, needle)
}

fn parse(data: &[u8], needle: &str) -> Result<Vec<String>> {
    if data.get(..4) != Some(b"\x7fELF") {
        return Err(fail("不是 ELF 文件 [not an ELF file]"));
    }
    if data.get(4) != Some(&2) {
        return Err(fail("只支持 64 位 ELF [only ELF64 is supported]"));
    }
    if data.get(5) != Some(&1) {
        return Err(fail("只支持小端 ELF [only little-endian is supported]"));
    }

    let section_offset = usize_of(u64_at(data, 0x28)?)?;
    // e_shentsize(0x3a) 与 e_shnum(0x3c) 都是 u16，不能按 u64 读。
    let section_size = usize_of(u64::from(u16_at(data, 0x3a).unwrap_or(64)))?;
    let section_count = usize_of(u64::from(u16_at(data, 0x3c).unwrap_or(0)))?;
    if section_size < 64 || section_count == 0 {
        return Err(fail("节表不可用 [section table unavailable]"));
    }

    // (类型, 偏移, 长度, 关联字符串表索引)
    let mut sections = Vec::with_capacity(section_count);
    for index in 0..section_count {
        let base = section_offset
            .checked_add(index.checked_mul(section_size).ok_or_else(overflow)?)
            .ok_or_else(overflow)?;
        sections.push((
            u32_at(data, base + 4)?,
            usize_of(u64_at(data, base + 24)?)?,
            usize_of(u64_at(data, base + 32)?)?,
            u32_at(data, base + 40)? as usize,
        ));
    }

    let mut names = Vec::new();
    for index in 0..sections.len() {
        let (kind, offset, size, link) = sections[index];
        if kind != SHT_DYNSYM {
            continue;
        }
        let Some((_, string_offset, string_size, _)) = sections.get(link) else {
            continue;
        };
        let Some(strings) = data.get(*string_offset..string_offset.saturating_add(*string_size))
        else {
            continue;
        };
        for symbol in 0..(size / SYMBOL_SIZE) {
            let Some(entry) = offset.checked_add(symbol * SYMBOL_SIZE) else {
                break;
            };
            let Some(name_offset) = u32_at(data, entry)
                .ok()
                .and_then(|value| usize::try_from(value).ok())
            else {
                continue;
            };
            let Some(info) = data.get(entry + 4) else {
                continue;
            };
            if info & 0x0f != STT_FUNC {
                continue;
            }
            if let Some(name) = read_name(strings, name_offset) {
                if name.contains(needle) {
                    names.push(name);
                }
            }
        }
    }
    names.sort();
    names.dedup();
    Ok(names)
}

fn read_name(strings: &[u8], offset: usize) -> Option<String> {
    let tail = strings.get(offset..)?;
    let end = tail.iter().position(|byte| *byte == 0)?;
    std::str::from_utf8(&tail[..end]).ok().map(str::to_owned)
}

fn u16_at(data: &[u8], offset: usize) -> Result<u16> {
    Ok(u16::from_le_bytes(
        data.get(offset..offset + 2)
            .ok_or_else(|| fail("ELF 读取越界 [out of bounds]"))?
            .try_into()
            .map_err(|_| fail("ELF 字段长度错误 [bad field width]"))?,
    ))
}

fn u32_at(data: &[u8], offset: usize) -> Result<u32> {
    Ok(u32::from_le_bytes(
        data.get(offset..offset + 4)
            .ok_or_else(|| fail("ELF 读取越界 [out of bounds]"))?
            .try_into()
            .map_err(|_| fail("ELF 字段长度错误 [bad field width]"))?,
    ))
}

fn u64_at(data: &[u8], offset: usize) -> Result<u64> {
    Ok(u64::from_le_bytes(
        data.get(offset..offset + 8)
            .ok_or_else(|| fail("ELF 读取越界 [out of bounds]"))?
            .try_into()
            .map_err(|_| fail("ELF 字段长度错误 [bad field width]"))?,
    ))
}

/// `u64` 到 `usize`；32 位目标上超出范围视为越界。
fn usize_of(value: u64) -> Result<usize> {
    usize::try_from(value).map_err(|_| fail("偏移超出地址空间 [offset exceeds address space]"))
}

fn overflow() -> TfError {
    fail("节表偏移溢出 [section offset overflow]")
}

fn fail(message: impl Into<String>) -> TfError {
    TfError::new(format!("符号表 [symbol table]: {}", message.into()))
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    #[test]
    fn non_elf_data_is_rejected() {
        assert!(parse(b"not an elf file", "send").is_err());
    }

    #[test]
    fn truncated_header_is_rejected() {
        assert!(parse(b"\x7fELF\x02\x01", "send").is_err());
    }

    #[test]
    fn zero_section_table_is_rejected() {
        let mut data = vec![0u8; 64];
        data[..4].copy_from_slice(b"\x7fELF");
        data[4] = 2;
        data[5] = 1;
        assert!(parse(&data, "send").is_err());
    }

    #[test]
    fn name_reader_stops_at_nul() {
        assert_eq!(read_name(b"send_cmd\0x", 0).as_deref(), Some("send_cmd"));
        assert!(read_name(b"send", 9).is_none());
    }
}
```

`crates/teeforge/src/rkp/explain.rs`:

```rs
//! KeyMint 状态码解释 [KeyMint status code explanations]
//!
//! 名称来自 AOSP `hardware/interfaces/security/keymint/aidl/.../ErrorCode.aidl`，
//! 说明是本项目针对 RKP 场景补充的。未收录的码统一回落到原始码 + 通用建议，
//! 不猜测含义。
//! Names come from AOSP's ErrorCode.aidl; the explanations are ours. Unknown
//! codes fall back to the raw value with a generic hint instead of a guess.

use crate::error::TfError;
use serde_json::json;

struct Meaning {
    name: &'static str,
    zh: &'static str,
    en: &'static str,
}

const fn entry(name: &'static str, zh: &'static str, en: &'static str) -> Meaning {
    Meaning { name, zh, en }
}

fn lookup(code: i32) -> Option<Meaning> {
    let meaning = match code {
        -18 => entry(
            "INCOMPATIBLE_KEY_FORMAT",
            "TA 不接受当前密钥格式，软件密钥调用 CSR_V2 时最常见",
            "The TA rejects this key format; common with software keys on CSR_V2",
        ),
        -38 => entry(
            "INVALID_ARGUMENT",
            "请求参数被 TA 拒绝，多为固件实现差异",
            "The TA rejected the request arguments, usually a firmware difference",
        ),
        -39 => entry(
            "UNSUPPORTED_TAG",
            "TA 不支持请求中的某个 tag",
            "The TA does not support one of the requested tags",
        ),
        -40 => entry(
            "INVALID_TAG",
            "请求中的 tag 取值非法",
            "A tag in the request has an invalid value",
        ),
        -63 => entry(
            "ATTESTATION_CHALLENGE_MISSING",
            "请求缺少证明 challenge",
            "The attestation challenge is missing",
        ),
        -64 => entry(
            "KEYMINT_NOT_CONFIGURED",
            "TA 尚未完成配置",
            "The TA is not configured yet",
        ),
        -65 => entry(
            "ATTESTATION_APPLICATION_ID_MISSING",
            "请求缺少应用标识",
            "The attestation application id is missing",
        ),
        -68 => entry(
            "HARDWARE_TYPE_UNAVAILABLE",
            "请求的硬件类型不可用（例如要求 TEE 但只有 StrongBox）",
            "The requested hardware type is unavailable",
        ),
        -74 => entry(
            "ATTESTATION_KEYS_NOT_PROVISIONED",
            "设备未预置证明密钥，无法本机签发",
            "Attestation keys are not provisioned on this device",
        ),
        -75 => entry(
            "ATTESTATION_IDS_NOT_PROVISIONED",
            "设备未预置证明 ID",
            "Attestation ids are not provisioned",
        ),
        -100 => entry(
            "UNIMPLEMENTED",
            "固件未实现该命令",
            "The firmware does not implement this command",
        ),
        -101 => entry(
            "VERSION_MISMATCH",
            "命令版本与 TA 不匹配",
            "The command version does not match the TA",
        ),
        -1000 => entry(
            "UNKNOWN_ERROR",
            "TA 内部错误",
            "The TA reported an internal error",
        ),
        _ => return None,
    };
    Some(meaning)
}

/// 把 TA 状态码转成带阶段与原始值的错误。
pub(crate) fn status_error(code: i32) -> TfError {
    let Some(meaning) = lookup(code) else {
        return TfError::staged(
            "ta",
            code.to_string(),
            format!("KeyMint 返回 {code}（未收录的代号）[KeyMint returned {code}: unmapped]"),
        )
        .with_hint("rkp.ta.status")
        .with_raw(json!({ "keymint": code }));
    };
    TfError::staged(
        "ta",
        code.to_string(),
        format!(
            "KeyMint 返回 {code}（{}）：{} [{}]",
            meaning.name, meaning.zh, meaning.en
        ),
    )
    .with_hint("rkp.ta.status")
    .with_raw(json!({ "keymint": code, "name": meaning.name }))
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    #[test]
    fn known_code_carries_name_and_stage() {
        let error = status_error(-18);
        assert_eq!(error.stage(), Some("ta"));
        assert_eq!(error.code(), Some("-18"));
        assert!(error.message().contains("INCOMPATIBLE_KEY_FORMAT"));
        assert_eq!(error.hint(), Some("rkp.ta.status"));
    }

    #[test]
    fn unknown_code_is_not_guessed() {
        let error = status_error(-4242);
        assert_eq!(error.code(), Some("-4242"));
        assert!(error.message().contains("未收录的代号"));
    }
}
```
