`crates/teeforge/src/rkp/keybox.rs`:

```rs
//! 签发证书链校验与 keybox 生成 [Issued chain validation and keybox output]
//!
//! 移植自 Duck-ToolBox 的 `duck-rkp-device::artifacts` 与 `duck-rkp::keybox_xml`（MIT）。
//! Ported from Duck-ToolBox `duck-rkp-device::artifacts` and `duck-rkp::keybox_xml` (MIT).
//!
//! 写入前必须确认：证书链是唯一的 leaf→root 路径，且叶子公钥与本机私钥匹配。
//! Before writing, the chain must be exactly one leaf-to-root path and its leaf
//! must carry the public key of the private key we keep.

use crate::error::{Result, TfError};
use base64::Engine;
use base64::engine::general_purpose::STANDARD;
use p256::SecretKey;
use sha2::{Digest, Sha256};
use std::fmt::Write as _;
use x509_cert::Certificate;
use x509_cert::der::asn1::ObjectIdentifier;
use x509_cert::der::{Decode, Encode};

const MAX_CERTIFICATES: usize = 8;
const EC_PUBLIC_KEY_OID: &str = "1.2.840.10045.2.1";
const P256_CURVE_OID: &str = "1.2.840.10045.3.1.7";

#[derive(Clone, Debug)]
struct ParsedCertificate {
    der: Vec<u8>,
    subject_der: Vec<u8>,
    issuer_der: Vec<u8>,
    /// 未压缩 P-256 公钥；非 EC P-256 主体时为 `None`。
    public_key: Option<Vec<u8>>,
}

fn fail(message: impl Into<String>) -> TfError {
    TfError::new(format!(
        "RKP 证书链 [RKP certificate chain]: {}",
        message.into()
    ))
}

/// 完整产物：XML 之外还带上便于排查的 PEM 链与元信息。
/// Everything the caller needs to persist: XML plus a readable chain and metadata.
pub(crate) struct Built {
    pub(crate) xml: String,
    pub(crate) chain_pem: String,
    pub(crate) device_id: String,
    pub(crate) certificates: usize,
}

/// 同 `build`，但额外返回 PEM 链、device id 与证书数量。
pub(crate) fn build_full(secret: &SecretKey, chain_der: &[u8]) -> Result<Built> {
    let chain = parse_chain(chain_der)?;
    ensure_leaf_matches(secret, &chain)?;
    let ordered = leaf_to_root(&chain)?;
    if ordered.len() != chain.len() {
        return Err(fail(
            "存在不在 leaf→root 路径上的证书 [certificate outside the path]",
        ));
    }
    let device_id = device_id(secret);
    let private_key = secret
        .to_sec1_der()
        .map_err(|error| fail(format!("导出私钥失败 [encode private key]: {error}")))?;
    let ordered_certificates = ordered
        .iter()
        .map(|index| &chain[*index])
        .collect::<Vec<_>>();
    let mut chain_pem = String::new();
    for certificate in &ordered_certificates {
        chain_pem.push_str(&pem("CERTIFICATE", &certificate.der));
    }
    Ok(Built {
        xml: render_xml(&private_key, &ordered_certificates, &device_id),
        chain_pem,
        device_id,
        certificates: ordered_certificates.len(),
    })
}

fn parse_chain(data: &[u8]) -> Result<Vec<ParsedCertificate>> {
    if data.is_empty() {
        return Err(fail("证书链为空 [empty chain]"));
    }
    if data.len() > crate::rkp::MAX_CHAIN_BYTES {
        return Err(fail("证书链过大 [chain too large]"));
    }
    let mut certificates = Vec::new();
    let mut offset = 0usize;
    while offset < data.len() {
        let slice = &data[offset..];
        let (header, body) = der_len(slice)?;
        let total = header
            .checked_add(body)
            .ok_or_else(|| fail("DER 长度溢出 [length overflow]"))?;
        let end = offset
            .checked_add(total)
            .ok_or_else(|| fail("DER 长度溢出 [length overflow]"))?;
        if end > data.len() {
            return Err(fail("证书截断 [truncated certificate]"));
        }
        let der = &data[offset..end];
        let certificate = Certificate::from_der(der)
            .map_err(|error| fail(format!("解析 X.509 证书失败 [parse certificate]: {error}")))?;
        let tbs = &certificate.tbs_certificate;
        certificates.push(ParsedCertificate {
            der: der.to_vec(),
            subject_der: tbs
                .subject
                .to_der()
                .map_err(|error| fail(format!("编码 subject 失败 [encode subject]: {error}")))?,
            issuer_der: tbs
                .issuer
                .to_der()
                .map_err(|error| fail(format!("编码 issuer 失败 [encode issuer]: {error}")))?,
            public_key: p256_spki(tbs),
        });
        if certificates.len() > MAX_CERTIFICATES {
            return Err(fail("证书数量超过 8 [more than 8 certificates]"));
        }
        offset = end;
    }
    Ok(certificates)
}

fn der_len(bytes: &[u8]) -> Result<(usize, usize)> {
    if bytes.len() < 2 {
        return Err(fail("DER 序列截断 [truncated DER]"));
    }
    if bytes[0] != 0x30 {
        return Err(fail(
            "DER 证书必须以 SEQUENCE 开头 [must start with SEQUENCE]",
        ));
    }
    if bytes[1] & 0x80 == 0 {
        return Ok((2, usize::from(bytes[1])));
    }
    if bytes[1] == 0x80 {
        return Err(fail("不支持 DER 不定长 [indefinite length]"));
    }
    let count = usize::from(bytes[1] & 0x7f);
    if count == 0 || count > std::mem::size_of::<usize>() {
        return Err(fail("DER 长度字段无效 [invalid length field]"));
    }
    let length_bytes = bytes
        .get(2..2 + count)
        .ok_or_else(|| fail("DER 长度截断 [truncated length]"))?;
    let mut length = 0usize;
    for byte in length_bytes {
        length = (length << 8) | usize::from(*byte);
    }
    Ok((2 + count, length))
}

/// 叶子证书：链中没有其他证书以其为 issuer。
fn leaf_index(chain: &[ParsedCertificate]) -> Option<usize> {
    chain.iter().position(|candidate| {
        !chain.iter().any(|other| {
            other.subject_der != other.issuer_der && other.issuer_der == candidate.subject_der
        })
    })
}

fn ensure_leaf_matches(secret: &SecretKey, chain: &[ParsedCertificate]) -> Result<()> {
    let leaf = leaf_index(chain)
        .and_then(|index| chain[index].public_key.as_deref())
        .ok_or_else(|| fail("找不到可用的叶子证书 [no usable leaf certificate]"))?;
    if leaf != secret.public_key().to_sec1_bytes().as_ref() {
        return Err(fail(
            "叶子公钥与本机私钥不匹配 [leaf key does not match the private key]",
        ));
    }
    Ok(())
}

fn leaf_to_root(chain: &[ParsedCertificate]) -> Result<Vec<usize>> {
    let start = leaf_index(chain).ok_or_else(|| fail("找不到叶子证书 [no leaf certificate]"))?;
    let mut current = start;
    let mut ordered = vec![start];
    while chain[current].issuer_der != chain[current].subject_der {
        let issuer = chain
            .iter()
            .position(|candidate| candidate.subject_der == chain[current].issuer_der)
            .ok_or_else(|| fail("证书链缺少签发者 [missing issuer]"))?;
        if ordered.contains(&issuer) {
            return Err(fail("证书链存在环 [cycle in chain]"));
        }
        ordered.push(issuer);
        current = issuer;
    }
    Ok(ordered)
}

fn p256_spki(tbs: &x509_cert::TbsCertificate) -> Option<Vec<u8>> {
    let spki = &tbs.subject_public_key_info;
    if spki.algorithm.oid.to_string() != EC_PUBLIC_KEY_OID {
        return None;
    }
    let curve = spki
        .algorithm
        .parameters
        .as_ref()?
        .decode_as::<ObjectIdentifier>()
        .ok()?;
    if curve.to_string() != P256_CURVE_OID {
        return None;
    }
    let point = spki.subject_public_key.raw_bytes();
    p256::PublicKey::from_sec1_bytes(point)
        .ok()
        .map(|key| key.to_sec1_bytes().to_vec())
}

fn device_id(secret: &SecretKey) -> String {
    let digest = Sha256::digest(secret.public_key().to_sec1_bytes());
    let suffix = digest[..6]
        .iter()
        .map(|byte| format!("{byte:02x}"))
        .collect::<String>();
    format!("teeforge-{suffix}")
}

fn render_xml(private_key_der: &[u8], chain: &[&ParsedCertificate], device_id: &str) -> String {
    let mut xml = String::new();
    writeln!(xml, "<?xml version=\"1.0\"?>").unwrap();
    writeln!(xml, "<AndroidAttestation>").unwrap();
    writeln!(xml, "    <NumberOfKeyboxes>1</NumberOfKeyboxes>").unwrap();
    writeln!(
        xml,
        "    <Keybox DeviceID=\"{}\">",
        escape_attribute(device_id)
    )
    .unwrap();
    writeln!(xml, "        <Key algorithm=\"ecdsa\">").unwrap();
    writeln!(xml, "            <PrivateKey format=\"pem\">").unwrap();
    writeln!(
        xml,
        "{}",
        indent(&pem("EC PRIVATE KEY", private_key_der), "                ")
    )
    .unwrap();
    writeln!(xml, "            </PrivateKey>").unwrap();
    writeln!(xml, "            <CertificateChain>").unwrap();
    writeln!(
        xml,
        "                <NumberOfCertificates>{}</NumberOfCertificates>",
        chain.len()
    )
    .unwrap();
    for certificate in chain {
        let certificate_pem = pem("CERTIFICATE", &certificate.der);
        writeln!(xml, "                <Certificate format=\"pem\">").unwrap();
        writeln!(xml, "{}", indent(&certificate_pem, "                    ")).unwrap();
        writeln!(xml, "                </Certificate>").unwrap();
    }
    writeln!(xml, "            </CertificateChain>").unwrap();
    writeln!(xml, "        </Key>").unwrap();
    writeln!(xml, "    </Keybox>").unwrap();
    writeln!(xml, "</AndroidAttestation>").unwrap();
    xml
}

fn pem(label: &str, der: &[u8]) -> String {
    let encoded = STANDARD.encode(der);
    let mut pem = String::new();
    writeln!(pem, "-----BEGIN {label}-----").unwrap();
    for chunk in encoded.as_bytes().chunks(64) {
        writeln!(pem, "{}", String::from_utf8_lossy(chunk)).unwrap();
    }
    write!(pem, "-----END {label}-----").unwrap();
    pem
}

fn indent(block: &str, prefix: &str) -> String {
    block
        .lines()
        .map(|line| format!("{prefix}{line}"))
        .collect::<Vec<_>>()
        .join("\n")
}

fn escape_attribute(value: &str) -> String {
    let mut escaped = String::with_capacity(value.len());
    for character in value.chars() {
        match character {
            '&' => escaped.push_str("&amp;"),
            '<' => escaped.push_str("&lt;"),
            '>' => escaped.push_str("&gt;"),
            '"' => escaped.push_str("&quot;"),
            '\'' => escaped.push_str("&apos;"),
            _ => escaped.push(character),
        }
    }
    escaped
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    fn secret(seed: u8) -> SecretKey {
        SecretKey::from_slice(&[seed; 32]).expect("valid scalar")
    }

    #[test]
    fn der_length_parser_handles_short_and_long_forms() {
        assert_eq!(der_len(&[0x30, 0x05]).unwrap(), (2, 5));
        assert_eq!(der_len(&[0x30, 0x81, 0x80]).unwrap(), (3, 128));
        assert_eq!(der_len(&[0x30, 0x82, 0x01, 0x00]).unwrap(), (4, 256));
        assert!(der_len(&[0x30, 0x80]).is_err());
        assert!(der_len(&[0x31, 0x00]).is_err());
    }

    #[test]
    fn truncated_chain_is_rejected() {
        assert!(parse_chain(&[0x30, 0x05, 0x01]).is_err());
        assert!(parse_chain(&[]).is_err());
    }

    #[test]
    fn device_id_is_stable_and_escaped() {
        let first = device_id(&secret(1));
        assert_eq!(first, device_id(&secret(1)));
        assert!(first.starts_with("teeforge-"));
        assert_eq!(escape_attribute("a\"<&>'"), "a&quot;&lt;&amp;&gt;&apos;");
    }

    #[test]
    fn pem_wraps_at_64_columns() {
        let block = pem("CERTIFICATE", &[0u8; 96]);
        assert!(block.starts_with("-----BEGIN CERTIFICATE-----\n"));
        assert!(block.ends_with("-----END CERTIFICATE-----"));
        for line in block
            .lines()
            .skip(1)
            .take_while(|line| !line.starts_with("-----"))
        {
            assert!(line.len() <= 64);
        }
    }
}

```

`crates/teeforge/src/rkp/mod.rs`:

```rs
//! 设备端 RKP 签发 [Device-backed RKP issuance]
//!
//! 来源与许可 [Origin and license]
//! 本模块移植并适配自 Duck-ToolBox 的 Device RKP Issuer：
//! Ported and adapted from Duck-ToolBox's Device RKP Issuer:
//!   https://github.com/eltavine/Duck-ToolBox
//!   参考修订 Revision: fb6b1e427f2773ac6139d2eba27068fbdfd089de
//!   Copyright (c) 2026 Eltavine, MIT License（见 module/licenses/duck-toolbox.txt）
//!
//! 相对上游的裁剪 [Deviations from upstream]
//! - 仅保留 V2（`CSR_V2` / 0x3122）设备路径，不包含 V1 AIDL、原生 V1 与离线工作台。
//!   V2 only; the V1 AIDL bridge, native V1 and the offline workbench are not included.
//! - HTTP 改用设备上的 `curl` / `wget`，不引入 reqwest 与异步运行时。
//!   HTTP uses the device's `curl` / `wget`; no reqwest and no async runtime.
//! - 本地只校验 CSR 结构与 challenge/keysToSign 绑定；DICE/COSE 密码学校验由 Google
//!   RKP 服务端完成，非法 CSR 不会签发证书。
//!   Only structure and binding are checked locally; Google performs the
//!   cryptographic validation and never signs an invalid CSR.
//!
//! 职责边界 [Boundary]
//! 本模块只负责"产出产物"（写入 `RKP/`），不负责安装到 Tricky Store 等后端；
//! 安装由 `keybox` 模块按 `--source rkp` 完成。两者解耦，互不回退。
//! This module only produces artifacts; installation lives in `keybox`.

use crate::config::Config;
use crate::error::{Result, TfError};
use crate::logging::{self, Level};
use crate::process;
use p256::SecretKey;
use serde_json::{Value, json};
use sha2::{Digest, Sha256};
use std::fs;
use std::io::Read;
use std::path::{Path, PathBuf};
use std::time::{Instant, SystemTime, UNIX_EPOCH};

mod elf;
mod explain;
mod keybox;
mod protocol;
mod server;
mod soc;
mod ta;
mod worker;

const VENDOR_LIBRARY: &str = "/vendor/lib64/libkeymasterdeviceutils.so";
const SMCINVOKE: &str = "/dev/smcinvoke";
/// 服务端证书链的拼接上限 [Upper bound for the concatenated server chain].
pub(crate) const MAX_CHAIN_BYTES: usize = 0x4000;

/// 一次签发的结果，足以落盘与展示。
pub(crate) struct Issued {
    pub(crate) keybox_xml: String,
    pub(crate) chain_pem: String,
    pub(crate) device_id: String,
    pub(crate) certificates: usize,
    pub(crate) fingerprint: String,
    pub(crate) duration_ms: u128,
    /// 命中的响应帧布局，写入 `meta.json` 便于回溯固件差异。
    pub(crate) frame_layout: &'static str,
}

/// 落盘后的产物路径。
pub(crate) struct SavedArtifacts {
    pub(crate) keybox: PathBuf,
    pub(crate) chain: PathBuf,
    pub(crate) meta: PathBuf,
}

pub(crate) struct Diagnosis {
    pub(crate) arm64_android: bool,
    pub(crate) library_present: bool,
    pub(crate) device_present: bool,
    pub(crate) fingerprint: String,
    pub(crate) manufacturer: String,
    pub(crate) soc_model: String,
    pub(crate) detection: soc::Detection,
    pub(crate) blockers: Vec<(&'static str, String)>,
}

/// 执行一次完整签发：前置检查 → 生成密钥 → 取 challenge → 调 TA → 取证书 → 生成 XML。
pub(crate) fn issue(config: &Config) -> Result<Issued> {
    let started = Instant::now();
    let diagnosis = diagnose();
    if let Some((id, detail)) = diagnosis.blockers.first() {
        return Err(blocker_error(id, detail));
    }
    logging::log(
        Level::Info,
        format!(
            "设备端 RKP 签发 [Device RKP issuance]: {}",
            if diagnosis.manufacturer.is_empty() {
                "Qualcomm"
            } else {
                &diagnosis.manufacturer
            }
        ),
    );

    let key = generate_key()?;
    let tmp = work_dir(config);
    let challenge = server::fetch_challenge(&config.rkp_server, &diagnosis.fingerprint, &tmp)?;
    logging::log(
        Level::Debug,
        format!("RKP challenge: {} 字节 [bytes]", challenge.len()),
    );

    let cose_key = protocol::cose_key(&key.public_key())?;
    let request = protocol::build_request(&challenge, &cose_key)?;
    let frame = worker::sign(&tmp, &request)?;
    // 帧只在这里解一次。worker 返回原始缓冲区，布局由业务结构判定。
    // The frame is decoded exactly once here; the worker returns raw bytes.
    let (layout, csr) = protocol::decode_frame(&frame.bytes, protocol::unwrap_csr)?;
    logging::log(
        Level::Debug,
        format!("TA 帧布局 [frame layout]: {}", layout.label()),
    );
    let info = protocol::validate_csr(&csr, &challenge, &cose_key)?;
    logging::log(
        Level::Debug,
        format!(
            "CSR 校验通过 [CSR verified]: DICE={} version={}",
            info.dice_entries, info.version
        ),
    );

    let chain = server::sign_certificates(&config.rkp_server, &csr, &challenge, &tmp)?;
    let built = keybox::build_full(&key, &chain)?;
    Ok(Issued {
        keybox_xml: built.xml,
        chain_pem: built.chain_pem,
        device_id: built.device_id,
        certificates: built.certificates,
        fingerprint: diagnosis.fingerprint,
        duration_ms: started.elapsed().as_millis(),
        frame_layout: layout.label(),
    })
}

/// 多变异探测：定位「空成功」的成因。
///
/// 现场出现过 `send_cmd` 返回 0 但缓冲区零字节的情况：既不是帧格式问题，也不是
/// 长度字段问题。探测把缓冲区初始化、security_level、请求封装三个变量拆开，
/// 每个变体独立子进程执行，用一次装包换回全部对照数据。
/// Probes one variable at a time to locate the "empty success" path.
pub(crate) fn probe(config: &Config) -> Result<Value> {
    let diagnosis = diagnose();
    if let Some((id, detail)) = diagnosis.blockers.first() {
        return Err(blocker_error(id, detail));
    }
    let key = generate_key()?;
    let tmp = work_dir(config);
    let challenge = server::fetch_challenge(&config.rkp_server, &diagnosis.fingerprint, &tmp)?;
    let cose_key = protocol::cose_key(&key.public_key())?;
    let request = protocol::build_request(&challenge, &cose_key)?;
    logging::log(
        Level::Info,
        format!(
            "开始探测 TA 响应，共 {} 个变体 [probing TA response with {} variants]",
            ta::PROBES.len(),
            ta::PROBES.len()
        ),
    );
    let variants = worker::probe_all(&tmp, &request)?;
    // 符号表能说明固件是否提供初始化入口或其它重载；读不到不影响探测结果。
    let mut library_symbols = elf::function_symbols(VENDOR_LIBRARY, "KeymasterUtils")
        .unwrap_or_else(|error| {
            logging::log(
                Level::Warn,
                format!("读取库符号失败 [symbols unavailable]: {error}"),
            );
            Vec::new()
        });
    if let Ok(more) = elf::function_symbols(VENDOR_LIBRARY, "send_cmd") {
        library_symbols.extend(more);
    }
    library_symbols.sort();
    library_symbols.dedup();
    Ok(json!({
        "soc": diagnosis.soc_model,
        "fingerprint": diagnosis.fingerprint,
        "challenge_bytes": challenge.len(),
        "request_bytes": request.len(),
        "library_symbols": library_symbols,
        "variants": variants,
    }))
}

/// 签发并写入 `RKP/`：keybox.xml、cert-chain.pem、meta.json。
pub(crate) fn issue_and_save(config: &Config) -> Result<SavedArtifacts> {
    let issued = issue(config)?;
    save(config, &issued)
}

fn save(config: &Config, issued: &Issued) -> Result<SavedArtifacts> {
    create_private_dir(&config.rkp_dir)?;
    let keybox = config.rkp_dir.join("keybox.xml");
    let chain = config.rkp_dir.join("cert-chain.pem");
    let meta = config.rkp_dir.join("meta.json");

    let meta_value = json!({
        "version": 1,
        "created_at": now(),
        "fingerprint": issued.fingerprint,
        "device_id": issued.device_id,
        "certificates": issued.certificates,
        "protocol": "csr_v2",
        "frame_layout": issued.frame_layout,
        "duration_ms": issued.duration_ms,
        "ta_status": 0,
        "tool_version": crate::VERSION,
        "sha256": hex_sha256(issued.keybox_xml.as_bytes()),
    });
    let meta_text = format!(
        "{}\n",
        serde_json::to_string_pretty(&meta_value).unwrap_or_default()
    );

    // 三个文件都是私钥材料，统一 0600 + 原子替换。
    // All three files contain private material: 0600 and atomic replace.
    crate::atomic_file::write_private(&keybox, issued.keybox_xml.as_bytes())?;
    crate::atomic_file::write_private(&chain, issued.chain_pem.as_bytes())?;
    crate::atomic_file::write_private(&meta, meta_text.as_bytes())?;
    logging::log(
        Level::Info,
        format!(
            "RKP 产物已写入 [RKP artifacts written]: {}（{} 张证书）",
            keybox.display(),
            issued.certificates
        ),
    );
    Ok(SavedArtifacts {
        keybox,
        chain,
        meta,
    })
}

/// 只读探测：不联网、不调用 TA。
pub(crate) fn diagnose() -> Diagnosis {
    let arm64_android = device_path_supported();
    let library_present = Path::new(VENDOR_LIBRARY).is_file();
    let device_present = Path::new(SMCINVOKE).exists();
    let fingerprint = getprop("ro.build.fingerprint");
    let detection = soc::detect();
    let mut blockers = Vec::new();
    if !arm64_android {
        blockers.push((
            "arch",
            "设备端 RKP 仅支持 Android arm64 [requires Android arm64]".to_owned(),
        ));
    }
    if !library_present {
        blockers.push((
            "library",
            format!("缺少 {VENDOR_LIBRARY} [vendor library missing]"),
        ));
    }
    if !device_present {
        blockers.push((
            "smcinvoke",
            "缺少 /dev/smcinvoke [smcinvoke missing]".to_owned(),
        ));
    }
    if detection.state != soc::State::Qualcomm {
        blockers.push((
            "soc",
            format!(
                "需要高通 SoC [Qualcomm SoC required]: {}",
                detection.summary()
            ),
        ));
    }
    if fingerprint.is_empty() {
        blockers.push((
            "fingerprint",
            "ro.build.fingerprint 为空 [fingerprint is empty]".to_owned(),
        ));
    }
    Diagnosis {
        arm64_android,
        library_present,
        device_present,
        manufacturer: getprop("ro.soc.manufacturer"),
        soc_model: getprop("ro.soc.model"),
        fingerprint,
        detection,
        blockers,
    }
}

fn blocker_error(id: &'static str, detail: &str) -> TfError {
    TfError::staged(
        "prerequisite",
        id,
        format!("前置条件不满足 [prerequisite]: {detail}"),
    )
    .with_hint(format!("rkp.blocker.{id}"))
    .with_raw(json!({ "blocker": id }))
}

/// `--rkp-status` 的结构化输出。
pub(crate) fn status_value(config: &Config) -> Value {
    let diagnosis = diagnose();
    let blockers = diagnosis
        .blockers
        .iter()
        .map(|(id, detail)| json!({ "id": id, "detail": detail }))
        .collect::<Vec<_>>();
    let artifact_meta = read_meta(config);
    json!({
        "supported": blockers.is_empty(),
        "platform": {
            "os": std::env::consts::OS,
            "arch": std::env::consts::ARCH,
            "arm64_android": diagnosis.arm64_android,
        },
        "root": {
            "method": config.root_method,
            "version": config.root_version,
        },
        "soc": {
            "state": diagnosis.detection.state.label(),
            "manufacturer": diagnosis.manufacturer,
            "model": diagnosis.soc_model,
            "evidence": diagnosis.detection.evidence,
        },
        "library": { "path": VENDOR_LIBRARY, "present": diagnosis.library_present },
        "device": { "path": SMCINVOKE, "present": diagnosis.device_present },
        "fingerprint": diagnosis.fingerprint,
        "server": config.rkp_server,
        "blockers": blockers,
        "artifacts": {
            "exists": config.rkp_dir.join("keybox.xml").is_file(),
            "dir": config.rkp_dir.display().to_string(),
            "meta": artifact_meta,
        },
    })
}

/// 产物列表：目录不存在时返回 `exists=false`，不报错。
pub(crate) fn artifacts_value(config: &Config) -> Value {
    let entry = |name: &str| {
        let path = config.rkp_dir.join(name);
        match fs::metadata(&path) {
            Ok(metadata) => json!({
                "path": path.display().to_string(),
                "size": metadata.len(),
                "mtime": metadata
                    .modified()
                    .ok()
                    .and_then(|time| time.duration_since(UNIX_EPOCH).ok())
                    .map(|value| value.as_secs()),
            }),
            Err(_) => Value::Null,
        }
    };
    json!({
        "exists": config.rkp_dir.is_dir(),
        "dir": config.rkp_dir.display().to_string(),
        "keybox": entry("keybox.xml"),
        "chain": entry("cert-chain.pem"),
        "meta_file": entry("meta.json"),
        "meta": read_meta(config),
    })
}

/// 删除整个 `RKP/` 目录。目录原本不存在按成功处理。
pub(crate) fn delete_artifacts(config: &Config) -> Result<Value> {
    let freed = directory_size(&config.rkp_dir);
    match fs::remove_dir_all(&config.rkp_dir) {
        Ok(()) => {}
        Err(error) if error.kind() == std::io::ErrorKind::NotFound => {}
        Err(error) => {
            return Err(TfError::staged(
                "artifacts",
                "delete_failed",
                format!("无法删除 RKP 目录 [cannot delete RKP dir]: {error}"),
            )
            .with_raw(json!({ "dir": config.rkp_dir.display().to_string() })));
        }
    }
    logging::log(
        Level::Info,
        format!("RKP 产物已删除 [RKP artifacts removed]: {freed} 字节 [bytes]"),
    );
    Ok(json!({
        "deleted": true,
        "dir": config.rkp_dir.display().to_string(),
        "freed_bytes": freed,
    }))
}

/// `--rkp-status` 的文本输出（非 JSON 模式）。
pub(crate) fn status_lines(config: &Config) -> Vec<String> {
    let diagnosis = diagnose();
    let mut lines = Vec::new();
    lines.push(format!("服务 Server: {}", config.rkp_server));
    lines.push(format!(
        "平台 Platform: {}/{}",
        std::env::consts::OS,
        std::env::consts::ARCH
    ));
    lines.push(format!(
        "SoC: {} {}",
        diagnosis.detection.state.label(),
        if diagnosis.detection.evidence.is_empty() {
            "(无证据 no evidence)".to_owned()
        } else {
            format!("[{}]", diagnosis.detection.evidence.join(", "))
        }
    ));
    lines.push(format!(
        "厂商库 Vendor library: {} ({})",
        VENDOR_LIBRARY,
        if diagnosis.library_present {
            "存在 present"
        } else {
            "缺失 missing"
        }
    ));
    lines.push(format!(
        "SMCInvoke: {} ({})",
        SMCINVOKE,
        if diagnosis.device_present {
            "存在 present"
        } else {
            "缺失 missing"
        }
    ));
    lines.push(format!(
        "Fingerprint: {}",
        if diagnosis.fingerprint.is_empty() {
            "(缺失 missing)"
        } else {
            &diagnosis.fingerprint
        }
    ));
    match diagnosis.blockers.first() {
        Some((_, detail)) => lines.push(format!("结论 Result: 不可用 [unavailable]: {detail}")),
        None => lines.push("结论 Result: 可用 [available]".to_owned()),
    }
    let artifacts = config.rkp_dir.join("keybox.xml");
    lines.push(format!(
        "产物 Artifacts: {} ({})",
        config.rkp_dir.display(),
        if artifacts.is_file() {
            "已生成 present"
        } else {
            "未生成 absent"
        }
    ));
    lines
}

pub(crate) fn available() -> bool {
    diagnose().blockers.is_empty()
}

/// 厂商调用路径只在 arm64 Android 编译；其余 ABI 无法进行设备端签发。
/// The vendor path only exists on arm64 Android.
pub(crate) fn device_path_supported() -> bool {
    cfg!(all(target_os = "android", target_arch = "aarch64"))
}

pub(crate) fn execute_worker(directory: &Path) -> Result<()> {
    worker::execute(directory)
}

/// 探测子进程入口，供 CLI 的隐藏参数转发。
pub(crate) fn execute_probe(directory: &Path, variant: usize) -> Result<()> {
    worker::execute_probe(directory, variant)
}

fn read_meta(config: &Config) -> Value {
    fs::read_to_string(config.rkp_dir.join("meta.json"))
        .ok()
        .and_then(|text| serde_json::from_str::<Value>(&text).ok())
        .unwrap_or(Value::Null)
}

fn directory_size(directory: &Path) -> u64 {
    let Ok(entries) = fs::read_dir(directory) else {
        return 0;
    };
    entries
        .flatten()
        .filter_map(|entry| entry.metadata().ok())
        .map(|metadata| metadata.len())
        .sum()
}

fn generate_key() -> Result<SecretKey> {
    let mut file = fs::File::open("/dev/urandom")
        .map_err(|error| TfError::new(format!("无法读取 /dev/urandom [urandom]: {error}")))?;
    for _ in 0..8 {
        let mut bytes = [0u8; 32];
        file.read_exact(&mut bytes)?;
        if let Ok(key) = SecretKey::from_slice(&bytes) {
            return Ok(key);
        }
    }
    Err(TfError::new(
        "无法生成有效 P-256 私钥 [key generation failed]",
    ))
}

fn work_dir(config: &Config) -> PathBuf {
    config.keybox_dir.join("tmp")
}

fn getprop(name: &str) -> String {
    process::output("getprop", [name])
        .ok()
        .and_then(|output| process::stdout_text(output, name).ok())
        .map(|value| value.trim().to_owned())
        .unwrap_or_default()
}

fn hex_sha256(bytes: &[u8]) -> String {
    Sha256::digest(bytes)
        .iter()
        .map(|byte| format!("{byte:02x}"))
        .collect()
}

fn now() -> u64 {
    SystemTime::now()
        .duration_since(UNIX_EPOCH)
        .map(|value| value.as_secs())
        .unwrap_or(0)
}

/// 创建 0700 目录并返回唯一的运行子目录。
pub(super) fn unique_dir(root: &Path) -> Result<PathBuf> {
    create_private_dir(root)?;
    let nanos = SystemTime::now()
        .duration_since(UNIX_EPOCH)
        .map(|value| value.as_nanos())
        .unwrap_or(0);
    for attempt in 0..16 {
        let candidate = root.join(format!("rkp-{}-{nanos}-{attempt}", std::process::id()));
        match fs::create_dir(&candidate) {
            Ok(()) => {
                restrict_directory(&candidate);
                return Ok(candidate);
            }
            Err(error) if error.kind() == std::io::ErrorKind::AlreadyExists => continue,
            Err(error) => {
                return Err(TfError::new(format!(
                    "无法创建 RKP 临时目录 [cannot create RKP temp dir]: {error}"
                )));
            }
        }
    }
    Err(TfError::new(
        "无法创建唯一 RKP 临时目录 [cannot allocate a unique RKP temp dir]",
    ))
}

pub(super) fn create_private_dir(path: &Path) -> Result<()> {
    let mut builder = fs::DirBuilder::new();
    builder.recursive(true);
    #[cfg(unix)]
    {
        use std::os::unix::fs::DirBuilderExt;
        builder.mode(0o700);
    }
    builder.create(path)?;
    restrict_directory(path);
    Ok(())
}

fn restrict_directory(_path: &Path) {
    #[cfg(unix)]
    {
        use std::os::unix::fs::PermissionsExt;
        let _ = fs::set_permissions(_path, fs::Permissions::from_mode(0o700));
    }
}

pub(super) fn write_private(path: &Path, bytes: &[u8]) -> Result<()> {
    use std::io::Write;
    let mut options = fs::OpenOptions::new();
    options.write(true).create(true).truncate(true);
    #[cfg(unix)]
    {
        use std::os::unix::fs::OpenOptionsExt;
        options.mode(0o600);
    }
    let mut file = options.open(path)?;
    file.write_all(bytes)?;
    file.sync_all()?;
    Ok(())
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    #[test]
    fn unique_directories_do_not_collide() {
        let root = std::env::temp_dir().join(format!("teeforge-rkp-unique-{}", std::process::id()));
        let first = unique_dir(&root).unwrap();
        let second = unique_dir(&root).unwrap();
        assert_ne!(first, second);
        assert!(first.is_dir() && second.is_dir());
        let _ = fs::remove_dir_all(root);
    }

    #[test]
    fn artifacts_are_written_owner_only_and_removed() {
        let root = std::env::temp_dir().join(format!("teeforge-rkp-store-{}", std::process::id()));
        let config = Config {
            rkp_dir: root.clone(),
            ..Config::default()
        };
        let issued = Issued {
            keybox_xml: "<AndroidAttestation/>".into(),
            chain_pem: "-----BEGIN CERTIFICATE-----\n".into(),
            device_id: "1A2B".into(),
            certificates: 2,
            fingerprint: "brand/device".into(),
            duration_ms: 12,
            frame_layout: "status+len",
        };
        let saved = save(&config, &issued).unwrap();
        assert!(saved.keybox.is_file() && saved.chain.is_file() && saved.meta.is_file());

        let value = artifacts_value(&config);
        assert_eq!(value["exists"], json!(true));
        assert_eq!(value["meta"]["certificates"], json!(2));
        assert_eq!(value["meta"]["device_id"], json!("1A2B"));

        let removed = delete_artifacts(&config).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(removed["deleted"], json!(true));
        assert!(!root.exists());
        // 目录不存在时删除仍然成功（幂等）。
        assert!(delete_artifacts(&config).is_ok());
    }

    #[test]
    fn sha256_helper_is_lowercase_hex() {
        let digest = hex_sha256(b"abc");
        assert_eq!(digest.len(), 64);
        assert!(
            digest
                .chars()
                .all(|character| character.is_ascii_hexdigit())
        );
        assert!(
            digest
                .chars()
                .all(|character| !character.is_ascii_uppercase())
        );
    }
}

```

`crates/teeforge/src/rkp/protocol.rs`:

```rs
//! CSR_V2 请求帧与响应校验 [CSR_V2 framing and response checks]
//!
//! 移植自 Duck-ToolBox 的 `duck-rkp-device::protocol`（MIT），仅保留 V2 路径。
//! Ported from Duck-ToolBox `duck-rkp-device::protocol` (MIT); V2 only.
//!
//! 本地只校验结构与绑定关系（challenge、keysToSign）。DICE 链与 COSE 签名的
//! 密码学校验由 Google RKP 服务端完成，非法 CSR 不会签发证书。
//! Locally only structure and binding are checked. DICE and COSE signature
//! verification is performed by the RKP server; an invalid CSR is never signed.

use crate::error::{Result, TfError};
use ciborium::value::{Integer, Value};
use p256::PublicKey;
use std::io::Cursor;

/// TA 写入固定共享缓冲区的命令号 [Fixed shared-buffer command id].
pub(crate) const CSR_COMMAND: u32 = 0x3122;
/// TA 响应缓冲区大小 [TA response buffer size].
pub(crate) const BUFFER_SIZE: usize = 0xa000;
/// 超过该大小的请求在进入厂商代码前拒绝 [Requests above this never reach vendor code].
pub(crate) const MAX_REQUEST_SIZE: usize = 0x9000;

const MAX_CBOR_BYTES: usize = 256 * 1024;
const MAX_DEPTH: usize = 16;
const MAX_CONTAINER_ITEMS: u64 = 64;
const MAX_CHALLENGE_LEN: usize = 64;
/// AuthenticatedRequest 版本与 CSR payload 版本 [Versions from the RKP CDDL].
const AUTHENTICATED_REQUEST_VERSION: i128 = 1;
const CSR_PAYLOAD_VERSION: i128 = 3;

#[derive(Debug)]
pub(crate) struct CsrInfo {
    pub(crate) dice_entries: usize,
    pub(crate) version: i128,
}

fn fail(message: impl Into<String>) -> TfError {
    TfError::new(format!(
        "RKP CSR 无效 [invalid RKP CSR]: {}",
        message.into()
    ))
}

fn cb(value: i128) -> Value {
    if value >= 0 {
        Value::Integer(Integer::from(value as u64))
    } else {
        Value::Integer(Integer::from(value as i64))
    }
}

pub(crate) fn encode(value: &Value) -> Result<Vec<u8>> {
    let value = canonicalize(value)?;
    let mut bytes = Vec::new();
    ciborium::ser::into_writer(&value, &mut bytes)
        .map_err(|error| fail(format!("CBOR 编码失败 [encode]: {error}")))?;
    Ok(bytes)
}

fn encode_unordered(value: &Value) -> Result<Vec<u8>> {
    let mut bytes = Vec::new();
    ciborium::ser::into_writer(value, &mut bytes)
        .map_err(|error| fail(format!("CBOR 编码失败 [encode]: {error}")))?;
    Ok(bytes)
}

/// 规范化映射顺序，避免服务端因键序差异拒绝请求。
/// Canonical map ordering keeps the request byte-stable for the server.
fn canonicalize(value: &Value) -> Result<Value> {
    match value {
        Value::Array(items) => Ok(Value::Array(
            items.iter().map(canonicalize).collect::<Result<Vec<_>>>()?,
        )),
        Value::Map(entries) => {
            let mut sorted = Vec::with_capacity(entries.len());
            for (key, value) in entries {
                let key = canonicalize(key)?;
                let value = canonicalize(value)?;
                sorted.push((encode_unordered(&key)?, key, value));
            }
            sorted.sort_by(|left, right| {
                left.0
                    .len()
                    .cmp(&right.0.len())
                    .then_with(|| left.0.cmp(&right.0))
            });
            Ok(Value::Map(
                sorted
                    .into_iter()
                    .map(|(_, key, value)| (key, value))
                    .collect(),
            ))
        }
        Value::Tag(tag, inner) => Ok(Value::Tag(*tag, Box::new(canonicalize(inner)?))),
        other => Ok(other.clone()),
    }
}

pub(crate) fn decode(bytes: &[u8]) -> Result<Value> {
    preflight(bytes)?;
    let mut reader = Cursor::new(bytes);
    let value: Value = ciborium::de::from_reader(&mut reader)
        .map_err(|error| fail(format!("CBOR 解码失败 [decode]: {error}")))?;
    if reader.position() != bytes.len() as u64 {
        return Err(fail("CBOR 存在尾随数据 [trailing data]"));
    }
    Ok(value)
}

/// 在通用解码器分配容器前检查长度与嵌套，避免深度炸弹。
/// Bound lengths and nesting before the general decoder allocates containers.
fn preflight(bytes: &[u8]) -> Result<()> {
    if bytes.is_empty() || bytes.len() > MAX_CBOR_BYTES {
        return Err(fail("CBOR 长度超出 1..262144 字节 [length out of range]"));
    }
    let mut scanner = Scanner { bytes, position: 0 };
    scanner.item(0)?;
    if scanner.position != bytes.len() {
        return Err(fail("CBOR 存在尾随数据 [trailing data]"));
    }
    Ok(())
}

struct Scanner<'a> {
    bytes: &'a [u8],
    position: usize,
}

impl Scanner<'_> {
    fn byte(&mut self) -> Result<u8> {
        let byte = *self
            .bytes
            .get(self.position)
            .ok_or_else(|| fail("CBOR 截断 [truncated]"))?;
        self.position += 1;
        Ok(byte)
    }

    fn argument(&mut self, additional: u8) -> Result<Option<u64>> {
        let count = match additional {
            0..=23 => return Ok(Some(u64::from(additional))),
            24 => 1,
            25 => 2,
            26 => 4,
            27 => 8,
            31 => return Ok(None),
            _ => return Err(fail("CBOR 保留的附加信息 [reserved additional info]")),
        };
        let mut value = 0;
        for _ in 0..count {
            value = (value << 8) | u64::from(self.byte()?);
        }
        Ok(Some(value))
    }

    fn item(&mut self, depth: usize) -> Result<()> {
        if depth > MAX_DEPTH {
            return Err(fail("CBOR 嵌套超过 16 层 [nesting exceeds 16]"));
        }
        let initial = self.byte()?;
        let major = initial >> 5;
        let argument = self.argument(initial & 31)?;
        match major {
            0 | 1 | 7 => {
                if argument.is_none() {
                    return Err(fail("意外的 CBOR break [unexpected break]"));
                }
            }
            2 | 3 => match argument {
                Some(length) => {
                    let length = usize::try_from(length)
                        .map_err(|_| fail("CBOR 长度溢出 [length overflow]"))?;
                    self.position = self
                        .position
                        .checked_add(length)
                        .ok_or_else(|| fail("CBOR 长度溢出 [length overflow]"))?;
                    if self.position > self.bytes.len() {
                        return Err(fail("CBOR 字符串截断 [truncated string]"));
                    }
                }
                None => {
                    while self.bytes.get(self.position) != Some(&0xff) {
                        let chunk = *self
                            .bytes
                            .get(self.position)
                            .ok_or_else(|| fail("CBOR 字符串截断 [truncated string]"))?;
                        if chunk >> 5 != major || chunk & 31 == 31 {
                            return Err(fail("非法的不定长 CBOR 字符串块 [invalid chunk]"));
                        }
                        self.item(depth + 1)?;
                    }
                    self.position += 1;
                }
            },
            4 | 5 => {
                if let Some(length) = argument {
                    if length > MAX_CONTAINER_ITEMS {
                        return Err(fail("CBOR 容器超过 64 项 [container exceeds 64]"));
                    }
                    for _ in 0..length {
                        self.item(depth + 1)?;
                        if major == 5 {
                            self.item(depth + 1)?;
                        }
                    }
                } else {
                    let mut count = 0;
                    while self.bytes.get(self.position) != Some(&0xff) {
                        count += 1;
                        if count > MAX_CONTAINER_ITEMS {
                            return Err(fail("CBOR 容器超过 64 项 [container exceeds 64]"));
                        }
                        self.item(depth + 1)?;
                        if major == 5 {
                            self.item(depth + 1)?;
                        }
                    }
                    self.position += 1;
                }
            }
            6 => {
                if argument.is_none() {
                    return Err(fail("非法 CBOR 标签 [invalid tag]"));
                }
                self.item(depth + 1)?;
            }
            _ => return Err(fail("非法 CBOR 主类型 [invalid major type]")),
        }
        Ok(())
    }
}

fn as_array<'a>(value: &'a Value, label: &str) -> Result<&'a [Value]> {
    match value {
        Value::Array(items) => Ok(items),
        _ => Err(fail(format!("{label} 不是数组 [not an array]"))),
    }
}

fn as_map<'a>(value: &'a Value, label: &str) -> Result<&'a [(Value, Value)]> {
    match value {
        Value::Map(entries) => Ok(entries),
        _ => Err(fail(format!("{label} 不是映射 [not a map]"))),
    }
}

fn as_bytes<'a>(value: &'a Value, label: &str) -> Result<&'a [u8]> {
    match value {
        Value::Bytes(bytes) => Ok(bytes),
        _ => Err(fail(format!("{label} 不是字节串 [not a byte string]"))),
    }
}

fn as_int(value: &Value, label: &str) -> Result<i128> {
    match value {
        Value::Integer(number) => Ok(i128::from(*number)),
        _ => Err(fail(format!("{label} 不是整数 [not an integer]"))),
    }
}

fn map_get(entries: &[(Value, Value)], key: i128) -> Option<&Value> {
    entries
        .iter()
        .find(|(candidate, _)| as_int(candidate, "key").ok() == Some(key))
        .map(|(_, value)| value)
}

fn array_get<'a>(items: &'a [Value], index: usize, label: &str) -> Result<&'a Value> {
    items
        .get(index)
        .ok_or_else(|| fail(format!("{label} 缺少第 {index} 项 [missing entry {index}]")))
}

/// 构造 COSE_Key（ES256 / P-256，未压缩点）。
pub(crate) fn cose_key(public_key: &PublicKey) -> Result<Value> {
    let point = public_key.to_sec1_bytes();
    if point.len() != 65 || point[0] != 4 {
        return Err(fail(
            "公钥不是未压缩 P-256 点 [not an uncompressed P-256 point]",
        ));
    }
    Ok(Value::Map(vec![
        (cb(1), cb(2)),
        (cb(3), cb(-7)),
        (cb(-1), cb(1)),
        (cb(-2), Value::Bytes(point[1..33].to_vec())),
        (cb(-3), Value::Bytes(point[33..65].to_vec())),
    ]))
}

/// 构造 `{4: challenge, 57: bstr([COSE_Key])}` 并加上小端命令头。
pub(crate) fn build_request(challenge: &[u8], key: &Value) -> Result<Vec<u8>> {
    let keys = Value::Bytes(encode(&Value::Array(vec![key.clone()]))?);
    let payload = encode(&Value::Map(vec![
        (cb(4), Value::Bytes(challenge.to_vec())),
        (cb(57), keys),
    ]))?;
    validate_request(&payload)?;
    let mut request = CSR_COMMAND.to_le_bytes().to_vec();
    request.extend_from_slice(&payload);
    Ok(request)
}

fn validate_request(payload: &[u8]) -> Result<()> {
    if payload.len() > MAX_REQUEST_SIZE - 4 {
        return Err(fail("请求超出 TA 缓冲区 [request exceeds TA buffer]"));
    }
    let value = decode(payload)?;
    let entries = as_map(&value, "CSR 请求 [CSR request]")?;
    if entries.len() != 2 || map_get(entries, 4).is_none() || map_get(entries, 57).is_none() {
        return Err(fail(
            "请求只能包含 challenge 与 keysToSign [unexpected fields]",
        ));
    }
    let challenge = as_bytes(
        map_get(entries, 4).ok_or_else(|| fail("缺少 challenge [missing challenge]"))?,
        "challenge",
    )?;
    let keys = decode(as_bytes(
        map_get(entries, 57).ok_or_else(|| fail("缺少 keysToSign [missing keysToSign]"))?,
        "keysToSign",
    )?)?;
    let keys = as_array(&keys, "keysToSign")?;
    if keys.len() != 1 {
        return Err(fail(
            "必须且只能包含一把 P-256 密钥 [exactly one key required]",
        ));
    }
    validate_inputs(challenge, &keys[0])
}

fn validate_inputs(challenge: &[u8], key: &Value) -> Result<()> {
    if challenge.is_empty() || challenge.len() > MAX_CHALLENGE_LEN {
        return Err(fail(
            "challenge 必须为 1..64 字节 [challenge must be 1..64 bytes]",
        ));
    }
    let entries = as_map(key, "P-256 密钥 [P-256 key]")?;
    for label in [1, 3, -1, -2, -3] {
        if map_get(entries, label).is_none() {
            return Err(fail("P-256 密钥字段不完整 [incomplete P-256 key]"));
        }
    }
    for (label, expected) in [(1, 2), (3, -7), (-1, 1)] {
        if as_int(map_get(entries, label).unwrap(), "COSE 字段")? != expected {
            return Err(fail("必须使用 ES256 / P-256 [ES256 / P-256 required]"));
        }
    }
    let mut point = vec![4];
    for label in [-2, -3] {
        let coordinate = as_bytes(map_get(entries, label).unwrap(), "P-256 坐标")?;
        if coordinate.len() != 32 {
            return Err(fail(
                "P-256 坐标必须为 32 字节 [coordinate must be 32 bytes]",
            ));
        }
        point.extend_from_slice(coordinate);
    }
    PublicKey::from_sec1_bytes(&point)
        .map_err(|_| fail("P-256 公钥点无效 [invalid P-256 point]"))?;
    Ok(())
}

/// 厂商 `send_cmd` 可能使用的响应帧布局。
///
/// 不同固件对帧头的约定不一致：有的带 status，有的长度含头，有的干脆直接回 CBOR。
/// 只靠长度字段无法可靠区分，因此由 [`decode_frame`] 结合业务解析结果判定。
/// Firmware disagrees on the frame header; the layout is confirmed by business parsing.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub(crate) enum Layout {
    /// `[i32 status][u32 payload_len][payload]`
    StatusLen,
    /// `[i32 status][u32 total_len][payload]`，长度包含 8 字节头
    StatusTotal,
    /// `[u32 payload_len][payload]`，status 由 `send_cmd` 返回值给出
    LenOnly,
    /// `[i32 status][payload]`，无长度字段
    StatusOnly,
    /// 纯 payload
    Bare,
}

impl Layout {
    pub(crate) fn label(self) -> &'static str {
        match self {
            Layout::StatusLen => "status+len",
            Layout::StatusTotal => "status+total",
            Layout::LenOnly => "len",
            Layout::StatusOnly => "status",
            Layout::Bare => "bare",
        }
    }

    /// 按布局切出候选区间；只做边界判断，不判断内容。
    fn slice(self, buf: &[u8]) -> Option<&[u8]> {
        match self {
            Layout::StatusLen => {
                let length = read_u32(buf, 4)? as usize;
                bounded(buf, 8, length)
            }
            Layout::StatusTotal => {
                let total = read_u32(buf, 4)? as usize;
                bounded(buf, 8, total.checked_sub(8)?)
            }
            Layout::LenOnly => {
                let length = read_u32(buf, 0)? as usize;
                bounded(buf, 4, length)
            }
            Layout::StatusOnly => {
                if buf.len() <= 4 {
                    return None;
                }
                Some(&buf[4..])
            }
            Layout::Bare => Some(buf),
        }
    }
}

/// 依次尝试候选布局，返回第一个能被 `accept` 完整解析的结果。
///
/// `accept` 必须是业务解析器（例如 [`unwrap_csr`]）：长度字段可以骗人，
/// 能解出合法业务结构才是真的。命中布局会一路带到日志与 `meta.json`。
/// Tries every candidate layout and keeps the first one `accept` can parse.
pub(crate) fn decode_frame<T>(
    buf: &[u8],
    accept: impl Fn(&[u8]) -> Result<T>,
) -> Result<(Layout, T)> {
    const LAYOUTS: [Layout; 5] = [
        Layout::StatusLen,
        Layout::StatusTotal,
        Layout::LenOnly,
        Layout::StatusOnly,
        Layout::Bare,
    ];

    // 先严格匹配（长度字段正好等于真实 item 长度），再放宽到"长度字段含 padding"。
    // 严格轮保证 `Layout` 标签可信；宽松轮保证异常固件也能解析。
    // Strict pass first so the reported layout is trustworthy, then a lenient pass.
    for strict in [true, false] {
        for layout in LAYOUTS {
            let Some(candidate) = layout.slice(buf) else {
                continue;
            };
            // 收紧到真实 item 边界：去掉长度字段里的 padding 与缓冲区尾部的零填充。
            // Trim to the real CBOR item so trailing zero padding never reaches the server.
            let Some(length) = cbor_item_len(candidate) else {
                continue;
            };
            if strict && length != candidate.len() {
                continue;
            }
            let payload = &candidate[..length];
            if let Ok(value) = accept(payload) {
                return Ok((layout, value));
            }
        }
    }

    // 所有布局都失败后才看头部数值：落在 KeyMint 错误码区间说明 TA 明确拒绝，
    // 比"无法识别帧"更能定位问题。放在最后避免干扰正常解析。
    // A negative header in the KeyMint range means the TA rejected the call.
    if let Some(code) = read_status(buf) {
        if (-1000..=-1).contains(&code) {
            return Err(crate::rkp::explain::status_error(code));
        }
    }
    Err(frame_error(buf))
}

/// 无法识别帧时的诊断错误：带上原始字节，便于区分"TA 拒绝"与"ABI 漂移"。
fn frame_error(buf: &[u8]) -> TfError {
    let sentinel = i32::MIN.to_le_bytes();
    if buf.starts_with(&sentinel) {
        return TfError::staged(
            "frame",
            "abi",
            "TA 未写入响应缓冲区 [TA never wrote a response]",
        )
        .with_hint("rkp.frame.abi")
        .with_raw(serde_json::json!({
            "buffer": BUFFER_SIZE,
            "buffer_len": buf.len(),
            "head_hex": head_hex(buf),
        }));
    }
    TfError::staged(
        "frame",
        "unparsed",
        "无法识别 TA 响应帧 [unrecognized TA response frame]",
    )
    .with_hint("rkp.frame.unparsed")
    .with_raw(serde_json::json!({
        "buffer": BUFFER_SIZE,
        "buffer_len": buf.len(),
        "written": written_len(buf),
        "status_le": read_status(buf),
        "head_hex": head_hex(buf),
    }))
}

fn read_u32(buf: &[u8], offset: usize) -> Option<u32> {
    Some(u32::from_le_bytes(
        buf.get(offset..offset + 4)?.try_into().ok()?,
    ))
}

fn read_status(buf: &[u8]) -> Option<i32> {
    Some(i32::from_le_bytes(buf.get(..4)?.try_into().ok()?))
}

fn bounded(buf: &[u8], start: usize, length: usize) -> Option<&[u8]> {
    if length == 0 || length > BUFFER_SIZE.saturating_sub(8) {
        return None;
    }
    buf.get(start..start.checked_add(length)?)
}

/// 前 64 字节的 hex；用于把真实字节带回诊断界面。
pub(crate) fn head_hex(buf: &[u8]) -> String {
    buf.iter()
        .take(64)
        .map(|byte| format!("{byte:02x}"))
        .collect()
}

/// 最后一个非零字节之后的长度；只作为"厂商写了多少"的粗略证据。
fn written_len(buf: &[u8]) -> usize {
    buf.iter()
        .rposition(|byte| *byte != 0)
        .map(|index| index + 1)
        .unwrap_or(0)
}

/// 缓冲区开头一个完整 CBOR item 的字节长度，不做语义解析。
///
/// 只需要覆盖厂商可能返回的结构（数组、映射、tag、字节串、整数），
/// indefinite length 不支持——本项目的请求与响应都不会产生它。
fn cbor_item_len(buf: &[u8]) -> Option<usize> {
    const MAX_ITEMS: u64 = 4096;

    fn header(buf: &[u8], pos: &mut usize) -> Option<(u8, u64)> {
        let first = *buf.get(*pos)?;
        *pos += 1;
        let major = first >> 5;
        let info = first & 0x1f;
        let value = match info {
            0..=23 => u64::from(info),
            24 => {
                let value = u64::from(*buf.get(*pos)?);
                *pos += 1;
                value
            }
            25 => {
                let value = u64::from(u16::from_be_bytes(
                    buf.get(*pos..*pos + 2)?.try_into().ok()?,
                ));
                *pos += 2;
                value
            }
            26 => {
                let value = u64::from(u32::from_be_bytes(
                    buf.get(*pos..*pos + 4)?.try_into().ok()?,
                ));
                *pos += 4;
                value
            }
            27 => {
                let value = u64::from_be_bytes(buf.get(*pos..*pos + 8)?.try_into().ok()?);
                *pos += 8;
                value
            }
            _ => return None,
        };
        Some((major, value))
    }

    fn item(buf: &[u8], pos: &mut usize, depth: usize) -> Option<()> {
        if depth > MAX_DEPTH {
            return None;
        }
        let (major, value) = header(buf, pos)?;
        match major {
            0 | 1 => Some(()),
            2 | 3 => {
                let length = usize::try_from(value).ok()?;
                *pos = pos.checked_add(length)?;
                (*pos <= buf.len()).then_some(())
            }
            4 | 5 => {
                if value > MAX_ITEMS {
                    return None;
                }
                let items = if major == 5 { value * 2 } else { value };
                for _ in 0..items {
                    item(buf, pos, depth + 1)?;
                }
                Some(())
            }
            6 => item(buf, pos, depth + 1),
            _ => Some(()),
        }
    }

    let mut pos = 0;
    item(buf, &mut pos, 0)?;
    (pos > 0).then_some(pos)
}

/// 固件可能返回裸 AuthenticatedRequest，也可能包在字段 61 中。
pub(crate) fn unwrap_csr(payload: &[u8]) -> Result<Vec<u8>> {
    let value = decode(payload)?;
    if matches!(value, Value::Array(_)) {
        return Ok(payload.to_vec());
    }
    let entries = as_map(&value, "KeyMint CSR 响应 [KeyMint CSR response]")?;
    if entries.len() != 1 {
        return Err(fail(
            "CSR 响应必须只含一个字段 [exactly one field required]",
        ));
    }
    let csr = as_bytes(
        map_get(entries, 61).ok_or_else(|| fail("CSR 响应缺少字段 61 [missing field 61]"))?,
        "AuthenticatedRequest",
    )?;
    if csr.is_empty() {
        return Err(fail("TA 返回空 CSR [empty CSR]"));
    }
    Ok(csr.to_vec())
}

/// 校验 CSR 结构，并绑定本次 challenge 与本次生成的公钥。
pub(crate) fn validate_csr(csr: &[u8], challenge: &[u8], key: &Value) -> Result<CsrInfo> {
    let value = decode(csr)?;
    let request = as_array(&value, "AuthenticatedRequest")?;
    if request.len() != 4 {
        return Err(fail(
            "AuthenticatedRequest 必须包含 4 项 [expected 4 entries]",
        ));
    }
    if as_int(&request[0], "版本")? != AUTHENTICATED_REQUEST_VERSION {
        return Err(fail(
            "AuthenticatedRequest 版本不受支持 [unsupported version]",
        ));
    }
    let dice = as_array(&request[2], "DICE 链 [DICE chain]")?;
    if dice.len() < 2 {
        return Err(fail("DICE 链过短 [DICE chain too short]"));
    }
    let signed = as_array(&request[3], "SignedData")?;
    if signed.len() != 4 {
        return Err(fail("SignedData 必须包含 4 项 [expected 4 entries]"));
    }
    if as_bytes(&signed[0], "protected")?.is_empty() {
        return Err(fail("SignedData protected 为空 [empty protected header]"));
    }
    if as_bytes(&signed[3], "signature")?.is_empty() {
        return Err(fail("SignedData 缺少签名 [missing signature]"));
    }

    let payload = decode(as_bytes(&signed[2], "SignedData payload")?)?;
    let payload = as_array(&payload, "signed payload")?;
    if as_bytes(array_get(payload, 0, "challenge")?, "challenge")? != challenge {
        return Err(fail("CSR challenge 与本次请求不一致 [challenge mismatch]"));
    }
    let contents = decode(as_bytes(
        array_get(payload, 1, "CSR payload")?,
        "CSR payload",
    )?)?;
    let contents = as_array(&contents, "CSR payload")?;
    if as_int(array_get(contents, 0, "CSR 版本")?, "CSR 版本")? != CSR_PAYLOAD_VERSION {
        return Err(fail(
            "CSR payload 版本不受支持 [unsupported payload version]",
        ));
    }
    let keys = as_array(array_get(contents, 3, "keysToSign")?, "keysToSign")?;
    if keys.len() != 1 {
        return Err(fail("keysToSign 必须只有一项 [exactly one key]"));
    }
    if encode(&keys[0])? != encode(key)? {
        return Err(fail("CSR 公钥与本次生成密钥不一致 [public key mismatch]"));
    }

    Ok(CsrInfo {
        dice_entries: dice.len(),
        version: AUTHENTICATED_REQUEST_VERSION,
    })
}

/// worker 只接受 CSR_V2 请求。
pub(crate) fn ta_payload(request: &[u8]) -> Result<&[u8]> {
    let (command, payload) = request
        .split_at_checked(4)
        .ok_or_else(|| fail("请求缺少命令头 [missing command header]"))?;
    if command != CSR_COMMAND.to_le_bytes() {
        return Err(fail("worker 只接受 CSR_V2 请求 [only CSR_V2 accepted]"));
    }
    validate_request(payload)?;
    Ok(payload)
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;
    use p256::SecretKey;

    fn secret(seed: u8) -> SecretKey {
        SecretKey::from_slice(&[seed; 32]).expect("valid scalar")
    }

    #[test]
    fn request_has_command_header_and_two_fields() {
        let key = cose_key(&secret(7).public_key()).expect("cose key");
        let request = build_request(b"challenge", &key).expect("request");
        assert_eq!(&request[..4], &CSR_COMMAND.to_le_bytes());
        let value = decode(&request[4..]).expect("payload");
        assert_eq!(as_map(&value, "request").unwrap().len(), 2);
    }

    #[test]
    fn request_rejects_empty_and_oversized_challenges() {
        let key = cose_key(&secret(9).public_key()).expect("cose key");
        assert!(build_request(b"", &key).is_err());
        assert!(build_request(&[0u8; 65], &key).is_err());
    }

    #[test]
    fn ta_payload_rejects_foreign_command() {
        let mut request = build_request(b"c", &cose_key(&secret(3).public_key()).unwrap()).unwrap();
        request[0] = 0x1e;
        assert!(ta_payload(&request).is_err());
    }

    /// 固定 CBOR 样本：数组 `[1, 2, 3]`。
    fn sample_cbor() -> Vec<u8> {
        encode(&Value::Array(vec![cb(1), cb(2), cb(3)])).expect("cbor")
    }

    fn accept_sample(payload: &[u8]) -> Result<Vec<u8>> {
        if payload == sample_cbor() {
            Ok(payload.to_vec())
        } else {
            Err(fail("not the sample"))
        }
    }

    fn buffer_with(payload: &[u8], header: &[u8]) -> Vec<u8> {
        let mut buffer = vec![0u8; BUFFER_SIZE];
        buffer[..header.len()].copy_from_slice(header);
        let start = header.len();
        buffer[start..start + payload.len()].copy_from_slice(payload);
        buffer
    }

    #[test]
    fn status_len_layout_is_detected() {
        let payload = sample_cbor();
        let header = [0u8, 0, 0, 0, payload.len() as u8, 0, 0, 0];
        let buffer = buffer_with(&payload, &header);
        let (layout, value) = decode_frame(&buffer, accept_sample).expect("decode");
        assert_eq!(layout, Layout::StatusLen);
        assert_eq!(value, payload);
    }

    #[test]
    fn status_total_layout_is_detected() {
        let payload = sample_cbor();
        let total = (payload.len() + 8) as u8;
        let header = [0u8, 0, 0, 0, total, 0, 0, 0];
        let buffer = buffer_with(&payload, &header);
        let (layout, _) = decode_frame(&buffer, accept_sample).expect("decode");
        assert_eq!(layout, Layout::StatusTotal);
    }

    #[test]
    fn len_only_layout_is_detected() {
        let payload = sample_cbor();
        let header = [payload.len() as u8, 0, 0, 0];
        let buffer = buffer_with(&payload, &header);
        let (layout, _) = decode_frame(&buffer, accept_sample).expect("decode");
        assert_eq!(layout, Layout::LenOnly);
    }

    #[test]
    fn status_only_layout_is_detected() {
        let payload = sample_cbor();
        let header = [0u8, 0, 0, 0];
        let buffer = buffer_with(&payload, &header);
        let (layout, _) = decode_frame(&buffer, accept_sample).expect("decode");
        assert_eq!(layout, Layout::StatusOnly);
    }

    #[test]
    fn bare_layout_ignores_zero_padding() {
        let payload = sample_cbor();
        let mut buffer = vec![0u8; BUFFER_SIZE];
        buffer[..payload.len()].copy_from_slice(&payload);
        let (layout, value) = decode_frame(&buffer, accept_sample).expect("decode");
        assert_eq!(layout, Layout::Bare);
        assert_eq!(value, payload);
    }

    #[test]
    fn untouched_buffer_reports_abi() {
        let mut buffer = vec![0u8; BUFFER_SIZE];
        buffer[..4].copy_from_slice(&i32::MIN.to_le_bytes());
        let error = decode_frame(&buffer, accept_sample).expect_err("must fail");
        assert_eq!(error.stage(), Some("frame"));
        assert_eq!(error.code(), Some("abi"));
        assert!(error.raw().is_some());
    }

    #[test]
    fn negative_header_maps_to_keymint_status() {
        let mut buffer = vec![0u8; BUFFER_SIZE];
        buffer[..4].copy_from_slice(&(-18i32).to_le_bytes());
        let error = decode_frame(&buffer, accept_sample).expect_err("must fail");
        assert_eq!(error.stage(), Some("ta"));
        assert_eq!(error.code(), Some("-18"));
    }

    #[test]
    fn unrecognized_buffer_keeps_head_hex() {
        let mut buffer = vec![0x00u8; BUFFER_SIZE];
        buffer[..4].copy_from_slice(&[0xfe, 0xfe, 0xfe, 0xfe]);
        let error = decode_frame(&buffer, accept_sample).expect_err("must fail");
        assert_eq!(error.code(), Some("unparsed"));
        let raw = error.raw().expect("raw");
        assert!(raw.get("head_hex").is_some());
        assert!(raw.get("written").is_some());
    }

    /// 构造一个结构合法、绑定正确的 CSR，用于验证本地检查逻辑。
    fn build_test_csr(challenge: &[u8], _key: &Value, planted: &Value) -> Vec<u8> {
        let contents = encode(&Value::Array(vec![
            cb(3),
            Value::Text("keymint".into()),
            Value::Map(vec![]),
            Value::Array(vec![planted.clone()]),
        ]))
        .unwrap();
        let payload = encode(&Value::Array(vec![
            Value::Bytes(challenge.to_vec()),
            Value::Bytes(contents),
        ]))
        .unwrap();
        let signed = Value::Array(vec![
            Value::Bytes(vec![1, 2, 3]),
            Value::Map(vec![]),
            Value::Bytes(payload),
            Value::Bytes(vec![9, 9]),
        ]);
        encode(&Value::Array(vec![
            cb(1),
            Value::Map(vec![]),
            Value::Array(vec![Value::Map(vec![]), Value::Map(vec![])]),
            signed,
        ]))
        .unwrap()
    }

    #[test]
    fn valid_csr_binds_challenge_and_key() {
        let key = cose_key(&secret(5).public_key()).unwrap();
        let csr = build_test_csr(b"nonce", &key, &key);
        let info = validate_csr(&csr, b"nonce", &key).expect("valid csr");
        assert_eq!(info.dice_entries, 2);
    }

    #[test]
    fn csr_with_other_challenge_is_rejected() {
        let key = cose_key(&secret(5).public_key()).unwrap();
        let csr = build_test_csr(b"nonce", &key, &key);
        assert!(validate_csr(&csr, b"other", &key).is_err());
    }

    #[test]
    fn csr_with_other_public_key_is_rejected() {
        let key = cose_key(&secret(5).public_key()).unwrap();
        let planted = cose_key(&secret(6).public_key()).unwrap();
        let csr = build_test_csr(b"nonce", &key, &planted);
        assert!(validate_csr(&csr, b"nonce", &key).is_err());
    }

    #[test]
    fn wrapped_csr_field_61_is_unwrapped() {
        let inner = encode(&Value::Array(vec![cb(1)])).unwrap();
        let wrapper = encode(&Value::Map(vec![(cb(61), Value::Bytes(inner.clone()))])).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(unwrap_csr(&wrapper).unwrap(), inner);
    }

    #[test]
    fn deep_nesting_is_rejected_before_decoding() {
        let mut bytes = vec![0x81; 20];
        bytes.push(0x00);
        assert!(decode(&bytes).is_err());
    }
}

```

`crates/teeforge/src/rkp/server.rs`:

```rs
//! Google RKP 服务客户端 [Google RKP server client]
//!
//! 移植自 Duck-ToolBox 的 `duck-rkp::http`（MIT），但改用设备已有的
//! `curl` / `wget` 子进程，保持本项目不引入异步运行时。
//! Ported from `duck-rkp::http` (MIT), but re-implemented on top of the
//! device's existing `curl` / `wget` so this project keeps its synchronous model.

use crate::error::{Result, TfError};
use crate::logging::{self, Level};
use crate::process;
use crate::rkp::protocol::{decode, encode};
use crate::rkp::{unique_dir, write_private};
use base64::Engine;
use base64::engine::general_purpose::URL_SAFE_NO_PAD;
use ciborium::value::Value;
use std::fs;
use std::path::Path;
use std::process::Command;
use std::time::Duration;

const MAX_RESPONSE: usize = 1024 * 1024;
const MAX_CHALLENGE_LEN: usize = 64;
const HTTP_TIMEOUT_SECS: u64 = 30;

fn fail(message: impl Into<String>) -> TfError {
    TfError::new(format!("RKP 服务 [RKP server]: {}", message.into()))
}

/// 取 challenge（`fetchEekChain` 的响应里带 anti-replay challenge）。
pub(crate) fn fetch_challenge(server: &str, fingerprint: &str, tmp_root: &Path) -> Result<Vec<u8>> {
    let url = method_url(server, "fetchEekChain")?;
    let body = encode(&Value::Map(vec![
        (
            Value::Text("fingerprint".into()),
            Value::Text(fingerprint.to_owned()),
        ),
        (Value::Text("id".into()), Value::Integer(42u64.into())),
    ]))?;
    let response = post(&url, &body, tmp_root)?;
    parse_challenge(&response)
}

/// 提交 CSR，返回拼接后的 leaf→root DER 链。
pub(crate) fn sign_certificates(
    server: &str,
    csr: &[u8],
    challenge: &[u8],
    tmp_root: &Path,
) -> Result<Vec<u8>> {
    let endpoint = method_url(server, "signCertificates")?;
    let url = format!("{endpoint}?challenge={}", URL_SAFE_NO_PAD.encode(challenge));
    let response = post(&url, csr, tmp_root)?;
    parse_certificates(&response)
}

fn method_url(server: &str, method: &str) -> Result<String> {
    let base = server.trim().trim_end_matches('/');
    if base.is_empty() || base.contains(['?', '#']) || base.contains(char::is_whitespace) {
        return Err(fail("服务地址无效 [invalid endpoint]"));
    }
    let loopback = base.starts_with("http://127.0.0.1") || base.starts_with("http://localhost");
    let https = base.starts_with("https://");
    if !https && !loopback {
        return Err(fail("服务地址必须是 https [endpoint must use https]"));
    }
    Ok(format!("{base}:{method}"))
}

fn post(url: &str, body: &[u8], tmp_root: &Path) -> Result<Vec<u8>> {
    let directory = unique_dir(tmp_root)?;
    let result = (|| {
        let request = directory.join("request.bin");
        let response = directory.join("response.bin");
        write_private(&request, body)?;
        post_with_tools(url, &request, &response)
    })();
    let _ = fs::remove_dir_all(&directory);
    result
}

fn post_with_tools(url: &str, request: &Path, response: &Path) -> Result<Vec<u8>> {
    let mut errors = Vec::new();
    for (label, command) in [
        ("curl", curl_command(url, request, response)),
        ("wget", wget_command("wget", url, request, response)),
        (
            "busybox wget",
            wget_command("busybox", url, request, response),
        ),
    ] {
        match run_post(command, label, response) {
            Ok(bytes) => {
                logging::log(
                    Level::Info,
                    format!("RKP 传输工具 [RKP transport]: {label}"),
                );
                return Ok(bytes);
            }
            Err(error) => errors.push(format!("{label}: {error}")),
        }
    }
    Err(fail(format!(
        "所有传输工具均失败 [all transports failed]: {}",
        errors.join("; ")
    )))
}

fn curl_command(url: &str, request: &Path, response: &Path) -> Command {
    let mut command = Command::new("curl");
    command.args([
        "-sS",
        "--max-time",
        &HTTP_TIMEOUT_SECS.to_string(),
        "--connect-timeout",
        "10",
        "--request",
        "POST",
        "--header",
        "Content-Type: application/cbor",
        "--header",
        "Accept: application/cbor",
        "--data-binary",
    ]);
    command
        .arg(format!("@{}", request.display()))
        .arg("--output")
        .arg(response)
        .arg("--write-out")
        .arg("%{http_code}")
        .arg(url);
    command
}

fn wget_command(program: &str, url: &str, request: &Path, response: &Path) -> Command {
    let mut command = Command::new(program);
    if program == "busybox" {
        command.arg("wget");
    }
    command
        .args(["-q", "--timeout=30", "-O"])
        .arg(response)
        .arg("--header=Content-Type: application/cbor")
        .arg("--header=Accept: application/cbor")
        .arg("--post-file")
        .arg(request)
        .arg(url);
    command
}

fn run_post(command: Command, label: &str, response: &Path) -> Result<Vec<u8>> {
    let program = command.get_program().to_string_lossy().into_owned();
    let output = process::run_bounded(
        command,
        label,
        Duration::from_secs(HTTP_TIMEOUT_SECS + 10),
        32,
        "RKP 请求 [RKP request]",
    )?;
    if !output.status.success() {
        return Err(TfError::new(format!(
            "{program} 退出码 {} [exit code {}]{}",
            output.status.code().unwrap_or(128),
            output.status.code().unwrap_or(128),
            stderr_detail(&output.stderr)
        )));
    }
    if label == "curl" {
        let text = String::from_utf8_lossy(&output.stdout);
        if let Ok(status) = text.trim().parse::<u16>() {
            if status == 444 {
                return Err(fail("设备未在 Google RKP 注册 [device not registered]"));
            }
            if !(200..300).contains(&status) {
                return Err(fail(format!("HTTP {status}")));
            }
        }
    }
    read_response(response)
}

fn stderr_detail(stderr: &[u8]) -> String {
    let text = String::from_utf8_lossy(stderr);
    let normalized = text.split_whitespace().collect::<Vec<_>>().join(" ");
    let summary = normalized.chars().take(200).collect::<String>();
    if summary.is_empty() {
        String::new()
    } else {
        format!(": {summary}")
    }
}

fn read_response(path: &Path) -> Result<Vec<u8>> {
    use std::io::Read;
    let file = fs::File::open(path).map_err(|_| fail("响应文件不存在 [response missing]"))?;
    let mut bytes = Vec::new();
    file.take((MAX_RESPONSE + 1) as u64)
        .read_to_end(&mut bytes)?;
    if bytes.is_empty() {
        return Err(fail("响应为空 [empty response]"));
    }
    if bytes.len() > MAX_RESPONSE {
        return Err(fail("响应超过 1 MiB [response exceeds 1 MiB]"));
    }
    Ok(bytes)
}

fn parse_challenge(body: &[u8]) -> Result<Vec<u8>> {
    let value = decode(body)?;
    let items = match &value {
        Value::Array(items) => items,
        _ => return Err(fail("challenge 响应不是数组 [response is not an array]")),
    };
    let challenge = items
        .get(1)
        .and_then(|value| match value {
            Value::Bytes(bytes) => Some(bytes.as_slice()),
            _ => None,
        })
        .ok_or_else(|| fail("challenge 响应缺少 challenge [missing challenge]"))?;
    if challenge.is_empty() || challenge.len() > MAX_CHALLENGE_LEN {
        return Err(fail("challenge 长度无效 [invalid challenge length]"));
    }
    Ok(challenge.to_vec())
}

fn parse_certificates(body: &[u8]) -> Result<Vec<u8>> {
    let value = decode(body)?;
    let top = match &value {
        Value::Array(items) if !items.is_empty() => items.as_slice(),
        _ => return Err(fail("签发响应为空 [empty issuance response]")),
    };
    let inner = match &top[0] {
        Value::Array(items) => items.as_slice(),
        _ => top,
    };
    if inner.len() < 2 {
        return Err(fail("签发响应缺少证书链 [missing certificate chains]"));
    }
    let shared = match &inner[0] {
        Value::Bytes(bytes) => bytes,
        _ => return Err(fail("共享证书链不是字节串 [shared chain is not bytes]")),
    };
    let unique = match &inner[1] {
        Value::Array(items) => items,
        _ => return Err(fail("唯一证书链列表无效 [invalid unique chain list]")),
    };
    if unique.len() != 1 {
        return Err(fail(format!(
            "预期一条证书链，实际 {} [expected one chain, got {}]",
            unique.len(),
            unique.len()
        )));
    }
    let unique = match &unique[0] {
        Value::Bytes(bytes) => bytes,
        _ => return Err(fail("唯一证书链不是字节串 [unique chain is not bytes]")),
    };
    let mut chain = shared.clone();
    chain.extend_from_slice(unique);
    if chain.is_empty() || chain.len() > crate::rkp::MAX_CHAIN_BYTES {
        return Err(fail(
            "证书链长度为 1..16384 字节 [chain length out of range]",
        ));
    }
    Ok(chain)
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    fn encode_value(value: Value) -> Vec<u8> {
        encode(&value).unwrap()
    }

    #[test]
    fn endpoint_requires_https_or_loopback() {
        assert!(method_url("https://example.test/v1", "fetchEekChain").is_ok());
        assert!(method_url("http://127.0.0.1:8080/v1", "fetchEekChain").is_ok());
        assert!(method_url("http://example.test/v1", "fetchEekChain").is_err());
        assert!(method_url("https://example.test/v1?x=1", "fetchEekChain").is_err());
    }

    #[test]
    fn challenge_is_read_from_second_item() {
        let body = encode_value(Value::Array(vec![
            Value::Array(vec![]),
            Value::Bytes(b"nonce".to_vec()),
        ]));
        assert_eq!(parse_challenge(&body).unwrap(), b"nonce");
    }

    #[test]
    fn challenge_length_is_bounded() {
        let body = encode_value(Value::Array(vec![
            Value::Array(vec![]),
            Value::Bytes(vec![1; 65]),
        ]));
        assert!(parse_challenge(&body).is_err());
    }

    #[test]
    fn certificate_chains_are_concatenated() {
        let body = encode_value(Value::Array(vec![Value::Array(vec![
            Value::Bytes(vec![1, 2]),
            Value::Array(vec![Value::Bytes(vec![3, 4])]),
        ])]));
        assert_eq!(parse_certificates(&body).unwrap(), vec![1, 2, 3, 4]);
    }

    #[test]
    fn multiple_unique_chains_are_rejected() {
        let body = encode_value(Value::Array(vec![Value::Array(vec![
            Value::Bytes(vec![1]),
            Value::Array(vec![Value::Bytes(vec![2]), Value::Bytes(vec![3])]),
        ])]));
        assert!(parse_certificates(&body).is_err());
    }
}
```
