`crates/teeforge/src/rkp/soc.rs`:

```rs
//! 高通 SoC 判定 [Qualcomm SoC detection]
//!
//! 多来源取证，任一来源冲突即拒绝，不靠单个属性猜测。
//! Multiple sources are inspected; conflicting evidence blocks issuance instead of guessing.
//! 移植自 Duck-ToolBox（MIT），见 `rkp/mod.rs` 顶部的来源说明。

use crate::process;
use std::fs::File;
use std::io::Read;

#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub(crate) enum State {
    Qualcomm,
    Other,
    Unknown,
    Conflicting,
}

impl State {
    pub(crate) fn label(self) -> &'static str {
        match self {
            Self::Qualcomm => "Qualcomm",
            Self::Other => "Other",
            Self::Unknown => "Unknown",
            Self::Conflicting => "Conflicting",
        }
    }
}

#[derive(Clone, Debug)]
pub(crate) struct Detection {
    pub(crate) state: State,
    pub(crate) evidence: Vec<String>,
}

impl Detection {
    /// 供 `--rkp-status` 与失败信息使用的单行摘要。
    pub(crate) fn summary(&self) -> String {
        if self.evidence.is_empty() {
            return self.state.label().to_owned();
        }
        format!("{} ({})", self.state.label(), self.evidence.join(", "))
    }
}

const PROPERTIES: [&str; 9] = [
    "ro.soc.manufacturer",
    "ro.vendor.soc.manufacturer",
    "ro.soc.model",
    "ro.vendor.soc.model",
    "ro.vendor.qti.soc_model",
    "ro.hardware",
    "ro.boot.hardware",
    "ro.board.platform",
    "ro.product.board",
];

const SYSFS: [&str; 4] = [
    "/sys/devices/soc0/family",
    "/sys/devices/soc0/machine",
    "/sys/devices/system/soc/soc0/family",
    "/sys/devices/system/soc/soc0/machine",
];

#[derive(Clone, Copy)]
enum Kind {
    Manufacturer,
    Model,
    Hardware,
    Family,
}

pub(crate) fn detect() -> Detection {
    let properties = PROPERTIES
        .iter()
        .filter_map(|name| read_property(name).map(|value| ((*name).to_owned(), value)))
        .collect::<Vec<_>>();
    let sysfs = SYSFS
        .iter()
        .filter_map(|path| read_sysfs(path).map(|value| ((*path).to_owned(), value)))
        .collect::<Vec<_>>();
    classify(&properties, &sysfs)
}

fn read_property(name: &str) -> Option<String> {
    let output = process::output("getprop", [name]).ok()?;
    let value = process::stdout_text(output, name).ok()?;
    non_empty(value.trim())
}

fn read_sysfs(path: &str) -> Option<String> {
    let file = File::open(path).ok()?;
    let mut value = String::new();
    // sysfs 属性可能报告整页长度，按 256 字节实际上限截断读取。
    // sysfs attributes can report a page-sized length; bound the actual read at 256 bytes.
    file.take(257).read_to_string(&mut value).ok()?;
    if value.len() > 256 {
        return None;
    }
    non_empty(value.trim())
}

fn non_empty(value: &str) -> Option<String> {
    if value.is_empty()
        || matches!(
            value.to_ascii_lowercase().as_str(),
            "unknown" | "null" | "none" | "n/a"
        )
    {
        return None;
    }
    Some(value.to_owned())
}

/// 纯函数形式便于主机侧测试 [Pure form so host tests can cover it].
pub(crate) fn classify(properties: &[(String, String)], sysfs: &[(String, String)]) -> Detection {
    let mut evidence = Vec::new();
    let mut qualcomm = false;
    let mut other = false;

    for (source, value) in properties {
        let kind = if source.contains("manufacturer") {
            Kind::Manufacturer
        } else if source.contains("soc_model") || source.ends_with("soc.model") {
            Kind::Model
        } else {
            Kind::Hardware
        };
        if let Some(vendor) = vendor_of(value, kind) {
            evidence.push(format!("{source}={value}"));
            match vendor {
                Vendor::Qualcomm => qualcomm = true,
                Vendor::Other => other = true,
            }
        }
    }

    for (source, value) in sysfs {
        let kind = if source.ends_with("family") {
            Kind::Family
        } else {
            Kind::Model
        };
        if let Some(vendor) = vendor_of(value, kind) {
            evidence.push(format!("{source}={value}"));
            match vendor {
                Vendor::Qualcomm => qualcomm = true,
                Vendor::Other => other = true,
            }
        }
    }

    let state = match (qualcomm, other) {
        (true, false) => State::Qualcomm,
        (false, true) => State::Other,
        (false, false) => State::Unknown,
        (true, true) => State::Conflicting,
    };
    Detection { state, evidence }
}

enum Vendor {
    Qualcomm,
    Other,
}

fn vendor_of(value: &str, kind: Kind) -> Option<Vendor> {
    match kind {
        Kind::Manufacturer => manufacturer(value),
        Kind::Model => qualcomm_model(value).then_some(Vendor::Qualcomm),
        Kind::Hardware => {
            manufacturer(value).or_else(|| qualcomm_model(value).then_some(Vendor::Qualcomm))
        }
        Kind::Family => {
            if value.eq_ignore_ascii_case("snapdragon") {
                Some(Vendor::Qualcomm)
            } else {
                manufacturer(value)
            }
        }
    }
}

fn manufacturer(value: &str) -> Option<Vendor> {
    let normalized = value
        .split(|character: char| !character.is_ascii_alphanumeric())
        .filter(|part| !part.is_empty())
        .collect::<Vec<_>>()
        .join(" ")
        .to_ascii_lowercase();
    match normalized.as_str() {
        "qti"
        | "qcom"
        | "qualcomm"
        | "qualcomm inc"
        | "qualcomm incorporated"
        | "qualcomm technologies"
        | "qualcomm technologies inc"
        | "qualcomm technologies incorporated"
        | "qualcomm technologies ltd" => Some(Vendor::Qualcomm),
        "mediatek"
        | "mediatek inc"
        | "mediatek incorporated"
        | "mtk"
        | "samsung"
        | "samsung electronics"
        | "samsung electronics co ltd"
        | "exynos"
        | "google"
        | "google inc"
        | "google tensor"
        | "tensor"
        | "hisilicon"
        | "hisilicon technologies co ltd"
        | "kirin"
        | "unisoc"
        | "spreadtrum"
        | "sprd"
        | "rockchip"
        | "amlogic"
        | "nvidia"
        | "intel"
        | "amd"
        | "apple"
        | "broadcom" => Some(Vendor::Other),
        _ => None,
    }
}

fn qualcomm_model(value: &str) -> bool {
    let model = value.to_ascii_uppercase();
    let (part, suffix) = model.split_once('-').unwrap_or((&model, ""));
    if suffix.len() > 4 || !suffix.bytes().all(|byte| byte.is_ascii_alphanumeric()) {
        return false;
    }
    ["MSM", "APQ", "SDM", "SM", "QCM", "QCS"]
        .iter()
        .any(|prefix| {
            let Some(number) = part.strip_prefix(prefix) else {
                return false;
            };
            let digits = number.bytes().take_while(u8::is_ascii_digit).count();
            let expected = if matches!(*prefix, "SM" | "QCM" | "QCS") {
                4..=4
            } else {
                3..=4
            };
            expected.contains(&digits)
                && number.len() - digits <= 2
                && number[digits..]
                    .bytes()
                    .all(|byte| byte.is_ascii_uppercase())
        })
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    fn pairs(values: &[(&str, &str)]) -> Vec<(String, String)> {
        values
            .iter()
            .map(|(key, value)| ((*key).to_owned(), (*value).to_owned()))
            .collect()
    }

    #[test]
    fn qualcomm_manufacturer_is_accepted() {
        let detection = classify(&pairs(&[("ro.soc.manufacturer", "Qualcomm")]), &[]);
        assert_eq!(detection.state, State::Qualcomm);
        assert_eq!(detection.evidence.len(), 1);
    }

    #[test]
    fn qualcomm_part_number_is_accepted_without_manufacturer() {
        let detection = classify(&pairs(&[("ro.board.platform", "SM8750")]), &[]);
        assert_eq!(detection.state, State::Qualcomm);
    }

    #[test]
    fn mediatek_is_rejected() {
        let detection = classify(&pairs(&[("ro.soc.manufacturer", "MediaTek")]), &[]);
        assert_eq!(detection.state, State::Other);
    }

    #[test]
    fn conflicting_sources_block_issuance() {
        let detection = classify(
            &pairs(&[
                ("ro.soc.manufacturer", "Qualcomm"),
                ("ro.vendor.soc.manufacturer", "MediaTek"),
            ]),
            &[],
        );
        assert_eq!(detection.state, State::Conflicting);
    }

    #[test]
    fn unknown_values_are_ignored() {
        let detection = classify(&pairs(&[("ro.soc.manufacturer", "unknown")]), &[]);
        assert_eq!(detection.state, State::Unknown);
        assert!(detection.evidence.is_empty());
    }

    #[test]
    fn sysfs_family_snapdragon_counts_as_qualcomm() {
        let detection = classify(&[], &pairs(&[("/sys/devices/soc0/family", "Snapdragon")]));
        assert_eq!(detection.state, State::Qualcomm);
    }
}
```

`crates/teeforge/src/rkp/ta.rs`:

```rs
//! 高通 KeymasterUtils 厂商 ABI 适配 [Qualcomm KeymasterUtils vendor ABI]
//!
//! 移植自 Duck-ToolBox 的 `duck-rkp-device::keymint`（MIT）。
//! Ported from Duck-ToolBox `duck-rkp-device::keymint` (MIT).
//!
//! 对象布局与符号随固件变化，存在性检查不能证明 ABI 兼容；调用一律在隔离子进程
//! 中进行，崩溃与厂商输出不会污染主进程。
//! Layout and symbols vary by firmware; calls always run in an isolated child.
//!
//! 本层只负责调用，**不解释响应**：返回原始缓冲区，帧布局由 `protocol::decode_frame`
//! 结合业务结构判定。早期实现在这里剥帧、上层又剥一次，导致 CBOR 被当成帧头解析。
//! This layer does not interpret the reply; framing is resolved exactly once upstream.
//!
//! 探测模式 [`PROBES`] 用于区分「固件不认命令」「缓冲区约定不同」「参数顺序相反」：
//! 每个变体在独立子进程里执行，崩溃只会丢失该组结果。
//! Probe variants run one per child process, so a crash only loses that variant.

use crate::error::Result;
use crate::rkp::protocol::BUFFER_SIZE;

/// 原始响应缓冲区与 `send_cmd` 返回码。
///
/// 保留整块缓冲区（而不是解析后的 payload），是为了让上层能在不同帧布局之间尝试，
/// 并在全部失败时把首字节 hex 透出给诊断界面。
/// The whole buffer is kept so the caller can try several layouts and report raw bytes.
#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub(crate) struct RawFrame {
    pub(crate) bytes: Vec<u8>,
    pub(crate) status: i32,
}

/// 响应缓冲区的初始化方式。
///
/// 部分固件会把 out 缓冲区的前几个字节当作「容量」或状态字段读取，预置哨兵反而会
/// 让它走空响应分支，因此探测时需要区分这三种做法。
/// Some firmware reads the first bytes of the out buffer as capacity or status.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub(crate) enum BufferInit {
    /// 前 4 字节写 `i32::MIN`，用于识别「完全未写入」。
    Sentinel,
    /// 全 0。
    Zero,
    /// 前 4 字节写缓冲区容量。
    Capacity,
}

/// 请求帧的封装方式。
///
/// 主机侧只验证协议与帧构造，厂商调用路径仅在 arm64 Android 编译，
/// 因此非该目标下 `LengthLe` 可能是未使用变体。
#[cfg_attr(
    not(all(target_os = "android", target_arch = "aarch64")),
    allow(dead_code)
)]
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub(crate) enum RequestFrame {
    /// `[u32 LE cmd][cbor]`，当前实现。
    Plain,
    /// `[u32 LE cmd][u32 BE len][cbor]`
    LengthBe,
    /// `[u32 LE cmd][u32 LE len][cbor]`
    ///
    /// 保留为候选：大端约定更常见，但小端实现存在，需要时加一行变体即可。
    /// Kept as a candidate; only big-endian is currently probed.
    #[allow(dead_code)]
    LengthLe,
}

/// 一次调用的全部可变参数。
#[cfg_attr(
    not(all(target_os = "android", target_arch = "aarch64")),
    allow(dead_code)
)]
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy)]
pub(crate) struct CallOptions {
    pub(crate) level: i32,
    pub(crate) buffer: BufferInit,
    pub(crate) buffer_len: usize,
    pub(crate) frame: RequestFrame,
    /// 传给厂商构造函数的对象存储大小。
    ///
    /// 上游按 0x40 分配，但那只是「观察到的客户端」的值；固件升级后对象可能变大，
    /// 构造写越界会破坏堆上相邻数据，表现为后续调用静默失败。
    /// Upstream allocates 0x40 from one observed client; newer firmware may need more.
    pub(crate) object_size: usize,
}

impl Default for CallOptions {
    fn default() -> Self {
        Self {
            level: 1,
            buffer: BufferInit::Sentinel,
            buffer_len: BUFFER_SIZE,
            frame: RequestFrame::Plain,
            object_size: 0x40,
        }
    }
}

/// 一次调用的原始观察，包含请求缓冲区被改写的情况。
pub(crate) struct CallResult {
    pub(crate) status: i32,
    pub(crate) response: Vec<u8>,
    pub(crate) request_before: Vec<u8>,
    pub(crate) request_after: Vec<u8>,
}

/// 探测变体表：标签 + 参数。
///
/// 顺序固定，`--rkp-probe` 的输出按这个顺序排列，便于与现场日志对照。
pub(crate) const PROBES: [(&str, CallOptions); 8] = [
    (
        "baseline",
        CallOptions {
            level: 1,
            buffer: BufferInit::Sentinel,
            buffer_len: BUFFER_SIZE,
            frame: RequestFrame::Plain,
            object_size: 0x40,
        },
    ),
    (
        "zero-buffer",
        CallOptions {
            level: 1,
            buffer: BufferInit::Zero,
            buffer_len: BUFFER_SIZE,
            frame: RequestFrame::Plain,
            object_size: 0x40,
        },
    ),
    (
        "capacity-header",
        CallOptions {
            level: 1,
            buffer: BufferInit::Capacity,
            buffer_len: BUFFER_SIZE,
            frame: RequestFrame::Plain,
            object_size: 0x40,
        },
    ),
    (
        "small-buffer",
        CallOptions {
            level: 1,
            buffer: BufferInit::Zero,
            buffer_len: 0x2000,
            frame: RequestFrame::Plain,
            object_size: 0x40,
        },
    ),
    (
        "level-0",
        CallOptions {
            level: 0,
            buffer: BufferInit::Zero,
            buffer_len: BUFFER_SIZE,
            frame: RequestFrame::Plain,
            object_size: 0x40,
        },
    ),
    (
        "length-prefix",
        CallOptions {
            level: 1,
            buffer: BufferInit::Zero,
            buffer_len: BUFFER_SIZE,
            frame: RequestFrame::LengthBe,
            object_size: 0x40,
        },
    ),
    (
        "object-0x80",
        CallOptions {
            level: 1,
            buffer: BufferInit::Zero,
            buffer_len: BUFFER_SIZE,
            frame: RequestFrame::Plain,
            object_size: 0x80,
        },
    ),
    (
        "object-0x100",
        CallOptions {
            level: 1,
            buffer: BufferInit::Zero,
            buffer_len: BUFFER_SIZE,
            frame: RequestFrame::Plain,
            object_size: 0x100,
        },
    ),
];

/// 按变体把命令头与 payload 重新封装成实际发送的字节。
#[cfg_attr(
    not(all(target_os = "android", target_arch = "aarch64")),
    allow(dead_code)
)]
pub(crate) fn frame_request(request: &[u8], frame: RequestFrame) -> Vec<u8> {
    let Some((command, payload)) = request.split_at_checked(4) else {
        return request.to_vec();
    };
    let length = payload.len().to_le_bytes();
    match frame {
        RequestFrame::Plain => request.to_vec(),
        RequestFrame::LengthBe => {
            let mut out = command.to_vec();
            out.extend_from_slice(&(payload.len() as u32).to_be_bytes());
            out.extend_from_slice(payload);
            out
        }
        RequestFrame::LengthLe => {
            let mut out = command.to_vec();
            out.extend_from_slice(&length);
            out.extend_from_slice(payload);
            out
        }
    }
}

/// 缓冲区中最后一个非零字节之后的长度；`0` 表示完全没被写入。
pub(crate) fn written_len(bytes: &[u8]) -> usize {
    bytes
        .iter()
        .rposition(|byte| *byte != 0)
        .map(|index| index + 1)
        .unwrap_or(0)
}

/// 请求前后是否被厂商代码改写；改写说明 out 参数可能落在了请求缓冲区上。
pub(crate) fn request_changed(before: &[u8], after: &[u8]) -> bool {
    before != after
}

#[cfg(all(target_os = "android", target_arch = "aarch64"))]
mod imp {
    use crate::error::{Result, TfError};
    use crate::rkp::protocol::BUFFER_SIZE;
    use crate::rkp::ta::{BufferInit, CallOptions, CallResult, RawFrame, frame_request};
    use std::ffi::c_void;

    use libloading::Library;

    const LIBRARY_PATH: &str = "/vendor/lib64/libkeymasterdeviceutils.so";
    /// 观察窗口：足够覆盖 CBOR 头部与帧头。
    const OBSERVE_BYTES: usize = 96;

    type Constructor = unsafe extern "C" fn(*mut c_void, i32);
    type Destructor = unsafe extern "C" fn(*mut c_void);
    type SendCommand = unsafe extern "C" fn(*mut c_void, *mut c_void, u32, *mut c_void, u32) -> i32;

    /// 对象存储的尾部余量。
    ///
    /// 探测「对象比预期大」时，若按较小尺寸分配，构造函数写越界会破坏相邻堆数据，
    /// 反而改变被测行为。预留余量后越界落在自己的存储内，结果才有意义。
    /// Slack keeps an oversized constructor from corrupting unrelated heap data.
    const OBJECT_SLACK: usize = 0x100;

    struct Session {
        /// `u128` 保证 16 字节对齐，与厂商类的要求一致。
        /// `u128` gives the 16-byte alignment the vendor class expects.
        object: Vec<u128>,
        destructor: Destructor,
        send: SendCommand,
        _library: Library,
    }

    fn fail(message: impl Into<String>) -> TfError {
        TfError::new(format!(
            "厂商 KeymasterUtils [vendor KeymasterUtils]: {}",
            message.into()
        ))
    }

    impl Session {
        fn open(level: i32, object_size: usize) -> Result<Self> {
            // SAFETY: 仅本适配层解析固定厂商 ABI，且只在 arm64 Android 的隔离子进程中调用。
            // SAFETY: only this adapter resolves the fixed vendor ABI, in an isolated child.
            let library: Library = unsafe {
                libloading::os::unix::Library::open(
                    Some(LIBRARY_PATH),
                    libc::RTLD_NOW | libc::RTLD_LOCAL,
                )
            }
            .map_err(|error| fail(format!("加载 {LIBRARY_PATH} 失败 [load failed]: {error}")))?
            .into();
            let constructor = *unsafe {
                library.get::<Constructor>(
                    b"_ZN14keymasterutils14KeymasterUtilsC1E26keymaster_security_level_t\0",
                )
            }
            .map_err(|_| fail("缺少构造函数符号 [constructor symbol missing]"))?;
            let destructor =
                *unsafe { library.get::<Destructor>(b"_ZN14keymasterutils14KeymasterUtilsD1Ev\0") }
                    .map_err(|_| fail("缺少析构函数符号 [destructor symbol missing]"))?;
            let send = *unsafe {
                library
                    .get::<SendCommand>(b"_ZN14keymasterutils14KeymasterUtils8send_cmdEPvjS1_j\0")
            }
            .map_err(|_| fail("缺少 send_cmd 符号 [send_cmd symbol missing]"))?;
            let words = (object_size.max(0x10) + OBJECT_SLACK).div_ceil(16);
            let mut object = vec![0u128; words];
            // SAFETY: 对齐的可写存储地址在 Drop 前保持稳定；容量含余量。
            // SAFETY: stable aligned storage with slack for oversized objects.
            unsafe { constructor(object.as_mut_ptr().cast(), level) };
            Ok(Self {
                object,
                destructor,
                send,
                _library: library,
            })
        }

        fn call(&mut self, request: &[u8], options: CallOptions) -> CallResult {
            let sent = frame_request(request, options.frame);
            let mut request = sent;
            let capacity = options.buffer_len.min(BUFFER_SIZE).max(16);
            let mut response = vec![0u8; capacity];
            match options.buffer {
                BufferInit::Sentinel => {
                    response[..4].copy_from_slice(&i32::MIN.to_le_bytes());
                }
                BufferInit::Zero => {}
                BufferInit::Capacity => {
                    response[..4].copy_from_slice(&(capacity as u32).to_le_bytes());
                }
            }
            let before = request[..request.len().min(OBSERVE_BYTES)].to_vec();
            // SAFETY: 实例存活，两块可写缓冲区在同步调用期间有效且长度匹配。
            // SAFETY: live instance, disjoint buffers valid for the synchronous call.
            let status = unsafe {
                (self.send)(
                    self.object.as_mut_ptr().cast(),
                    request.as_mut_ptr().cast(),
                    request.len() as u32,
                    response.as_mut_ptr().cast(),
                    response.len() as u32,
                )
            };
            let after = request[..request.len().min(OBSERVE_BYTES)].to_vec();
            CallResult {
                status,
                response,
                request_before: before,
                request_after: after,
            }
        }

        fn request(&mut self, request: &[u8]) -> Result<RawFrame> {
            let result = self.call(request, CallOptions::default());
            if result.status != 0 {
                return Err(crate::rkp::explain::status_error(result.status));
            }
            // 哨兵仍在说明厂商完全没有写入；此时直接报 ABI 问题，比让上层猜帧格式准确。
            if result.response.starts_with(&i32::MIN.to_le_bytes()) {
                return Err(TfError::staged(
                    "frame",
                    "abi",
                    "TA 未写入响应缓冲区 [TA never wrote a response]",
                )
                .with_hint("rkp.frame.abi")
                .with_raw(serde_json::json!({
                    "buffer": result.response.len(),
                    "request_changed": super::request_changed(&result.request_before, &result.request_after),
                    "head_hex": crate::rkp::protocol::head_hex(&result.response),
                })));
            }
            Ok(RawFrame {
                bytes: result.response,
                status: result.status,
            })
        }
    }

    impl Drop for Session {
        fn drop(&mut self) {
            // SAFETY: 析构只执行一次，且对象与库在调用期间均存活。
            // SAFETY: the destructor runs once while both the object and library are live.
            unsafe { (self.destructor)(self.object.as_mut_ptr().cast()) };
        }
    }

    pub(super) fn request(bytes: &[u8]) -> Result<RawFrame> {
        let options = CallOptions::default();
        Session::open(options.level, options.object_size)?.request(bytes)
    }

    pub(super) fn probe(bytes: &[u8], options: CallOptions) -> Result<CallResult> {
        Ok(Session::open(options.level, options.object_size)?.call(bytes, options))
    }
}

#[cfg(not(all(target_os = "android", target_arch = "aarch64")))]
mod imp {
    use crate::error::{Result, TfError};
    use crate::rkp::ta::{CallOptions, CallResult, RawFrame};

    pub(super) fn request(_bytes: &[u8]) -> Result<RawFrame> {
        Err(TfError::new(
            "设备端 RKP 只能由 Android arm64 触发 [device RKP requires Android arm64]",
        ))
    }

    pub(super) fn probe(_bytes: &[u8], _options: CallOptions) -> Result<CallResult> {
        Err(TfError::new(
            "设备端 RKP 只能由 Android arm64 触发 [device RKP requires Android arm64]",
        ))
    }
}

pub(crate) fn request(bytes: &[u8]) -> Result<RawFrame> {
    imp::request(bytes)
}

/// 用指定参数执行一次调用，不做状态判定。
pub(crate) fn probe(bytes: &[u8], options: CallOptions) -> Result<CallResult> {
    imp::probe(bytes, options)
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    #[test]
    fn plain_frame_keeps_bytes() {
        let request = [0x22, 0x31, 0x00, 0x00, 0xa1, 0x01];
        assert_eq!(
            frame_request(&request, RequestFrame::Plain),
            request.to_vec()
        );
    }

    #[test]
    fn length_prefix_is_inserted_after_command() {
        let request = [0x22, 0x31, 0x00, 0x00, 0xa1, 0x01];
        let framed = frame_request(&request, RequestFrame::LengthBe);
        assert_eq!(&framed[..4], &[0x22, 0x31, 0x00, 0x00]);
        assert_eq!(&framed[4..8], &[0, 0, 0, 2]);
        assert_eq!(&framed[8..], &[0xa1, 0x01]);
    }

    #[test]
    fn written_len_ignores_leading_zeros() {
        assert_eq!(written_len(&[0, 0, 0, 0]), 0);
        assert_eq!(written_len(&[0, 0, 7, 0]), 3);
    }

    #[test]
    fn request_change_is_detected() {
        assert!(request_changed(&[1, 2], &[1, 3]));
        assert!(!request_changed(&[1, 2], &[1, 2]));
    }

    #[test]
    fn probe_variants_have_unique_labels() {
        let mut labels = PROBES.iter().map(|(label, _)| *label).collect::<Vec<_>>();
        labels.sort_unstable();
        labels.dedup();
        assert_eq!(labels.len(), PROBES.len());
    }
}
```

`crates/teeforge/src/rkp/worker.rs`:

```rs
//! TA 调用的隔离子进程 [Isolated TA worker process]
//!
//! 移植自 Duck-ToolBox 的 `duck-rkp-device::worker`（MIT）。
//! Ported from Duck-ToolBox `duck-rkp-device::worker` (MIT).
//!
//! 厂商库可能崩溃或挂起，无法在主进程内安全中断；由父进程写入请求，子进程调用 TA
//! 并回写 JSON 回执，父进程超时后 kill 并回收。
//! Vendor calls cannot be interrupted in-process; a child owns the call and the
//! parent kills and reaps it on timeout.

use crate::error::{Result, TfError};
use crate::process;
use crate::rkp::protocol;
use crate::rkp::ta::RawFrame;
use crate::rkp::{unique_dir, write_private};
use base64::Engine;
use base64::engine::general_purpose::STANDARD;
use serde_json::{Value, json};
use std::env;
use std::fs;
use std::path::Path;
use std::process::Command;
use std::time::Duration;

const TIMEOUT: Duration = Duration::from_secs(30);
/// 0xa000 字节的 base64 约 54 KB，回执还带诊断字段，留足余量。
/// A 0xa000-byte frame is ~54 KB in base64; leave room for diagnostics.
const MAX_REPLY_BYTES: usize = 96 * 1024;
/// Bionic 在 main 之前读取该值；保留默认命名空间以继续解析系统依赖。
/// Bionic reads this before main; the default namespace keeps system dependency links.
const VENDOR_LIBRARY_PATH: &str =
    "/system/lib64:/vendor/lib64:/odm/lib64:/vendor/lib64/hw:/odm/lib64/hw";

pub(crate) fn sign(tmp_root: &Path, request: &[u8]) -> Result<RawFrame> {
    let directory = unique_dir(tmp_root)?;
    let result = (|| {
        write_private(&directory.join("request.bin"), request)?;
        let executable = env::current_exe().map_err(|error| {
            TfError::new(format!("无法定位当前可执行文件 [current_exe]: {error}"))
        })?;
        let mut command = Command::new(&executable);
        command
            .arg("--rkp-ta-worker")
            .arg(&directory)
            .env("LD_LIBRARY_PATH", VENDOR_LIBRARY_PATH);
        let output = process::run_bounded(
            command,
            "teeforge RKP worker",
            TIMEOUT,
            4096,
            "设备端 RKP 调用 [device RKP call]",
        )?;
        if !output.status.success() {
            return Err(TfError::new(format!(
                "RKP worker 退出码 {}，厂商 ABI 可能不兼容 [worker exit {}; vendor ABI may be incompatible]{}",
                output.status.code().unwrap_or(128),
                output.status.code().unwrap_or(128),
                stderr_detail(&output.stderr)
            )));
        }
        let reply = read_bounded(&directory.join("reply.json"), MAX_REPLY_BYTES)?;
        parse_reply(&reply)
    })();
    let _ = fs::remove_dir_all(&directory);
    result
}

/// 子进程入口：读取请求、调用 TA、写回执。
///
/// 回执必须保留结构化错误：早期用 `to_string()` 传字符串，父进程无法还原
/// `stage/code/hint/raw`，诊断信息（hex、长度）全部丢失。
/// Structured errors must survive the process boundary.
pub(crate) fn execute(directory: &Path) -> Result<()> {
    let result = (|| {
        let request = read_bounded(&directory.join("request.bin"), protocol::MAX_REQUEST_SIZE)?;
        protocol::ta_payload(&request)?;
        crate::rkp::ta::request(&request)
    })();
    let reply = match result {
        Ok(frame) => json!({
            "ok": true,
            "status": frame.status,
            "head_hex": protocol::head_hex(&frame.bytes),
            "frame": STANDARD.encode(&frame.bytes),
        }),
        Err(error) => json!({ "ok": false, "error": describe(&error) }),
    };
    let encoded = serde_json::to_vec(&reply)
        .map_err(|error| TfError::new(format!("编码 worker 回执失败 [encode reply]: {error}")))?;
    write_private(&directory.join("reply.json"), &encoded)
}

/// 逐个变体在独立子进程中探测。
///
/// 每个变体都会重新 dlopen 厂商库并构造对象：这样某一组触发崩溃时只丢失该组，
/// 而不是整轮探测；构造参数（security_level）不同也无法复用同一个对象。
/// Each variant gets its own child so a crash loses only that variant.
pub(crate) fn probe_all(tmp_root: &Path, request: &[u8]) -> Result<Vec<Value>> {
    let executable = env::current_exe()
        .map_err(|error| TfError::new(format!("无法定位当前可执行文件 [current_exe]: {error}")))?;
    let mut results = Vec::new();
    for (index, (label, _)) in crate::rkp::ta::PROBES.iter().enumerate() {
        let directory = unique_dir(tmp_root)?;
        let outcome = (|| -> Result<Value> {
            write_private(&directory.join("request.bin"), request)?;
            let mut command = Command::new(&executable);
            command
                .arg("--rkp-ta-probe")
                .arg(&directory)
                .arg(index.to_string())
                .env("LD_LIBRARY_PATH", VENDOR_LIBRARY_PATH);
            let output = process::run_bounded(
                command,
                "teeforge RKP probe",
                TIMEOUT,
                4096,
                "设备端 RKP 探测 [device RKP probe]",
            )?;
            if !output.status.success() {
                return Ok(json!({
                    "variant": label,
                    "ok": false,
                    "crashed": true,
                    "exit": output.status.code(),
                    "stderr": stderr_detail(&output.stderr),
                }));
            }
            let reply = read_bounded(&directory.join("probe.json"), MAX_REPLY_BYTES)?;
            let mut value: Value = serde_json::from_slice(&reply).map_err(|error| {
                TfError::new(format!("解析探测回执失败 [decode probe]: {error}"))
            })?;
            if value.is_object() {
                value["variant"] = json!(label);
            }
            Ok(value)
        })();
        let _ = fs::remove_dir_all(&directory);
        results.push(match outcome {
            Ok(value) => value,
            Err(error) => json!({ "variant": label, "ok": false, "error": error.to_string() }),
        });
    }
    Ok(results)
}

/// 子进程入口：按变体执行一次调用并写探测回执。
pub(crate) fn execute_probe(directory: &Path, index: usize) -> Result<()> {
    let result = (|| {
        let request = read_bounded(&directory.join("request.bin"), protocol::MAX_REQUEST_SIZE)?;
        protocol::ta_payload(&request)?;
        let (label, options) = crate::rkp::ta::PROBES
            .get(index)
            .ok_or_else(|| TfError::new("未知探测变体 [unknown probe variant]"))?;
        let call = crate::rkp::ta::probe(&request, *options)?;
        Ok(json!({
            "ok": true,
            "variant": label,
            "level": options.level,
            "buffer_len": options.buffer_len,
            "status": call.status,
            "written": crate::rkp::ta::written_len(&call.response),
            "response_head": protocol::head_hex(&call.response),
            "request_changed": crate::rkp::ta::request_changed(&call.request_before, &call.request_after),
            "request_before": protocol::head_hex(&call.request_before),
            "request_after": protocol::head_hex(&call.request_after),
        }))
    })();
    let reply = match result {
        Ok(value) => value,
        Err(error) => json!({ "ok": false, "error": describe(&error) }),
    };
    let encoded = serde_json::to_vec(&reply)
        .map_err(|error| TfError::new(format!("编码探测回执失败 [encode probe]: {error}")))?;
    write_private(&directory.join("probe.json"), &encoded)
}

/// 把 `TfError` 拆成可跨进程传输的对象。
fn describe(error: &TfError) -> Value {
    let mut payload = json!({ "message": error.message() });
    if let Some(value) = error.stage() {
        payload["stage"] = json!(value);
    }
    if let Some(value) = error.code() {
        payload["code"] = json!(value);
    }
    if let Some(value) = error.hint() {
        payload["hint"] = json!(value);
    }
    if let Some(value) = error.raw() {
        payload["raw"] = value.clone();
    }
    payload
}

fn parse_reply(bytes: &[u8]) -> Result<RawFrame> {
    let value: Value = serde_json::from_slice(bytes)
        .map_err(|error| TfError::new(format!("解析 worker 回执失败 [decode reply]: {error}")))?;
    if value.get("ok").and_then(Value::as_bool) != Some(true) {
        return Err(restore_error(value.get("error")));
    }
    let frame = value
        .get("frame")
        .and_then(Value::as_str)
        .ok_or_else(|| TfError::new("worker 回执缺少 frame [missing frame]"))?;
    let decoded = STANDARD
        .decode(frame)
        .map_err(|_| TfError::new("worker 回执 frame 解码失败 [frame decode failed]"))?;
    if decoded.len() > protocol::BUFFER_SIZE {
        return Err(TfError::new(
            "worker 回执超过 TA 缓冲区 [reply exceeds TA buffer]",
        ));
    }
    let status = value.get("status").and_then(Value::as_i64).unwrap_or(0) as i32;
    Ok(RawFrame {
        bytes: decoded,
        status,
    })
}

/// 还原子进程错误；兼容旧版纯字符串回执。
fn restore_error(value: Option<&Value>) -> TfError {
    let Some(value) = value else {
        return TfError::new("worker 回执缺少错误信息 [missing error]");
    };
    if let Some(text) = value.as_str() {
        return TfError::new(text.to_owned());
    }
    let text = |key: &str| value.get(key).and_then(Value::as_str);
    TfError::from_parts(
        text("stage"),
        text("code").map(str::to_owned),
        text("message").unwrap_or("未知错误 [unknown error]"),
        text("hint").map(str::to_owned),
        value.get("raw").cloned(),
    )
}

fn read_bounded(path: &Path, limit: usize) -> Result<Vec<u8>> {
    use std::io::Read;
    let metadata = fs::symlink_metadata(path).map_err(|error| {
        TfError::new(format!(
            "读取 {} 失败 [read failed]: {error}",
            path.display()
        ))
    })?;
    if !metadata.is_file() {
        return Err(TfError::new(format!(
            "{} 不是普通文件 [not a regular file]",
            path.display()
        )));
    }
    let file = fs::File::open(path)?;
    let mut bytes = Vec::new();
    file.take((limit + 1) as u64).read_to_end(&mut bytes)?;
    if bytes.len() > limit {
        return Err(TfError::new(format!(
            "{} 超出 {} 字节限制 [exceeds limit]",
            path.display(),
            limit
        )));
    }
    Ok(bytes)
}

fn stderr_detail(stderr: &[u8]) -> String {
    let text = String::from_utf8_lossy(stderr);
    let normalized = text.split_whitespace().collect::<Vec<_>>().join(" ");
    let summary = normalized.chars().take(200).collect::<String>();
    if summary.is_empty() {
        String::new()
    } else {
        format!(": {summary}")
    }
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    #[test]
    fn reply_without_frame_is_rejected() {
        assert!(parse_reply(br#"{"ok":true}"#).is_err());
    }

    #[test]
    fn legacy_string_error_is_accepted() {
        let error =
            parse_reply(r#"{"ok":false,"error":"TA 返回状态 -18"}"#.as_bytes()).unwrap_err();
        assert!(error.to_string().contains("-18"));
    }

    #[test]
    fn structured_error_keeps_stage_and_raw() {
        let reply = br#"{"ok":false,"error":{"stage":"frame","code":"unparsed","message":"x","hint":"rkp.frame.unparsed","raw":{"head_hex":"00"}}}"#;
        let error = parse_reply(reply).unwrap_err();
        assert_eq!(error.stage(), Some("frame"));
        assert_eq!(error.code(), Some("unparsed"));
        assert_eq!(error.hint(), Some("rkp.frame.unparsed"));
        assert!(error.raw().is_some());
    }

    #[test]
    fn frame_is_decoded() {
        let frame = parse_reply(br#"{"ok":true,"status":0,"frame":"YWJj"}"#).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(frame.bytes, b"abc");
        assert_eq!(frame.status, 0);
    }

    #[test]
    fn non_regular_reply_files_are_rejected() {
        let directory =
            std::env::temp_dir().join(format!("teeforge-rkp-worker-{}", std::process::id()));
        fs::create_dir_all(&directory).unwrap();
        assert!(read_bounded(&directory, 16).is_err());
        let _ = fs::remove_dir_all(directory);
    }
}
```
