`tests/webui_keybox.py`:

```py
"""本地模拟宿主交互回归；需要 Python Playwright；使用临时本地 HTTP 服务。
Mock-host WebUI regression; never connects to a device.
"""
import re
from functools import partial
from http.server import SimpleHTTPRequestHandler, ThreadingHTTPServer
from threading import Thread
from pathlib import Path
from playwright.sync_api import sync_playwright,expect
MOCK=r"""
localStorage.setItem('tf-lang', 'zh');
window.mock = {commands: [], failNext: false, pending: null};
window.ksu = {exec(command, options, callback) {
 window.mock.commands.push(command);
 const saved = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('test-config') || '{"keybox_source":"remote","keybox_remote_slot":1}');
 let data;
 if (command.includes('--bootstrap')) data={device:{brand:'Test',model:'Synthetic',name:'Test',android:'16',sdk:'36',security_patch:'',abi:'arm64-v8a',soc_model:'',soc_manufacturer:'',verified_boot:''},system:{kernel:'test',selinux:'Enforcing'},module:{version:'test',version_code:1},root:{method:'KernelSU',version:'test'},config:{...saved,target_mode:'auto',update_channel:'dev'},capabilities:{rkp_artifacts:true,rkp_supported:false,rkp_platform:true},paths:{rkp_dir:'/test/rkp',keybox_dir:'/test/keybox'}};
 else if (command.includes('--rkp-artifacts')) data={exists:true};
 else if (command.includes('--keybox')) {
  window.mock.pending=()=>{
   if(window.mock.failNext){window.mock.failNext=false;window[callback](1,JSON.stringify({ok:false,error:{message:'synthetic download failure'}}),'');return;}
   const source=command.includes('--source rkp')?'rkp':'remote';
   const slot=Number(command.match(/--remote-slot (\d)/)?.[1]||saved.keybox_remote_slot);
   localStorage.setItem('test-config',JSON.stringify({keybox_source:source,keybox_remote_slot:slot}));
   window[callback](0,JSON.stringify({ok:true,data:{source,remote_slot:source==='remote'?slot:null,targets:[{path:'/test/local',changed:command.includes('--force')},{path:'/test/backend',changed:true}]}}),'');
  };
  return;
 }
 else {window[callback](1,'{}','unexpected mock command');return;}
 setTimeout(()=>window[callback](0,JSON.stringify({ok:true,data}),''),0);
}};
"""
server = ThreadingHTTPServer(('127.0.0.1', 0), partial(SimpleHTTPRequestHandler, directory=str(Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1] / 'module/webroot')))
thread = Thread(target=server.serve_forever, daemon=True)
thread.start()
try:
    with sync_playwright() as p:
     browser=p.chromium.launch(headless=True)
     page=browser.new_page(viewport={"width":390,"height":844})
     errors=[]
     page.on('pageerror',lambda e:errors.append(str(e)))
     page.add_init_script(MOCK)
     page.goto(f'http://127.0.0.1:{server.server_port}/#/keybox')
     page.wait_for_load_state('networkidle')
     slot=lambda n:page.get_by_role('button',name=f'槽位 {n}',exact=True)
     expect(slot(1)).to_have_attribute('aria-pressed','true')
     slot(2).click()
     page.get_by_role('button',name='应用所选来源',exact=True).click()
     expect(page.get_by_role('dialog')).to_contain_text('槽位 2')
     page.evaluate('window.mock.failNext=true')
     page.get_by_role('button',name='确认',exact=True).click()
     expect(slot(1)).to_be_disabled()
     expect(page.get_by_role('button',name='取消',exact=True)).to_be_disabled()
     page.keyboard.press('Escape')
     expect(page.get_by_role('dialog')).to_be_visible()
     assert '--remote-slot 2' in page.evaluate('window.mock.commands.at(-1)')
     assert '--force' not in page.evaluate('window.mock.commands.at(-1)')
     page.evaluate('window.mock.pending()')
     expect(page.get_by_role('dialog')).not_to_be_visible()
     expect(page.get_by_text('synthetic download failure',exact=True)).to_be_visible()
     assert page.evaluate("JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('test-config')||'{}').keybox_remote_slot") is None
     page.get_by_role('button',name='应用所选来源',exact=True).click()
     page.get_by_role('button',name='确认',exact=True).click()
     page.evaluate('window.mock.pending()')
     expect(page.get_by_role('dialog')).not_to_be_visible()
     expect(page.get_by_text('操作成功，实际写入 1 个目标',exact=True)).to_be_visible()
     assert page.evaluate("JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('test-config')).keybox_remote_slot")==2
     page.reload();page.wait_for_load_state('networkidle')
     expect(slot(2)).to_have_attribute('aria-pressed','true')
     slot(3).click()
     page.get_by_role('button',name='强制拉取并替换',exact=True).click()
     expect(page.get_by_role('dialog')).to_contain_text('即使内容与本地相同')
     page.get_by_role('button',name='确认',exact=True).click()
     command=page.evaluate('window.mock.commands.at(-1)')
     assert '--remote-slot 3' in command and '--force' in command and '--save-selection' in command
     page.evaluate('window.mock.pending()')
     expect(page.get_by_text('操作成功，实际写入 2 个目标',exact=True)).to_be_visible()
     Path('out').mkdir(exist_ok=True)
     page.screenshot(path='out/keybox-slots-remote.png',full_page=True)
     page.get_by_role('button',name=re.compile('本机 RKP 签发')).click()
     expect(slot(3)).not_to_be_visible()
     expect(page.get_by_role('button',name='强制拉取并替换',exact=True)).not_to_be_visible()
     page.get_by_role('button',name='应用所选来源',exact=True).click()
     page.get_by_role('button',name='确认',exact=True).click()
     command=page.evaluate('window.mock.commands.at(-1)')
     assert '--source rkp' in command and '--remote-slot' not in command and '--force' not in command
     page.evaluate('window.mock.pending()')
     assert page.evaluate("JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('test-config')).keybox_remote_slot")==3
     page.get_by_role('button',name=re.compile('远程公共密钥池')).click()
     expect(slot(3)).to_have_attribute('aria-pressed','true')
     assert not errors,errors
     assert page.locator('[data-render-error]').count()==0
     browser.close()
     print('WebUI mocked-host checks passed: default/slots/normal/force/failure/save/reload/busy/RKP; no runtime errors.')
finally:
    server.shutdown()
    server.server_close()
    thread.join()

```

`webroot/CLAUDE.md`:

```md
# TeeForge WebUI

KernelSU 模块 WebUI，Vite + Vue 3 + vue-router（hash）+ Tailwind 3.4。

## 开发

```bash
cd webroot
npm install
npm run dev        # 本地开发服务器
npm run build      # 输出到 module/webroot/
npm run preview
```

## 结构

```
index.html              主题初始化脚本（首帧前）+ 挂载点
src/main.ts             i18n → createApp → router → loadSession
src/router.ts           hash 路由：/ /target /keybox /rkp /update
src/lib/ksu.ts          宿主探测（hasHost/canSpawn）、exec/spawn、应用名与图标
src/lib/api.ts          唯一的命令出口：拼 --json 参数、解析信封、抛 ApiError
src/lib/i18n.ts         字典加载、t()、setLanguage；缺键返回键名
src/lib/format.ts       字节/时间/指纹格式化
src/state/session.ts    --bootstrap 结果单例，跨页共享
src/components/ui/      AppButton/AppSheet/AppBadge/SegmentedControl/AppIcon/EmptyState
src/components/layout/  AppBar/DeviceInfo/SectionCard/NavRow/PreferencesSheet
src/pages/              HomePage/TargetPage/KeyboxPage/RkpPage/UpdatePage
src/styles/tokens.css   设计令牌（明/暗两套，纯 hex/rgba）
src/styles/tailwind.css Tailwind 入口与全局基样式
public/i18n/*.json      运行时字典（zh/en/ja）
```

## 关键约定

**命令契约**：所有后端调用必须走 `src/lib/api.ts`，参数带 `--json`。成功是
`{"ok":true,"data":{...}}`，失败是 `{"ok":false,"error":{stage,code,message,hint,raw}}`。
`--json` 只允许搭配一个命令；进度日志在 stderr，不会污染 stdout。

**流式执行**：耗时命令（`--rkp-issue`、`--keybox`、`--self-update install`）用
`callStream`，stderr 直接当进度日志展示；没有 `ksu.spawn` 时自动退回一次性执行。

**降级**：`hasHost()` 为假时页面只读，不发起任何命令；`ksu.getPackagesInfo` /
`ksu://icon` 不可用时，列表退回包名与首字母占位。

**主题与语言**：`<html data-mode="auto|light|dark">` 由 `index.html` 的内联脚本在首帧前设定；
持久化键固定为 `tf-theme`（JSON `{theme,mode}`）与 `tf-lang`，升级不能改。

**样式**：颜色只通过 `tokens.css` 的 CSS 变量，Tailwind 配置把它们映射成
`bg-surface` / `text-muted` 这类工具类。不要在组件里写死色值；不要使用
`color-mix()`、`:has()`、`dvh`，构建目标是 `chrome87`。

**i18n**：新增文案必须同时补 `public/i18n/{zh,en,ja}.json` 三份，键集合保持一致；
动态键（如 `rkp.code.<code>`）用 `hasKey()` 判断后再取。

## 构建

- `npm run build` → `../module/webroot/`（`emptyOutDir` 会清空旧产物）
- `module/webroot/` 是构建产物，已 gitignore
- `package.json` 为锁定依赖，`xtask` 使用 `npm ci`

## 文档

- Astro 已移除，不要再按 Astro 方式新增页面或组件
- KernelSU WebUI: https://kernelsu.org/zh_CN/guide/module-webui.html

```

`webroot/index.html`:

```html
<!doctype html>
<html lang="zh-CN" data-mode="auto">
  <head>
    <meta charset="UTF-8" />
    <meta
      name="viewport"
      content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1.0, maximum-scale=1.0, user-scalable=no, viewport-fit=cover"
    />
    <meta name="color-scheme" content="light dark" />
    <meta name="theme-color" content="#eef2f1" media="(prefers-color-scheme: light)" />
    <meta name="theme-color" content="#0c1211" media="(prefers-color-scheme: dark)" />
    <title>TeeForge</title>
    <!-- 主题在首帧前确定，避免明暗闪烁 -->
    <script>
      (function () {
        var mode = 'auto';
        try {
          var saved = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('tf-theme') || 'null');
          if (saved && (saved.mode === 'auto' || saved.mode === 'light' || saved.mode === 'dark')) {
            mode = saved.mode;
          }
        } catch (_) {}
        document.documentElement.setAttribute('data-mode', mode);
        if (mode !== 'auto') document.documentElement.classList.add(mode);
      })();
    </script>
  </head>
  <body>
    <div id="app"></div>
    <script type="module" src="/src/main.ts"></script>
  </body>
</html>

```

`webroot/src/App.vue`:

```vue
<script setup lang="ts">
import { computed } from 'vue';
import { useRoute } from 'vue-router';

import AppBar from './components/layout/AppBar.vue';
import { t } from './lib/i18n';

const route = useRoute();
const title = computed(() => t(String(route.meta.titleKey ?? 'app.title')));
const isHome = computed(() => route.name === 'home');
</script>

<template>
  <div class="shell safe-x">
    <AppBar :title="title" :show-back="!isHome" />
    <main class="mx-auto w-full max-w-[560px] px-4 pb-10">
      <router-view />
    </main>
  </div>
</template>

`webroot/src/components/layout/AppBar.vue`:

```vue
<script setup lang="ts">
import { ref } from 'vue';
import { useRouter } from 'vue-router';

import AppIcon from '../ui/AppIcon.vue';
import PreferencesSheet from './PreferencesSheet.vue';

const props = defineProps<{ title: string; showBack: boolean }>();
const router = useRouter();
const preferencesOpen = ref(false);

function goBack() {
  // 直接打开二级页时没有可回退的历史，回到首页而不是退出 WebUI。
  const state = window.history.state as { back?: string | null } | null;
  if (state?.back) router.back();
  else void router.push('/');
}
</script>

<template>
  <header
    class="sticky top-0 z-30 backdrop-blur-xl backdrop-saturate-150 safe-top"
    :style="{ background: 'var(--bar-bg)' }"
  >
    <div class="mx-auto flex h-14 w-full max-w-[560px] items-center gap-2 px-4">
      <button
        v-if="props.showBack"
        type="button"
        class="-ml-2 grid h-10 w-10 place-items-center rounded-full text-muted tap hover:bg-surface-alt hover:text-fg active:scale-95"
        aria-label="back"
        @click="goBack"
      >
        <AppIcon name="back" :size="20" />
      </button>
      <div v-else class="ml-1 grid h-9 w-9 place-items-center rounded-[11px] border border-accent-strong bg-accent-soft text-accent">
        <svg width="20" height="20" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.8" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true">
          <path d="M12 2.9 20 7.5v9L12 21.1 4 16.5v-9z" />
          <path d="M12 7.8v3.6" />
          <circle cx="12" cy="14.7" r="1.5" fill="currentColor" stroke="none" />
        </svg>
      </div>

      <h1 class="min-w-0 flex-1 truncate px-1 text-[17px] font-semibold leading-6">
        {{ props.title }}
      </h1>

      <button
        type="button"
        class="-mr-2 grid h-10 w-10 place-items-center rounded-full text-muted tap hover:bg-surface-alt hover:text-fg active:scale-95"
        :aria-label="'preferences'"
        @click="preferencesOpen = true"
      >
        <AppIcon name="sliders" :size="20" />
      </button>
    </div>
  </header>

  <PreferencesSheet v-model:open="preferencesOpen" />
</template>

```

`webroot/src/components/layout/DeviceHeader.vue`:

```vue
<script setup lang="ts">
import { computed } from 'vue';

import type { Bootstrap } from '../../lib/api';
import { formatVersion } from '../../lib/format';
import { t } from '../../lib/i18n';
import AppBadge from '../ui/AppBadge.vue';
import AppIcon from '../ui/AppIcon.vue';

const props = defineProps<{ data: Bootstrap | null; loading: boolean }>();

const chips = computed(() => {
  const data = props.data;
  if (!data) return [];
  const items = [
    data.root.method ? `${data.root.method}` : '',
    data.device.soc_model || data.device.soc_manufacturer || '',
    data.device.abi || '',
    data.device.security_patch
      ? `${t('device.patch')} ${data.device.security_patch}`
      : '',
    data.system.selinux ? `SELinux · ${data.system.selinux}` : '',
    data.device.verified_boot ? `VB · ${data.device.verified_boot}` : '',
  ];
  return items.filter(Boolean);
});

const androidLine = computed(() => {
  const data = props.data;
  if (!data) return '';
  if (!data.device.android && !data.device.sdk) return '';
  return t('device.android', {
    release: data.device.android || '?',
    sdk: data.device.sdk || '?',
  });
});
</script>

<template>
  <section class="rounded-card border border-line bg-surface p-4 shadow-card">
    <div v-if="props.loading && !props.data" class="flex items-center gap-3">
      <span class="h-12 w-12 animate-pulse rounded-full bg-surface-alt" />
      <div class="flex-1 space-y-2">
        <span class="block h-5 w-2/3 animate-pulse rounded bg-surface-alt" />
        <span class="block h-3 w-1/2 animate-pulse rounded bg-surface-alt" />
      </div>
    </div>

    <template v-else-if="props.data">
      <div class="flex items-center gap-3">
        <span class="grid h-12 w-12 shrink-0 place-items-center rounded-full bg-surface-alt text-accent">
          <AppIcon name="shield" :size="24" />
        </span>
        <div class="min-w-0 flex-1">
          <p class="truncate text-[22px] font-semibold leading-7">
            {{ props.data.device.name || t('device.unknown') }}
          </p>
          <p class="truncate text-[13px] text-muted">{{ androidLine }}</p>
        </div>
        <AppBadge tone="neutral">{{ formatVersion(props.data.module.version) }}</AppBadge>
      </div>

      <div class="mt-3 flex flex-wrap gap-1.5">
        <span
          v-for="chip in chips"
          :key="chip"
          class="inline-flex h-7 max-w-full items-center gap-1 truncate rounded-full border border-line px-2.5 text-[11px] text-muted"
        >
          {{ chip }}
        </span>
      </div>
    </template>

    <p v-else class="py-4 text-center text-sm text-muted">{{ t('common.unavailable') }}</p>
  </section>
</template>

```

`webroot/src/components/layout/NavRow.vue`:

```vue
<script setup lang="ts">
import { useRouter } from 'vue-router';

import AppIcon from '../ui/AppIcon.vue';
import AppBadge from '../ui/AppBadge.vue';

const props = defineProps<{
  icon: string;
  title: string;
  description?: string;
  to: string;
  badge?: string;
  badgeTone?: 'neutral' | 'ok' | 'warn' | 'error' | 'accent';
  disabled?: boolean;
}>();

const router = useRouter();

function open() {
  if (props.disabled) return;
  void router.push(props.to);
}
</script>

<template>
  <button
    type="button"
    class="flex w-full items-center gap-3 px-4 py-3 text-left tap disabled:cursor-default disabled:opacity-50"
    :class="'hover:bg-sunken active:bg-surface-alt'"
    :disabled="props.disabled"
    @click="open"
  >
    <span class="grid h-10 w-10 shrink-0 place-items-center rounded-tile bg-accent-soft text-accent">
      <AppIcon :name="props.icon" :size="20" />
    </span>
    <span class="flex min-w-0 flex-1 flex-col gap-0.5">
      <span class="flex items-center gap-2">
        <span class="truncate text-[15px] font-semibold leading-5">{{ props.title }}</span>
        <AppBadge v-if="props.badge" :tone="props.badgeTone ?? 'neutral'">{{ props.badge }}</AppBadge>
      </span>
      <span v-if="props.description" class="line-clamp-2 text-xs leading-relaxed text-muted">
        {{ props.description }}
      </span>
    </span>
    <AppIcon name="chevron" :size="16" class="text-faint" />
  </button>
</template>

```

`webroot/src/components/layout/PreferencesSheet.vue`:

```vue
<script setup lang="ts">
import { computed, ref, watch } from 'vue';

import AppSheet from '../ui/AppSheet.vue';
import SegmentedControl from '../ui/SegmentedControl.vue';
import { i18nState, setLanguage, t, type LangChoice } from '../../lib/i18n';

const props = defineProps<{ open: boolean }>();
const emit = defineEmits<{ (event: 'update:open', value: boolean): void }>();

function readMode(): string {
  try {
    const saved = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('tf-theme') || 'null');
    if (saved?.mode === 'light' || saved?.mode === 'dark') return saved.mode;
  } catch {
    /* ignore malformed value */
  }
  return document.documentElement.getAttribute('data-mode') || 'auto';
}

function readLang(): LangChoice {
  const saved = localStorage.getItem('tf-lang');
  return saved === 'en' || saved === 'zh' || saved === 'ja' ? saved : 'auto';
}

const mode = ref(readMode());
const lang = ref<LangChoice>(readLang());

watch(
  () => props.open,
  (open) => {
    if (!open) return;
    mode.value = readMode();
    lang.value = readLang();
  },
);

function setMode(value: string) {
  mode.value = value;
  document.documentElement.setAttribute('data-mode', value);
  localStorage.setItem('tf-theme', JSON.stringify({ theme: 'material', mode: value }));
}

function setLang(value: string) {
  lang.value = value as LangChoice;
  void setLanguage(lang.value);
}

const appearanceOptions = computed(() => [
  { value: 'auto', label: t('prefs.auto') },
  { value: 'light', label: t('prefs.light') },
  { value: 'dark', label: t('prefs.dark') },
]);

const languageOptions = computed(() => [
  { value: 'auto', label: t('prefs.auto') },
  { value: 'zh', label: '中文' },
  { value: 'en', label: 'EN' },
  { value: 'ja', label: '日本語' },
]);

const detected = computed(() => i18nState.lang.toUpperCase());
</script>

<template>
  <AppSheet
    :open="props.open"
    :title="t('prefs.title')"
    :subtitle="t('prefs.subtitle')"
    @update:open="emit('update:open', $event)"
  >
    <div class="flex flex-col gap-5 pt-1">
      <div class="flex flex-col gap-2">
        <span class="text-[11px] font-bold uppercase tracking-[0.09em] text-muted">
          {{ t('prefs.appearance') }}
        </span>
        <SegmentedControl
          :model-value="mode"
          :options="appearanceOptions"
          :label="t('prefs.appearance')"
          @update:model-value="setMode"
        />
      </div>

      <div class="flex flex-col gap-2">
        <span class="text-[11px] font-bold uppercase tracking-[0.09em] text-muted">
          {{ t('prefs.language') }}
        </span>
        <SegmentedControl
          :model-value="lang"
          :options="languageOptions"
          :label="t('prefs.language')"
          @update:model-value="setLang"
        />
        <p class="text-[11px] text-faint">{{ t('prefs.detected', { lang: detected }) }}</p>
      </div>
    </div>

    <template #footer>
      <div class="flex items-center justify-between gap-3">
        <span class="text-xs text-faint">{{ t('prefs.persist') }}</span>
        <button
          type="button"
          class="inline-flex h-10 items-center rounded-full bg-surface-alt px-4 text-sm font-semibold tap hover:bg-line"
          @click="emit('update:open', false)"
        >
          {{ t('prefs.done') }}
        </button>
      </div>

`webroot/src/components/ui/AppBusyOverlay.vue`:

```vue
<script setup lang="ts">
import { onBeforeUnmount, ref, watch } from 'vue';

import { t } from '../../lib/i18n';

const props = withDefaults(defineProps<{ open: boolean; label?: string }>(), { label: '' });

/**
 * 等待浮层 [Busy overlay]
 *
 * 只在命令确实要跑一会儿时出现：350ms 内结束的操作不弹，避免闪一下反而更像卡顿。
 * 计时器每秒递增，是"还活着"的最直接证据；纯转圈看不出新进展。
 * The elapsed counter is the actual liveness signal; a bare spinner is not.
 */
const SHOW_DELAY = 350;

const visible = ref(false);
const seconds = ref(0);
let showTimer: number | undefined;
let tickTimer: number | undefined;

function clearTimers() {
  if (showTimer !== undefined) window.clearTimeout(showTimer);
  if (tickTimer !== undefined) window.clearInterval(tickTimer);
  showTimer = undefined;
  tickTimer = undefined;
}

watch(
  () => props.open,
  (open) => {
    clearTimers();
    if (!open) {
      visible.value = false;
      return;
    }
    seconds.value = 0;
    showTimer = window.setTimeout(() => {
      visible.value = true;
      tickTimer = window.setInterval(() => {
        seconds.value += 1;
      }, 1000);
    }, SHOW_DELAY);
  },
  { immediate: true },
);

onBeforeUnmount(clearTimers);
</script>

<template>
  <Teleport to="body">
    <transition name="busy">
      <div
        v-if="visible"
        class="fixed inset-0 z-[60] flex items-center justify-center bg-black/30 px-6"
        role="status"
        aria-live="polite"
      >
        <div class="w-full max-w-[300px] rounded-card bg-surface px-6 py-6 text-center shadow-sheet">
          <span
            class="mx-auto block h-9 w-9 animate-spin rounded-full border-[3px] border-accent-soft border-t-accent"
            aria-hidden="true"
          />
          <p class="mt-4 text-sm font-semibold leading-relaxed">
            {{ props.label || t('common.working') }}
          </p>
          <p class="mt-1 font-mono text-[11px] text-faint">
            {{ t('common.busy.elapsed', { n: seconds }) }}
          </p>
          <p class="mt-3 text-[11px] leading-relaxed text-muted">{{ t('common.busy.hint') }}</p>
        </div>
      </div>
    </transition>
  </Teleport>
</template>

```

`webroot/src/components/ui/AppSheet.vue`:

```vue
<script setup lang="ts">
import { onBeforeUnmount, onMounted, ref, watch } from 'vue';

import AppIcon from './AppIcon.vue';

const props = defineProps<{ open: boolean; title?: string; subtitle?: string }>();
const emit = defineEmits<{ (event: 'update:open', value: boolean): void }>();

const panel = ref<HTMLElement | null>(null);

function close() {
  emit('update:open', false);
}

function onKeydown(event: KeyboardEvent) {
  if (event.key === 'Escape' && props.open) close();
}

watch(
  () => props.open,
  (open) => {
    document.body.style.overflow = open ? 'hidden' : '';
    if (open) panel.value?.focus();
  },
);

onMounted(() => window.addEventListener('keydown', onKeydown));
onBeforeUnmount(() => {
  window.removeEventListener('keydown', onKeydown);
  document.body.style.overflow = '';
});
</script>

<template>
  <Teleport to="body">
    <transition name="sheet">
      <div
        v-if="props.open"
        class="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-end justify-center bg-black/45 sm:items-center"
        @click.self="close"
      >
        <div
          ref="panel"
          tabindex="-1"
          role="dialog"
          aria-modal="true"
          class="sheet-panel flex w-full max-w-[560px] flex-col overflow-hidden rounded-t-sheet bg-surface shadow-sheet outline-none sm:rounded-sheet"
        >
          <div class="mx-auto mt-2.5 h-1 w-9 rounded-full bg-line-strong sm:hidden" aria-hidden="true" />
          <header class="flex items-start gap-3 px-5 pb-2 pt-3">
            <div class="min-w-0 flex-1">
              <h2 class="text-[17px] font-bold leading-6">{{ props.title }}</h2>
              <p v-if="props.subtitle" class="mt-0.5 truncate font-mono text-xs text-faint">
                {{ props.subtitle }}
              </p>
            </div>
            <button
              type="button"
              class="-mr-1 grid h-9 w-9 place-items-center rounded-full text-muted tap hover:bg-surface-alt hover:text-fg"
              aria-label="close"
              @click="close"
            >
              <AppIcon name="close" :size="18" />
            </button>
          </header>

          <div class="min-h-0 flex-1 overflow-y-auto px-5 pb-4">
            <slot />
          </div>

          <footer v-if="$slots.footer" class="border-t border-line px-5 pt-3 safe-bottom">
            <slot name="footer" />
          </footer>
        </div>
      </div>

`webroot/src/lib/api.ts`:

```ts
/**
 * 命令封装 [Command wrappers]
 *
 * 所有后端调用都走 `--json`，统一解析 `{ok,data}` / `{ok,error}`。
 * 前端不拼业务逻辑，只做参数与错误搬运。
 */

import {
  CONFIG,
  SELECTION_FILE,
  TEEFORGE,
  exec,
  hasHost,
  spawn,
} from './ksu';

export interface ApiErrorPayload {
  stage?: string;
  code?: string;
  message?: string;
  hint?: string;
  raw?: unknown;
}

export class ApiError extends Error {
  stage?: string;
  code?: string;
  hint?: string;
  raw?: unknown;

  constructor(payload: ApiErrorPayload, fallback = 'command failed') {
    super(payload.message || fallback);
    this.name = 'ApiError';
    this.stage = payload.stage;
    this.code = payload.code;
    this.hint = payload.hint;
    this.raw = payload.raw;
  }
}

interface Envelope<T> {
  ok: boolean;
  data?: T;
  error?: ApiErrorPayload;
}

function command(args: string): string {
  return `${TEEFORGE} --config ${CONFIG} ${args} --json`;
}

function parseEnvelope<T>(stdout: string, exitCode: number): T {
  const text = stdout.trim();
  const start = text.indexOf('{');
  const end = text.lastIndexOf('}');
  if (start < 0 || end <= start) {
    throw new ApiError(
      { message: text || `命令无输出 [no output], exit ${exitCode}` },
      'invalid response',
    );
  }
  let envelope: Envelope<T>;
  try {
    envelope = JSON.parse(text.slice(start, end + 1)) as Envelope<T>;
  } catch {
    throw new ApiError({ message: text.slice(0, 400) }, 'invalid JSON');
  }
  if (!envelope.ok) {
    throw new ApiError(envelope.error ?? { message: 'unknown error' });
  }
  return envelope.data as T;
}

/** 一次性执行，等待结果。 */
export async function call<T>(args: string): Promise<T> {
  if (!hasHost()) throw new ApiError({ message: 'bridge-unavailable' });
  const result = await exec(command(args), JSON.stringify({ cwd: '/data/adb/modules/teeforge_cd' }));
  return parseEnvelope<T>(result.stdout, result.code);
}

/**
 * 流式执行：stderr 是进度日志，stdout 最后输出 JSON 信封。
 * 宿主没有 spawn 时退回一次性执行（没有实时日志，但结果一致）。
 */
export async function callStream<T>(
  args: string,
  onLog?: (chunk: string) => void,
): Promise<T> {
  if (!hasHost()) throw new ApiError({ message: 'bridge-unavailable' });
  const child = spawn(command(args));
  if (!child) return call<T>(args);

  return new Promise<T>((resolve, reject) => {
    let stdout = '';
    child.stdout.on('data', (chunk) => {
      stdout += chunk;
    });
    child.stderr.on('data', (chunk) => {
      onLog?.(chunk);
    });
    child.on('exit', (code) => {
      try {
        resolve(parseEnvelope<T>(stdout, code));
      } catch (error) {
        reject(error);
      }
    });
    child.on('error', (error) => {
      reject(new ApiError({ message: String(error) }));
    });
  });
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------- 数据类型

export interface Bootstrap {
  device: {
    brand: string;
    model: string;
    name: string;
    android: string;
    sdk: string;
    security_patch: string;
    abi: string;
    soc_model: string;
    soc_manufacturer: string;
    verified_boot: string;
  };
  system: { kernel: string; selinux: string };
  module: { version: string; version_code: number };
  root: { method: string; version: string };
  config: { keybox_source: 'remote' | 'rkp'; keybox_remote_slot: 1 | 2 | 3; target_mode: 'auto' | 'manual'; update_channel: string };
  capabilities: { rkp_platform: boolean; rkp_supported: boolean; rkp_artifacts: boolean };
  paths: { rkp_dir: string; keybox_dir: string };
}

export interface TargetList {
  mode: 'auto' | 'manual';
  backends: string[];
  packages: { name: string; uids: number[]; selected: boolean }[];
  counts: { installed: number; selected: number };
}

export interface ApplyResult {
  mode: string;
  selected: number;
  outcome: { state: string; packages?: number; backends?: number };
}

export type RkpState = 'Qualcomm' | 'Other' | 'Unknown' | 'Conflicting';

export interface RkpStatus {
  supported: boolean;
  platform: { os: string; arch: string; arm64_android: boolean };
  root: { method: string; version: string };
  soc: { state: RkpState; manufacturer: string; model: string; evidence: string[] };
  library: { path: string; present: boolean };
  device: { path: string; present: boolean };
  fingerprint: string;
  server: string;
  blockers: { id: string; detail: string }[];
  artifacts: { exists: boolean; dir: string; meta: Record<string, unknown> | null };
}

export interface RkpArtifacts {
  exists: boolean;
  dir: string;
  keybox: { path: string; size: number; mtime: number | null } | null;
  chain: { path: string; size: number; mtime: number | null } | null;
  meta_file: { path: string; size: number; mtime: number | null } | null;
  meta: Record<string, unknown> | null;
}

export interface UpdateInfo {
  channel: string;
  endpoint: string;
  current: { version: string; version_code: number };
  latest: {
    version: string;
    version_code: number;
    zip_url: string;
    changelog_url: string | null;
    sha256: string | null;
    size: number | null;
  };
  available: boolean;
}

export interface UpdateInstall {
  installed: boolean;
  reason?: string;
  version?: string;
  installer?: string;
  needs_reboot?: boolean;
  saved_to?: string;
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------- 具体命令

export const api = {
  bootstrap: () => call<Bootstrap>('--bootstrap'),

  targetList: () => call<TargetList>('--target-list'),
  targetMode: (mode: 'auto' | 'manual') => call<ApplyResult>(`--target-mode ${mode}`),

  rkpStatus: () => call<RkpStatus>('--rkp-status'),
  rkpIssue: (onLog?: (chunk: string) => void) => callStream<Record<string, unknown>>('--rkp-issue', onLog),
  rkpArtifacts: () => call<RkpArtifacts>('--rkp-artifacts'),
  rkpDelete: () => call<{ deleted: boolean; freed_bytes: number }>('--rkp-delete'),

  keybox: (source: 'remote' | 'rkp', slot: 1 | 2 | 3, force: boolean, onLog?: (chunk: string) => void) =>
    callStream<{ source: string; remote_slot: 1 | 2 | 3 | null; targets: { path: string; changed: boolean }[] }>(
      `--keybox --source ${source} --save-selection${source === 'remote' ? ` --remote-slot ${slot}${force ? ' --force' : ''}` : ''}`,
      onLog,
    ),

  updateChannel: (channel: 'stable' | 'dev') =>
    call<{ channel: string }>(`--update-channel ${channel}`),
  updateCheck: () => callStream<UpdateInfo>('--self-update check'),
  updateInstall: (onLog?: (chunk: string) => void) =>
    callStream<UpdateInstall>('--self-update install', onLog),

  /**
   * 手动选择集合：先写文件再让后端读取，避免把几百个包名塞进命令行。
   * The selection is written as a file so argument length never becomes a limit.
   */
  async targetApply(packages: string[]): Promise<ApplyResult> {
    const payload = JSON.stringify({ version: 1, packages }, null, 0);
    const encoded = btoa(unescape(encodeURIComponent(payload)));
    const write = await exec(
      `printf '%s' '${encoded}' | base64 -d > '${SELECTION_FILE}'`,
      '{}',
    );
    if (write.code !== 0) {
      throw new ApiError({ message: write.stderr || '无法写入选择文件 [cannot write selection]' });
    }
    return call<ApplyResult>(`--target-apply ${SELECTION_FILE}`);
  },
};

```

`webroot/src/lib/clipboard.ts`:

```ts
/** 复制到剪贴板，兼容没有 Clipboard API 的 WebView。 */
export async function copyText(text: string): Promise<boolean> {
  try {
    if (navigator.clipboard?.writeText) {
      await navigator.clipboard.writeText(text);
      return true;
    }
  } catch {
    /* fall through to the legacy path */
  }
  try {
    const area = document.createElement('textarea');
    area.value = text;
    area.setAttribute('readonly', 'readonly');
    area.style.position = 'fixed';
    area.style.opacity = '0';
    document.body.appendChild(area);
    area.select();
    const ok = document.execCommand('copy');
    document.body.removeChild(area);
    return ok;
  } catch {
    return false;
  }
}

```

`webroot/src/lib/format.ts`:

```ts
/** 展示层格式化 [Presentation helpers] */

export function formatBytes(bytes: number | null | undefined): string {
  if (!bytes || bytes <= 0) return '0 B';
  if (bytes < 1024) return `${bytes} B`;
  if (bytes < 1024 * 1024) return `${(bytes / 1024).toFixed(1)} KB`;
  return `${(bytes / 1024 / 1024).toFixed(2)} MB`;
}

export function formatUnix(seconds: number | null | undefined): string {
  if (!seconds) return '—';
  const date = new Date(seconds * 1000);
  if (Number.isNaN(date.getTime())) return '—';
  return date.toLocaleString();
}

/**
 * 统一版本展示：剥掉已有的 v/V 前缀。
 *
 * `module.prop` 写的是 `0.6.0`，CDN 清单写的是 `v0.6.0`，调用点若再自行拼 `v`
 * 就会出现 `vv0.6.0` 或同一屏 `0.6.0` 与 `v0.6.0` 并存。归一化只发生在展示层，
 * 两个来源文件都不改：前者是模块规范字段，后者被 root 管理器直接消费。
 */
export function formatVersion(raw: string | null | undefined): string {
  if (!raw) return '—';
  return raw.trim().replace(/^[vV]/, '');
}

export function shortFingerprint(value: string, keep = 12): string {
  if (!value) return '—';
  if (value.length <= keep * 2) return value;
  return `${value.slice(0, keep)}…${value.slice(-keep)}`;
}

export function stageLabel(stage?: string): string {
  switch (stage) {
    case 'prerequisite':
      return 'prerequisite';
    case 'transport':
      return 'transport';
    case 'frame':
      return 'frame';
    case 'ta':
      return 'ta';
    case 'server':
      return 'server';
    case 'keybox':
      return 'keybox';
    case 'target':
      return 'target';
    case 'update':
      return 'update';
    default:
      return stage || 'unknown';
  }
}

```

`webroot/src/lib/i18n.ts`:

```ts
/**
 * 轻量 i18n [Minimal i18n]
 *
 * 字典放在 public/i18n/{lang}.json，运行时按需加载；不引入 vue-i18n。
 * 缺少键时直接返回键名，便于发现漏翻。
 */

import { reactive } from 'vue';

export type Lang = 'zh' | 'en' | 'ja';
export type LangChoice = 'auto' | Lang;

const LANGS: Lang[] = ['zh', 'en', 'ja'];
const state = reactive({
  lang: 'zh' as Lang,
  dict: {} as Record<string, string>,
  ready: false,
});

const cache: Partial<Record<Lang, Record<string, string>>> = {};

export function detectLang(): Lang {
  const saved = localStorage.getItem('tf-lang');
  if (saved === 'en' || saved === 'zh' || saved === 'ja') return saved;
  const nav = (navigator.language || 'en').toLowerCase();
  if (nav.startsWith('zh')) return 'zh';
  if (nav.startsWith('ja')) return 'ja';
  return 'en';
}

export function savedLang(): LangChoice {
  const saved = localStorage.getItem('tf-lang');
  return saved === 'en' || saved === 'zh' || saved === 'ja' ? saved : 'auto';
}

async function load(lang: Lang): Promise<Record<string, string>> {
  if (cache[lang]) return cache[lang]!;
  try {
    const response = await fetch(`./i18n/${lang}.json`);
    if (!response.ok) throw new Error(String(response.status));
    const dict = (await response.json()) as Record<string, string>;
    cache[lang] = dict;
    return dict;
  } catch {
    return {};
  }
}

export async function setLanguage(choice: LangChoice): Promise<void> {
  if (choice === 'auto') localStorage.removeItem('tf-lang');
  else localStorage.setItem('tf-lang', choice);
  await apply();
}

export async function initI18n(): Promise<void> {
  await apply();
}

async function apply(): Promise<void> {
  const lang = detectLang();
  let dict = await load(lang);
  // 字典缺失时退回英文，避免整页显示键名。
  if (Object.keys(dict).length === 0 && lang !== 'en') dict = await load('en');
  state.lang = lang;
  state.dict = dict;
  state.ready = true;
  document.documentElement.lang = lang === 'zh' ? 'zh-CN' : lang;
}

export function currentLang(): Lang {
  return state.lang;
}

export function hasKey(key: string): boolean {
  return key in state.dict;
}

export function t(key: string, params?: Record<string, string | number>): string {
  let text = state.dict[key] ?? key;
  if (params) {
    for (const [name, value] of Object.entries(params)) {
      text = text.replace(new RegExp(`\\{${name}\\}`, 'g'), String(value));
    }
  }
  return text;
}

export { LANGS, state as i18nState };

```

`webroot/src/lib/ksu.ts`:

```ts
/**
 * KernelSU WebUI 桥接层 [KernelSU WebUI bridge]
 *
 * 只暴露最小能力：执行命令、流式执行、读取应用信息、轻提示。
 * 所有调用都先判断宿主是否存在，避免在普通浏览器里抛异常。
 */

export const MODDIR = '/data/adb/modules/teeforge_cd';
export const DATA_DIR = '/data/adb/teeforge';
export const CONFIG = DATA_DIR + '/config.conf';
export const TEEFORGE = MODDIR + '/teeforge';
export const SELECTION_FILE = DATA_DIR + '/target-selection.json';

export interface ExecResult {
  code: number;
  stdout: string;
  stderr: string;
}

interface KsuHost {
  exec(command: string, options: string, callback: string): void;
  spawn?(command: string, options?: { cwd?: string }): SpawnChild;
  toast?(message: string): void;
  listPackages?(type: string): string[];
  getPackagesInfo?(packages: string[]): { packageName: string; appLabel: string }[];
}

export interface SpawnChild {
  stdout: { on(event: 'data', cb: (data: string) => void): void };
  stderr: { on(event: 'data', cb: (data: string) => void): void };
  on(event: 'exit', cb: (code: number) => void): void;
  on(event: 'error', cb: (error: unknown) => void): void;
}

function host(): KsuHost | null {
  const candidate = (window as unknown as { ksu?: KsuHost }).ksu;
  return candidate && typeof candidate.exec === 'function' ? candidate : null;
}

export function hasHost(): boolean {
  return host() !== null;
}

export function canSpawn(): boolean {
  return typeof host()?.spawn === 'function';
}

export function exec(command: string, options = '{}'): Promise<ExecResult> {
  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    const bridge = host();
    if (!bridge) {
      reject(new Error('KernelSU bridge unavailable'));
      return;
    }
    const callback = `tf_cb_${Date.now()}_${Math.floor(Math.random() * 10000)}`;
    (window as unknown as Record<string, unknown>)[callback] = (
      code: number,
      stdout: string,
      stderr: string,
    ) => {
      delete (window as unknown as Record<string, unknown>)[callback];
      resolve({ code, stdout: stdout || '', stderr: stderr || '' });
    };
    bridge.exec(command, options, callback);
  });
}

export function spawn(command: string): SpawnChild | null {
  const bridge = host();
  if (!bridge || typeof bridge.spawn !== 'function') return null;
  try {
    return bridge.spawn(command, { cwd: MODDIR });
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
}

export function toast(message: string): void {
  host()?.toast?.(message);
}

/** 应用名与图标的可选增强；接口不存在时由调用方回退到包名。 */
export async function appLabels(packages: string[]): Promise<Record<string, string>> {
  const bridge = host();
  if (!bridge?.getPackagesInfo || packages.length === 0) return {};
  try {
    const info = bridge.getPackagesInfo(packages);
    const map: Record<string, string> = {};
    for (const item of info) {
      if (item.appLabel) map[item.packageName] = item.appLabel;
    }
    return map;
  } catch {
    return {};
  }
}

export function iconUrl(packageName: string): string {
  return `ksu://icon/${packageName}`;
}

```

`webroot/src/main.ts`:

```ts
import { createApp } from 'vue';

import './styles/tailwind.css';
import App from './App.vue';
import { router } from './router';
import { initI18n } from './lib/i18n';
import { loadSession } from './state/session';

// 字典加载完成后再挂载，避免首帧显示键名。
initI18n().finally(() => {
  const app = createApp(App);
  // 渲染异常时留下可读标记，避免整页空白没有任何线索。
  app.config.errorHandler = (error) => {
    const message = error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error);
    console.error(message);
    document.getElementById('app')?.setAttribute('data-render-error', message);
  };
  app.use(router).mount('#app');
  void loadSession();
});

`webroot/src/pages/KeyboxPage.vue`:

<script setup lang="ts">
import { computed, onMounted, ref } from 'vue';
import { useRouter } from 'vue-router';

import AppButton from '../components/ui/AppButton.vue';
import AppBusyOverlay from '../components/ui/AppBusyOverlay.vue';
import AppIcon from '../components/ui/AppIcon.vue';
import AppSheet from '../components/ui/AppSheet.vue';
import SegmentedControl from '../components/ui/SegmentedControl.vue';
import SectionCard from '../components/layout/SectionCard.vue';
import { api } from '../lib/api';
import { t } from '../lib/i18n';
import { loadSession, session } from '../state/session';

const router = useRouter();
const source = ref<'rkp' | 'remote'>('remote');
const remoteSlot = ref<'1' | '2' | '3'>('1');
const force = ref(false);
const slotOptions = computed(() => ['1', '2', '3'].map(value => ({ value, label: t('keybox.slot.option', { n: value }), disabled: busy.value })));
function selectSlot(value: string) {
  if (!busy.value && (value === '1' || value === '2' || value === '3')) remoteSlot.value = value;
}
function confirm(rewrite: boolean) {
  if (busy.value) return;
  force.value = rewrite;
  confirmOpen.value = true;
}
const artifacts = ref(false);
const busy = ref(false);
const error = ref('');
const log = ref('');
const result = ref('');
const confirmOpen = ref(false);

const rkpAvailable = computed(() => artifacts.value);
const canApply = computed(() => source.value === 'remote' || rkpAvailable.value);

async function refresh() {
  try {
    const info = await api.rkpArtifacts();
    artifacts.value = info.exists;
  } catch {
    artifacts.value = false;
  }
}

onMounted(async () => {
  await loadSession();
  source.value = session.data?.config.keybox_source ?? 'remote';
  remoteSlot.value = String(session.data?.config.keybox_remote_slot ?? 1) as '1' | '2' | '3';
  await refresh();
});

function choose(next: 'rkp' | 'remote') {
  if (busy.value) return;
  if (next === 'rkp' && !rkpAvailable.value) return;
  source.value = next;
}

async function apply() {
  if (busy.value) return;
  busy.value = true;
  error.value = '';
  result.value = '';
  log.value = '';
  try {
    const report = await api.keybox(source.value, Number(remoteSlot.value) as 1 | 2 | 3, force.value, (chunk) => {
      log.value += chunk;
    });
    result.value = t('keybox.result', { n: report.targets.filter(target => target.changed).length });
    confirmOpen.value = false;
    await loadSession(true);
  } catch (failure) {
    error.value = failure instanceof Error ? failure.message : String(failure);
    confirmOpen.value = false;
  } finally {
    busy.value = false;
  }
}
</script>

<template>
  <div class="flex flex-col gap-5 pt-1">
    <p class="px-1 text-xs leading-relaxed text-muted">{{ t('keybox.intro') }}</p>

    <div class="flex flex-col gap-2.5">
      <div
        role="button"
        :tabindex="busy ? -1 : 0"
        class="rounded-card border p-4 text-left tap"
        :class="[
          source === 'rkp'
            ? 'border-accent bg-accent-soft'
            : 'border-line bg-surface hover:border-line-strong',
          rkpAvailable ? 'cursor-pointer' : 'cursor-default opacity-70',
        ]"
        :aria-pressed="source === 'rkp'"
        :aria-disabled="busy || !rkpAvailable"
        @click="choose('rkp')"
        @keydown.enter.prevent="choose('rkp')"
        @keydown.space.prevent="choose('rkp')"
      >
        <div class="flex items-start gap-3">

            class="mt-1 grid h-4 w-4 shrink-0 place-items-center rounded-full border-2"

            ? 'border-accent bg-accent-soft'

        </div>

  </div>
</template>

`webroot/src/pages/RkpPage.vue`:

```vue
<script setup lang="ts">
import { computed, onMounted, ref } from 'vue';

import SectionCard from '../components/layout/SectionCard.vue';
import AppBadge from '../components/ui/AppBadge.vue';
import AppBusyOverlay from '../components/ui/AppBusyOverlay.vue';
import AppButton from '../components/ui/AppButton.vue';
import AppIcon from '../components/ui/AppIcon.vue';
import AppSheet from '../components/ui/AppSheet.vue';
import { ApiError, api, type RkpArtifacts, type RkpStatus } from '../lib/api';
import { formatBytes, formatUnix, shortFingerprint } from '../lib/format';
import { hasKey, t } from '../lib/i18n';
import { loadSession } from '../state/session';

const status = ref<RkpStatus | null>(null);
const artifacts = ref<RkpArtifacts | null>(null);
const loading = ref(true);
const busy = ref(false);
const log = ref('');
const failure = ref<ApiError | null>(null);
const issueError = ref<ApiError | null>(null);
const confirmOpen = ref(false);
const deleteOpen = ref(false);
const succeeded = ref('');
const busyLabel = ref('');

const supported = computed(() => status.value?.supported ?? false);

const rows = computed(() => {
  const data = status.value;
  if (!data) return [];
  const yes = t('common.yes');
  const no = t('common.no');
  return [
    { label: t('rkp.row.platform'), value: `${data.platform.os}/${data.platform.arch}` },
    { label: t('rkp.row.root'), value: `${data.root.method} ${data.root.version}`.trim() },
    {
      label: t('rkp.row.soc'),
      value: [data.soc.manufacturer, data.soc.model].filter(Boolean).join(' ') || t('common.unknown'),
    },
    { label: t('rkp.row.socState'), value: data.soc.state },
    { label: t('rkp.row.library'), value: data.library.present ? data.library.path : no, mono: true },
    { label: t('rkp.row.smcinvoke'), value: data.device.present ? data.device.path : no, mono: true },
    { label: t('rkp.row.fingerprint'), value: data.fingerprint || t('common.unknown'), mono: true },
  ];
});

const meta = computed(() => artifacts.value?.meta ?? null);
const createdAt = computed(() => formatUnix(Number(meta.value?.created_at ?? 0)));
const certificates = computed(() => Number(meta.value?.certificates ?? 0));
const deviceId = computed(() => String(meta.value?.device_id ?? ''));

const explain = computed(() => {
  // hint 是后端给出的解释入口，优先于错误码；帧类错误没有数字码，只能走 hint。
  const hint = issueError.value?.hint;
  if (hint && hasKey(hint)) return t(hint);
  const code = issueError.value?.code;
  if (!code) return '';
  const key = `rkp.code.${code}`;
  return hasKey(key) ? t(key) : '';
});

function blockerText(id: string, detail: string): string {
  const key = `rkp.blocker.${id}`;
  return hasKey(key) ? t(key) : detail;
}

function toApiError(error: unknown): ApiError {
  if (error instanceof ApiError) return error;
  return new ApiError({ message: error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error) });
}

async function load() {
  loading.value = true;
  failure.value = null;
  try {
    status.value = await api.rkpStatus();
    artifacts.value = await api.rkpArtifacts();
  } catch (error) {
    failure.value = toApiError(error);
  } finally {
    loading.value = false;
  }
}

async function issue() {
  busy.value = true;
  busyLabel.value = t('common.busy.rkp');
  log.value = '';
  issueError.value = null;
  succeeded.value = '';
  confirmOpen.value = false;
  try {
    await api.rkpIssue((chunk) => {
      log.value += chunk;
    });
    succeeded.value = t('rkp.issue.done');
    await load();
    await loadSession(true);
  } catch (error) {
    issueError.value = toApiError(error);
  } finally {
    busy.value = false;
  }
}

async function removeArtifacts() {
  busy.value = true;
  busyLabel.value = '';
  try {
    await api.rkpDelete();
    deleteOpen.value = false;
    succeeded.value = '';
    await load();
    await loadSession(true);
  } catch (error) {
    failure.value = toApiError(error);
  } finally {
    busy.value = false;
  }
}

onMounted(load);
</script>

<template>
  <div class="flex flex-col gap-5 pt-1">
    <div class="flex items-start gap-2 rounded-card bg-warning-soft px-4 py-3 text-[11px] leading-relaxed text-warning">
      <AppIcon name="alert" :size="15" class="mt-0.5" />
      <span>{{ t('rkp.warning') }}</span>
    </div>

    <div v-if="loading" class="flex items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-card border border-line bg-surface py-10 text-xs text-muted">
      <span class="h-5 w-5 animate-spin rounded-full border-2 border-accent border-t-transparent" />
      {{ t('common.loading') }}
    </div>

    <template v-else-if="status">
      <SectionCard :title="t('rkp.section.status')">
        <div class="flex items-center justify-between gap-3">
          <AppBadge :tone="supported ? 'ok' : 'error'">
            {{ supported ? t('rkp.met') : t('rkp.unmet') }}
          </AppBadge>
          <button type="button" class="text-xs font-medium text-accent" @click="load">
            {{ t('common.refresh') }}
          </button>
        </div>

        <ul v-if="status.blockers.length" class="mt-3 flex flex-col gap-1.5 rounded-tile bg-danger-soft p-3">
          <li v-for="blocker in status.blockers" :key="blocker.id" class="flex gap-2 text-[11px] leading-relaxed text-danger">
            <span class="mt-1 h-1.5 w-1.5 shrink-0 rounded-full bg-danger" />
            {{ blockerText(blocker.id, blocker.detail) }}
          </li>
        </ul>

        <dl class="mt-3 divide-y divide-line">
          <div
            v-for="row in rows"
            :key="row.label"
            class="py-2 first:pt-0"
            :class="row.mono ? 'flex flex-col gap-1' : 'flex items-baseline justify-between gap-3'"
          >
            <dt class="shrink-0 text-[12px] text-muted">{{ row.label }}</dt>
            <dd
              class="min-w-0 break-all text-[12px] font-medium"
              :class="row.mono ? 'font-mono text-[11px] text-fg' : 'text-right'"
            >
              {{ row.value }}
            </dd>
          </div>
        </dl>

        <details v-if="status.soc.evidence.length" class="mt-3 border-t border-line pt-3">
          <summary class="cursor-pointer text-xs font-semibold text-muted">{{ t('rkp.evidence') }}</summary>
          <ul class="mt-2 flex flex-col gap-1">
            <li v-for="item in status.soc.evidence" :key="item" class="break-all font-mono text-[11px] text-faint">
              {{ item }}
            </li>
          </ul>
        </details>
      </SectionCard>

      <AppButton block size="lg" :disabled="!supported || busy" :loading="busy" @click="confirmOpen = true">
        {{ t('rkp.issue.button') }}
      </AppButton>

      <pre
        v-if="log"
        class="max-h-56 overflow-y-auto rounded-card border border-line bg-sunken p-3 font-mono text-[11px] leading-relaxed text-muted"
        >{{ log }}</pre
      >

      <p v-if="succeeded" class="rounded-card bg-accent-soft px-4 py-3 text-xs font-medium text-accent">
        {{ succeeded }}
      </p>

      <section v-if="issueError" class="rounded-card border border-[var(--danger-soft)] bg-surface p-4">
        <div class="flex items-center gap-2">
          <AppIcon name="alert" :size="16" class="text-danger" />
          <p class="text-sm font-semibold text-danger">{{ t('rkp.error.title') }}</p>
        </div>
        <p class="mt-2 text-[11px] font-mono text-faint">
          {{ t('rkp.error.stage') }}: {{ issueError.stage || 'unknown' }}
          <template v-if="issueError.code"> · {{ t('rkp.error.code') }}: {{ issueError.code }}</template>
        </p>
        <p class="mt-2 text-[12px] leading-relaxed text-fg">{{ issueError.message }}</p>
        <p v-if="explain" class="mt-2 rounded-tile bg-warning-soft px-3 py-2 text-[11px] leading-relaxed text-warning">
          {{ explain }}
        </p>
        <details v-if="issueError.raw" class="mt-3">
          <summary class="cursor-pointer text-[11px] font-semibold text-muted">{{ t('rkp.error.raw') }}</summary>
          <pre class="mt-2 overflow-x-auto rounded-tile bg-sunken p-2 font-mono text-[10px] leading-relaxed text-faint">{{ JSON.stringify(issueError.raw, null, 2) }}</pre>
        </details>
      </section>

      <p v-if="failure" class="rounded-card bg-danger-soft px-4 py-3 text-xs leading-relaxed text-danger">
        {{ failure.message }}
      </p>

      <SectionCard :title="t('rkp.section.artifacts')">
        <template v-if="artifacts?.exists">
          <dl class="divide-y divide-line">
            <div class="flex items-center justify-between gap-3 pb-2">
              <dt class="text-[12px] text-muted">{{ t('rkp.artifact.created') }}</dt>
              <dd class="text-[12px] font-medium">{{ createdAt }}</dd>
            </div>
            <div class="flex items-center justify-between gap-3 py-2">
              <dt class="text-[12px] text-muted">{{ t('rkp.artifact.certificates') }}</dt>
              <dd class="text-[12px] font-medium">{{ certificates }}</dd>
            </div>
            <div v-if="meta?.frame_layout" class="flex items-center justify-between gap-3 py-2">
              <dt class="text-[12px] text-muted">{{ t('rkp.frame.layout') }}</dt>
              <dd class="font-mono text-[11px]">{{ meta.frame_layout }}</dd>
            </div>
            <div class="flex items-center justify-between gap-3 py-2">
              <dt class="text-[12px] text-muted">{{ t('rkp.artifact.deviceId') }}</dt>
              <dd class="font-mono text-[11px]">{{ shortFingerprint(deviceId, 8) }}</dd>
            </div>
            <div class="flex items-center justify-between gap-3 py-2">
              <dt class="text-[12px] text-muted">{{ t('rkp.artifact.size') }}</dt>
              <dd class="text-[12px] font-medium">{{ formatBytes(artifacts.keybox?.size) }}</dd>
            </div>
            <div class="flex items-start justify-between gap-3 pt-2">
              <dt class="shrink-0 text-[12px] text-muted">{{ t('rkp.artifact.dir') }}</dt>
              <dd class="break-all text-right font-mono text-[11px] text-faint">{{ artifacts.dir }}</dd>
            </div>
          </dl>
          <AppButton variant="danger" block class="mt-4" :disabled="busy" @click="deleteOpen = true">
            <AppIcon name="trash" :size="16" />
            {{ t('rkp.delete.button') }}
          </AppButton>
        </template>
        <p v-else class="py-2 text-center text-xs text-muted">{{ t('rkp.artifact.empty') }}</p>
      </SectionCard>
    </template>

    <AppSheet v-model:open="confirmOpen" :title="t('rkp.confirm.title')">
      <p class="text-sm leading-relaxed text-muted">{{ t('rkp.confirm.body') }}</p>
      <template #footer>
        <div class="flex justify-end gap-2">
          <AppButton variant="ghost" @click="confirmOpen = false">{{ t('common.cancel') }}</AppButton>
          <AppButton :loading="busy" @click="issue">{{ t('common.confirm') }}</AppButton>
        </div>
      </template>
    </AppSheet>

    <AppSheet v-model:open="deleteOpen" :title="t('rkp.delete.title')">
      <p class="text-sm leading-relaxed text-muted">{{ t('rkp.delete.body') }}</p>
      <template #footer>
        <div class="flex justify-end gap-2">
          <AppButton variant="ghost" @click="deleteOpen = false">{{ t('common.cancel') }}</AppButton>
          <AppButton variant="danger" :loading="busy" @click="removeArtifacts">{{ t('common.delete') }}</AppButton>
        </div>
      </template>
    </AppSheet>

    <AppBusyOverlay :open="busy" :label="busyLabel" />

`webroot/src/pages/TargetPage.vue`:

```vue
<script setup lang="ts">
import { computed, onMounted, ref } from 'vue';

import SegmentedControl from '../components/ui/SegmentedControl.vue';
import AppBusyOverlay from '../components/ui/AppBusyOverlay.vue';
import AppButton from '../components/ui/AppButton.vue';
import AppIcon from '../components/ui/AppIcon.vue';
import AppSheet from '../components/ui/AppSheet.vue';
import EmptyState from '../components/ui/EmptyState.vue';
import { api, type TargetList } from '../lib/api';
import { t } from '../lib/i18n';
import { appLabels, iconUrl } from '../lib/ksu';
import { loadSession, session } from '../state/session';

const list = ref<TargetList | null>(null);
const labels = ref<Record<string, string>>({});
const loading = ref(false);
const busy = ref(false);
const error = ref('');
const query = ref('');
const selected = ref<Set<string>>(new Set());
const confirmOpen = ref(false);
const mode = ref<'auto' | 'manual'>('auto');

const filtered = computed(() => {
  const items = list.value?.packages ?? [];
  const keyword = query.value.trim().toLowerCase();
  if (!keyword) return items;
  return items.filter(
    (item) =>
      item.name.toLowerCase().includes(keyword) ||
      (labels.value[item.name] ?? '').toLowerCase().includes(keyword),
  );
});

const selectedCount = computed(() => selected.value.size);
const allSelected = computed(
  () => filtered.value.length > 0 && filtered.value.every((item) => selected.value.has(item.name)),
);

const backendNames = computed(() => {
  const map: Record<string, string> = {
    tricky: 'Tricky Store',
    teesim: 'TEESimulator',
    omk: 'Oh My Keymint',
  };
  return (list.value?.backends ?? []).map((name) => map[name] ?? name);
});

function hideIcon(event: Event) {
  const image = event.target as HTMLImageElement | null;
  if (image) image.style.display = 'none';
}

function toggle(name: string) {
  const next = new Set(selected.value);
  if (next.has(name)) next.delete(name);
  else next.add(name);
  selected.value = next;
}

function toggleAll() {
  const next = new Set(selected.value);
  if (allSelected.value) {
    for (const item of filtered.value) next.delete(item.name);
  } else {
    for (const item of filtered.value) next.add(item.name);
  }
  selected.value = next;
}

async function load() {
  loading.value = true;
  error.value = '';
  try {
    const data = await api.targetList();
    list.value = data;
    mode.value = data.mode;
    selected.value = new Set(data.packages.filter((item) => item.selected).map((item) => item.name));
    const names = data.packages.map((item) => item.name);
    labels.value = await appLabels(names);
  } catch (failure) {
    error.value = failure instanceof Error ? failure.message : String(failure);
  } finally {
    loading.value = false;
  }
}

async function switchMode(next: string) {
  if (next === mode.value || busy.value) return;
  busy.value = true;
  error.value = '';
  try {
    if (next === 'manual') {
      // 进入手动模式以"当前全量"为基线，避免空集合直接清空目标列表。
      const names = (list.value?.packages ?? []).map((item) => item.name);
      await api.targetApply(names);
      mode.value = 'manual';
      selected.value = new Set(names);
    } else {
      await api.targetMode('auto');
      mode.value = 'auto';
      selected.value = new Set((list.value?.packages ?? []).map((item) => item.name));
    }
    await loadSession(true);
  } catch (failure) {
    error.value = failure instanceof Error ? failure.message : String(failure);
  } finally {
    busy.value = false;
  }
}

async function apply() {
  busy.value = true;
  error.value = '';
  try {
    await api.targetApply([...selected.value]);
    confirmOpen.value = false;
    mode.value = 'manual';
    await loadSession(true);
    await load();
  } catch (failure) {
    error.value = failure instanceof Error ? failure.message : String(failure);
  } finally {
    busy.value = false;
  }
}

onMounted(load);
</script>

<template>
  <div class="flex flex-col gap-4 pt-1">
    <div class="flex flex-col gap-2">
      <SegmentedControl
        :model-value="mode"
        :label="t('target.mode.label')"
        :options="[
          { value: 'auto', label: t('target.mode.auto') },
          { value: 'manual', label: t('target.mode.manual') },
        ]"
        @update:model-value="switchMode"
      />
      <p class="px-1 text-[11px] leading-relaxed text-faint">
        {{ mode === 'auto' ? t('target.mode.autoHint') : t('target.mode.manualHint') }}
      </p>
    </div>

    <div class="flex items-center gap-2">
      <label class="relative flex h-10 flex-1 items-center gap-2 rounded-full border border-line bg-surface px-3">
        <AppIcon name="search" :size="16" class="text-faint" />
        <input
          v-model="query"
          type="search"
          :placeholder="t('target.search')"
          class="min-w-0 flex-1 bg-transparent text-sm outline-none placeholder:text-faint"
        />
      </label>
      <AppButton variant="tonal" size="sm" @click="toggleAll">
        {{ allSelected ? t('common.clear') : t('common.selectAll') }}
      </AppButton>
    </div>

    <div v-if="backendNames.length" class="flex flex-wrap gap-1.5 px-1">
      <span
        v-for="name in backendNames"
        :key="name"
        class="rounded-full bg-accent-soft px-2 py-0.5 text-[11px] font-medium text-accent"
      >
        {{ name }}
      </span>
    </div>

    <p v-if="error" class="rounded-card bg-danger-soft px-4 py-3 text-xs leading-relaxed text-danger">
      {{ error }}
    </p>

    <div
      v-if="loading"
      class="flex flex-col items-center gap-2 rounded-card border border-line bg-surface py-10 text-xs text-muted"
    >
      <span class="h-5 w-5 animate-spin rounded-full border-2 border-accent border-t-transparent" />
      {{ t('target.loading') }}
    </div>

    <EmptyState
      v-else-if="!filtered.length"
      icon="apps"
      :title="query ? t('target.noMatch') : t('target.empty')"
      :hint="query ? '' : t('target.emptyHint')"
    />

    <ul v-else class="divide-y divide-line overflow-hidden rounded-card border border-line bg-surface">
      <li v-for="item in filtered" :key="item.name">
        <label class="flex cursor-pointer items-center gap-3 px-3 py-2.5 tap hover:bg-sunken">
          <input
            type="checkbox"
            class="peer sr-only"
            :checked="selected.has(item.name)"
            @change="toggle(item.name)"
          />
          <span
            class="grid h-5 w-5 shrink-0 place-items-center rounded-md border-2 border-line-strong text-transparent peer-checked:border-accent peer-checked:bg-accent peer-checked:text-on-accent"
            aria-hidden="true"
          >
            <AppIcon name="check" :size="13" />
          </span>
          <span class="relative grid h-9 w-9 shrink-0 place-items-center overflow-hidden rounded-[10px] bg-surface-alt text-xs font-semibold text-muted">
            {{ item.name.slice(0, 1).toUpperCase() }}
            <img
              :src="iconUrl(item.name)"
              alt=""
              class="absolute inset-0 h-full w-full object-cover"
              loading="lazy"
              @error="hideIcon"
            />
          </span>
          <span class="min-w-0 flex-1">
            <span class="block truncate text-[14px] font-medium leading-5">
              {{ labels[item.name] || item.name }}
            </span>
            <span
              v-if="labels[item.name] && labels[item.name] !== item.name"
              class="block truncate font-mono text-[11px] text-faint"
            >
              {{ item.name }}
            </span>
          </span>
        </label>
      </li>
    </ul>

    <div
      class="sticky bottom-0 z-10 -mx-4 mt-1 flex items-center justify-between gap-3 border-t border-line px-4 pb-2 pt-3 backdrop-blur-xl"
      :style="{ background: 'var(--bar-bg)' }"
    >
      <span class="text-xs text-muted">
        {{ t('target.selected', { n: selectedCount }) }}
        <span class="text-faint">/ {{ list?.counts.installed ?? 0 }}</span>
      </span>
      <AppButton size="md" :disabled="mode !== 'manual' || busy" :loading="busy" @click="confirmOpen = true">
        {{ t('common.apply') }}
      </AppButton>
    </div>

    <AppSheet
      v-model:open="confirmOpen"
      :title="t('target.confirm.title')"
      :subtitle="t('target.selected', { n: selectedCount })"
    >
      <p class="text-sm leading-relaxed text-muted">{{ t('target.confirm.body') }}</p>
      <ul v-if="backendNames.length" class="mt-3 flex flex-col gap-1.5">
        <li v-for="name in backendNames" :key="name" class="flex items-center gap-2 text-xs text-muted">
          <AppIcon name="check" :size="14" class="text-accent" />
          {{ name }}
        </li>
      </ul>
      <template #footer>
        <div class="flex justify-end gap-2">
          <AppButton variant="ghost" @click="confirmOpen = false">{{ t('common.cancel') }}</AppButton>
          <AppButton :loading="busy" @click="apply">{{ t('common.confirm') }}</AppButton>
        </div>
      </template>
    </AppSheet>

    <AppBusyOverlay :open="busy" :label="t('common.busy.target')" />
  </div>
</template>

```

`webroot/src/pages/UpdatePage.vue`:

```vue
<script setup lang="ts">
import { computed, onMounted, ref } from 'vue';

import SectionCard from '../components/layout/SectionCard.vue';
import AppBadge from '../components/ui/AppBadge.vue';
import AppBusyOverlay from '../components/ui/AppBusyOverlay.vue';
import AppButton from '../components/ui/AppButton.vue';
import AppIcon from '../components/ui/AppIcon.vue';
import AppSheet from '../components/ui/AppSheet.vue';
import SegmentedControl from '../components/ui/SegmentedControl.vue';
import { ApiError, api, type UpdateInfo, type UpdateInstall } from '../lib/api';
import { copyText } from '../lib/clipboard';
import { formatBytes, formatVersion } from '../lib/format';
import { t } from '../lib/i18n';
import { loadSession, session } from '../state/session';

const info = ref<UpdateInfo | null>(null);
const channel = ref<'stable' | 'dev'>('stable');
const loading = ref(false);
const busy = ref(false);
const error = ref('');
const log = ref('');
const installResult = ref<UpdateInstall | null>(null);
const confirmOpen = ref(false);
const copied = ref('');

const available = computed(() => info.value?.available ?? false);
const latestSize = computed(() => formatBytes(info.value?.latest.size ?? 0));

async function check() {
  loading.value = true;
  error.value = '';
  try {
    info.value = await api.updateCheck();
  } catch (failure) {
    error.value = failure instanceof Error ? failure.message : String(failure);
  } finally {
    loading.value = false;
  }
}

async function switchChannel(next: string) {
  if (next === channel.value || loading.value || busy.value) return;
  channel.value = next as 'stable' | 'dev';
  error.value = '';
  try {
    await api.updateChannel(channel.value);
    await check();
  } catch (failure) {
    error.value = failure instanceof Error ? failure.message : String(failure);
  }
}

async function install() {
  busy.value = true;
  error.value = '';
  log.value = '';
  installResult.value = null;
  confirmOpen.value = false;
  try {
    installResult.value = await api.updateInstall((chunk) => {
      log.value += chunk;
    });
    await loadSession(true);
    await check();
  } catch (failure) {
    if (failure instanceof ApiError) error.value = failure.message;
    else error.value = failure instanceof Error ? failure.message : String(failure);
  } finally {
    busy.value = false;
  }
}

async function copy(text: string, tag: string) {
  const ok = await copyText(text);
  copied.value = ok ? tag : '';
  window.setTimeout(() => {
    if (copied.value === tag) copied.value = '';
  }, 1600);
}

onMounted(async () => {
  await loadSession();
  const configured = session.data?.config.update_channel;
  channel.value = configured === 'dev' ? 'dev' : 'stable';
  await check();
});
</script>

<template>
  <div class="flex flex-col gap-5 pt-1">
    <SectionCard :title="t('update.section.module')">
      <div class="flex items-center justify-between gap-3">
        <div class="min-w-0">
          <p class="text-[15px] font-semibold">
            {{ t('update.current') }}
            <span class="font-mono">{{ formatVersion(info?.current?.version ?? session.data?.module.version) }}</span>
          </p>
          <p class="mt-0.5 text-xs text-muted">
            versionCode {{ info?.current.version_code ?? session.data?.module.version_code ?? '—' }}
          </p>
        </div>
        <AppBadge :tone="available ? 'warn' : 'ok'">
          {{ available ? t('update.available') : t('update.upToDate') }}
        </AppBadge>
      </div>

      <div class="mt-4 flex flex-col gap-2">
        <span class="text-[11px] font-bold uppercase tracking-[0.09em] text-muted">
          {{ t('update.channel') }}
        </span>
        <SegmentedControl
          :model-value="channel"
          :label="t('update.channel')"
          :options="[
            { value: 'stable', label: t('update.channel.stable') },
            { value: 'dev', label: t('update.channel.dev') },
          ]"
          @update:model-value="switchChannel"
        />
      </div>

      <AppButton variant="tonal" block class="mt-4" :loading="loading" @click="check">
        <AppIcon name="refresh" :size="16" />
        {{ t('update.check') }}
      </AppButton>
    </SectionCard>

    <p v-if="error" class="rounded-card bg-danger-soft px-4 py-3 text-xs leading-relaxed text-danger">
      {{ error }}
    </p>

    <SectionCard v-if="available && info" :title="t('update.section.latest')">
      <div class="flex items-start justify-between gap-3">
        <div class="min-w-0">
          <p class="text-[15px] font-semibold">{{ formatVersion(info.latest.version) }}</p>
          <p class="mt-0.5 text-xs text-muted">
            versionCode {{ info.latest.version_code }} · {{ latestSize }}
          </p>
        </div>
        <AppBadge :tone="info.latest.sha256 ? 'ok' : 'warn'">
          {{ info.latest.sha256 ? t('update.signed') : t('update.unverified') }}
        </AppBadge>
      </div>

      <div class="mt-3 flex flex-col gap-2">
        <div class="flex items-center justify-between gap-2 rounded-tile bg-sunken px-3 py-2">
          <span class="min-w-0 truncate font-mono text-[10px] text-faint">{{ info.endpoint }}</span>
          <button
            type="button"
            class="shrink-0 text-[11px] font-semibold text-accent"
            @click="copy(info.endpoint, 'endpoint')"
          >
            {{ copied === 'endpoint' ? t('common.copied') : t('common.copy') }}
          </button>
        </div>

        <div v-if="info.latest.changelog_url" class="flex items-center justify-between gap-2 rounded-tile bg-sunken px-3 py-2">
          <span class="min-w-0 truncate font-mono text-[10px] text-faint">{{ info.latest.changelog_url }}</span>
          <button
            type="button"
            class="shrink-0 text-[11px] font-semibold text-accent"
            @click="copy(info.latest.changelog_url, 'changelog')"
          >
            {{ copied === 'changelog' ? t('common.copied') : t('common.copy') }}
          </button>
        </div>

        <p v-if="!info.latest.sha256" class="rounded-tile bg-warning-soft px-3 py-2 text-[11px] leading-relaxed text-warning">
          {{ t('update.noChecksum') }}
        </p>
      </div>

      <AppButton block size="lg" class="mt-4" :loading="busy" @click="confirmOpen = true">
        <AppIcon name="download" :size="18" />
        {{ t('update.install') }}
      </AppButton>
    </SectionCard>

    <SectionCard v-else-if="!loading && info" :title="t('update.section.latest')">
      <p class="py-2 text-center text-xs text-muted">{{ t('update.none') }}</p>
    </SectionCard>

    <pre
      v-if="log"
      class="max-h-52 overflow-y-auto rounded-card border border-line bg-sunken p-3 font-mono text-[11px] leading-relaxed text-muted"
      >{{ log }}</pre
    >

    <section
      v-if="installResult"
      class="rounded-card px-4 py-3 text-xs leading-relaxed"
      :class="installResult.installed ? 'bg-accent-soft text-accent' : 'bg-warning-soft text-warning'"
    >
      <p class="font-semibold">
        {{ installResult.installed ? t('update.installed') : t('update.notInstalled') }}
      </p>
      <p v-if="installResult.installed" class="mt-1">
        {{ t('update.reboot') }}
        <span v-if="installResult.installer" class="opacity-70">· {{ installResult.installer }}</span>
      </p>
      <p v-else-if="installResult.reason === 'checksum_missing'" class="mt-1">
        {{ t('update.savedManual') }}
      </p>
      <p v-if="installResult.saved_to" class="mt-1 break-all font-mono text-[10px]">
        {{ installResult.saved_to }}
      </p>
    </section>

    <AppSheet
      v-model:open="confirmOpen"
      :title="t('update.confirm.title')"
      :subtitle="formatVersion(info?.latest?.version)"
    >
      <p class="text-sm leading-relaxed text-muted">
        {{ info?.latest.sha256 ? t('update.confirm.body') : t('update.confirm.unverified') }}
      </p>
      <template #footer>
        <div class="flex justify-end gap-2">
          <AppButton variant="ghost" @click="confirmOpen = false">{{ t('common.cancel') }}</AppButton>
          <AppButton :loading="busy" @click="install">{{ t('common.confirm') }}</AppButton>
        </div>
      </template>
    </AppSheet>

    <AppBusyOverlay :open="busy" :label="t('common.busy.update')" />
  </div>
</template>

```

`webroot/src/router.ts`:

```ts
import { createRouter, createWebHashHistory } from 'vue-router';

import HomePage from './pages/HomePage.vue';
import KeyboxPage from './pages/KeyboxPage.vue';
import RkpPage from './pages/RkpPage.vue';
import TargetPage from './pages/TargetPage.vue';
import UpdatePage from './pages/UpdatePage.vue';

// hash 路由：管理器从固定入口加载 index.html，不需要服务端重写。
export const router = createRouter({
  history: createWebHashHistory(),
  routes: [
    { path: '/', name: 'home', component: HomePage, meta: { titleKey: 'app.title' } },
    { path: '/target', name: 'target', component: TargetPage, meta: { titleKey: 'entry.target.title' } },
    { path: '/keybox', name: 'keybox', component: KeyboxPage, meta: { titleKey: 'entry.keybox.title' } },
    { path: '/rkp', name: 'rkp', component: RkpPage, meta: { titleKey: 'entry.rkp.title' } },
    { path: '/update', name: 'update', component: UpdatePage, meta: { titleKey: 'entry.update.title' } },
    { path: '/:pathMatch(.*)*', redirect: '/' },
  ],
  scrollBehavior: () => ({ top: 0 }),
});

```

`webroot/src/state/session.ts`:

```ts
/** 会话状态：首屏数据只取一次，页面之间共享 [Shared session state] */

import { reactive } from 'vue';
import { api, type Bootstrap } from '../lib/api';

export const session = reactive({
  loading: false,
  loaded: false,
  error: '',
  data: null as Bootstrap | null,
});

let inflight: Promise<void> | null = null;

export async function loadSession(force = false): Promise<void> {
  if (session.loading && inflight) return inflight;
  if (session.loaded && !force) return;
  session.loading = true;
  session.error = '';
  inflight = (async () => {
    try {
      session.data = await api.bootstrap();
      session.loaded = true;
    } catch (error) {
      session.error = error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error);
      session.loaded = true;
    } finally {
      session.loading = false;
      inflight = null;
    }
  })();
  return inflight;
}

export function refreshSession(): Promise<void> {
  return loadSession(true);
}

```

@import './tokens.css';

@tailwind base;

  }

  html {
    height: 100%;

    text-size-adjust: 100%;
    touch-action: manipulation;
  }

  body {
    @apply bg-bg font-sans text-fg;

    /* 高度跟随宿主容器，不锁 100vh：KernelSU 的 WebUI 容器高度不等于最大视口高度，
       一旦强制撑满就会凭空多出一截可滚动区域。 */
    /* Follow the host container: the WebUI viewport is smaller than 100vh. */
    min-height: 100%;
    line-height: 1.55;
    overflow-x: clip;
    overscroll-behavior: none;
    background-image: radial-gradient(120% 55% at 50% -10%, var(--bg-wash), transparent 70%);
    background-repeat: no-repeat;
    -webkit-font-smoothing: antialiased;
    transition:
      background-color var(--dur-3) var(--ease),
      color var(--dur-3) var(--ease);
  }

  /* 旧 WebView 没有 overflow: clip，退回 hidden。clip 不创建滚动容器，
     因此不会让 sticky 吸顶/吸底失效。 */
  @supports not (overflow: clip) {
    body {
      overflow-x: hidden;
    }
  }

  :focus-visible {
    outline: 2px solid var(--accent);
    outline-offset: 2px;
    border-radius: 6px;
  }

  ::selection {
    background: var(--accent-soft-strong);
  }

  /* 滚动条细一点，避免在窄屏上占宽度 */
  ::-webkit-scrollbar {
    width: 4px;
    height: 4px;
  }
  ::-webkit-scrollbar-thumb {
    background: var(--line-strong);
    border-radius: 2px;
  }
}

    padding-right: var(--inset-right);
  }
  .tap {
    @apply transition-colors duration-150 ease-standard;

```css
/* ============================================================
 * TeeForge WebUI — 设计令牌 / Design tokens
 *
 * 视觉模型：色调分层（tonal layers）。页面是最低一层容器色，
 * 卡片高一层，浮层再高一层，因此容器之间不需要描边或阴影去"分隔"。
 * 色相只保留给品牌强调与状态，不做装饰性撞色。
 *
 * 兼容性：全部使用显式色值，不依赖 oklch() / color-mix()，
 * 以便在系统 WebView 版本较旧的设备上仍能正确渲染。
 * ============================================================ */

import vue from '@vitejs/plugin-vue';

// 输出到模块的 webroot 目录，与 xtask 打包路径保持一致。
// Assets use relative paths so the page works from any mount point.

  plugins: [vue()],
  base: './',
  build: {
    outDir: '../module/webroot',
    emptyOutDir: true,
    // 兼容较旧的 Android WebView（模块基线 API 24+）。

    cssCodeSplit: false,
